In a matched ten-seed Split Fashion-MNIST study, a plastic GrapNet+ER head reaches 63.16 percent seen-class accuracy versus 51.08 percent for a parameter-larger dense MLP+ER under the same seen-class loss and replay memory, with paired delta 12.08 points and p=1.3e-5. On Split CIFAR-10 with a frozen ImageNet ResNet-18 encoder, the same substrate improves the online head over MLP-256 by 3.81 points, with p=0.0026. These results support GrapNet as an editable neural graph substrate whose core value is structural programmability with faithful execution views.

</details>


### [181] [Be Your Own Teacher: Steering Protein Language Models via Unsupervised Reward Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18961)
*Lanqing Li,Shentong Mo,Yang Yu,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种利用无监督奖励优化（SRO/BRO）对蛋白质语言模型进行无需人工标注的微调框架，有效解决了生物分子设计中缺乏反馈数据的难题，在生成可控性和覆盖率上表现卓越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质语言模型（PLMs）的后训练适配高度依赖昂贵的湿实验数据或精选的偏好数据集，存在严重的监督瓶颈，限制了其在数据匮乏场景下的应用。

Method: 提出基于无监督奖励优化的框架，结合模型内在不确定性与蛋白质表征模型的语义一致性构建无监督奖励函数，并设计了两种离线算法：Soft Reward Optimization (SRO) 和 Binarized Reward Optimization (BRO) 来优化强化学习人类反馈（RLHF）目标。

Result: 在组合分布外提示（compositional OOD prompts）任务中，SRO和BRO显著优于DPO和KTO等基线方法，在多个采样温度、模型规模及蛋白质家族上均接近预言（oracle）性能，并在pass@k评估中展现出更高的生成覆盖率。

Conclusion: 该方法通过引入无监督奖励优化，成功在缺乏标注数据和实验反馈的情况下，实现了蛋白质语言模型（PLMs）的高效可控生成，为生物分子设计提供了一条可扩展的自我提升路径。

Abstract: Protein language models (PLMs) have emerged as powerful tools for controllable biomolecular design, yet their post-training adaptation typically relies on costly wet-lab validation or curated preference datasets. To overcome this supervision bottleneck, we introduce unsupervised reward optimization of PLMs, a comprehensive framework for steerable protein generation without ground-truth labels. Our key insight is that task-agnostic rewards, which combine intrinsic model uncertainty with extrinsic semantic consistency informed by protein representation models, exhibit strong correlation with controllability measures across base models and temperature regimes. Building upon this discovery, we propose two offline algorithms: Soft Reward Optimization (SRO) and Binarized Reward Optimization (BRO), which effectively maximize the classical RLHF objective induced by these proxy rewards. Extensive experiments on compositional out-of-distribution prompts demonstrate that both methods significantly outperform competitive baselines (DPO, KTO), while approaching oracle performance across multiple sampling temperatures, model scales and protein families. Moreover, PLMs fine-tuned with unsupervised rewards can achieve consistently higher coverage compared to their base model in pass@k evaluations. By enabling self-improvement of PLMs through their own generated experience, our framework provides a scalable pathway toward controllable biomolecular design in settings where labeled preferences or experimental feedback are scarce or unavailable.

</details>


### [182] [A Controlled Benchmark of Quantum-Latent GAN Augmentation for Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18970)
*Syed Mujtaba Haider,Silvia Figini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过严谨的基准测试验证发现，在控制参数量和评估协议的情况下，量子生成器在医学图像数据增强中并不具备超越经典模型的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有量子生成模型用于医学图像增强的研究存在评估不严谨（如单次运行、参数不匹配、缺乏数据规模特征分析等）的问题，旨在通过严谨的控制变量法验证量子生成器是否真的能带来性能增益。

Method: 构建了一个受控基准实验：在KL正则化的潜在空间中，对比变分量子生成器与参数量近乎相同的经典生成器（1648 vs 1632）在脑部MRI图像增强中的性能。实验涵盖5%至100%的不同标注数据比例，重复8次随机种子实验，并进行配对显著性检验、组内多样性及潜在分布分析。

Result: 在所有数据比例下，无论是有无量子增强，模型均未显著优于仅使用真实数据的训练效果；量子生成器与经典生成器在性能上统计学上无差异。 synthetic样本表现不佳，存在脱离分布（off-distribution）和严重模式坍缩现象。

Conclusion: 在医学图像增强任务中，量子生成对抗网络（GAN）相较于同等参数规模的经典GAN未表现出统计学上的显著优势。所谓的“性能提升”更多是作为一种正则化手段而非真实的数据扩展，且在数据极度稀缺时，该方法伴随严重的模式坍缩（mode collapse）。

Abstract: Medical image classification is often constrained by limited labeled data, motivating generative augmentation; recently, quantum generative models have been proposed for this purpose, frequently reporting accuracy gains. However, such claims are typically based on single training runs, do not match the parameter budgets of the quantum and classical generators, and do not characterize the data regime in which any benefit appears. We present a controlled benchmark that isolates the contribution of a quantum generator to brain-MRI augmentation. Images are encoded into a KL-regularized latent space in which a conditional Wasserstein GAN with gradient penalty is trained using either a variational quantum generator or a classical generator of near-identical parameter count (1648 vs. 1632). Synthetic samples are decoded and used to augment a pretrained classifier across labeled data fractions from 5% to 100%, evaluated over eight random seeds with paired significance testing (with multiple-comparison correction) and with intraset diversity and latent-distribution analyses. Across all fractions, no augmentation variant significantly outperforms real-data-only training, and the quantum and classical generators are statistically indistinguishable. Any low-data benefit behaves as regularization rather than faithful data expansion:synthetic samples are off distribution and severely mode collapsed precisely where data is scarce, and the quantum generator is no more diverse thanits classical counterpart. We release the protocol as a testbed for rigorous evaluation of quantum generative augmentation in medical imaging.

</details>


### [183] [Online Reward-Punishment Learning from Fixed-Channel Perceptual Event Streams without Environment Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18963)
*Zirong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在线奖励-惩罚学习框架（OHIRL），证明智能体无需外部奖励或标签，仅通过感知后果的内部评估与残差动力学建模，即可在复杂环境中自主推断效价并实现高效的策略决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服传统强化学习高度依赖外部标量奖励或人工评价标签的局限性。研究旨在探讨智能体如何在环境仅提供原始感知数据且无任何外部反馈的情况下，通过观察转移后果来自主学习如何评估并执行有益的动作。

Method: 提出了在线奖励-惩罚学习模型（OHIRL），由四个解耦模块组成：M_psi（预测下一感知包）、D_omega（建模残差动力学）、C_eta（固定的内部轨迹评估器，使用恢复正向/持久负向的评估规则）以及B_xi（基于价值证据学习策略和动作评分）。该方法通过条件误差分解进行验证，并在多个任务中与基于预测误差的奖励、即时数据打分等对照组进行对比。

Result: 在2x2-XOR、CartPole和Taxi等任务中，该模型表现出极高的鲁棒性：B_xi的奖励符号预测准确率达到0.940，最优动作选择准确率高达0.979；相比之下，基于预测误差或零奖励等基准方法在相同设置下均表现失效。此外，研究确认了其评估方案对不同单调变换的抗干扰能力。

Conclusion: 在无外部显式奖励信号的环境下，通过感知维度（如疼痛、能量、接触等）的后果推断其效价（valence），能够实现有效的策略优化。该研究证明了基于自监督预测与内部残差评估的框架可以稳健地从环境转移后果中提取行为引导信号，在无需人工标记或标量奖励的情况下达到优于基准对照组的性能。

Abstract: We study online reward-punishment learning when the environment provides no scalar reward or evaluative label. At each step the agent receives only a fixed-channel perceptual packet, and quantities such as pain, energy, contact, damage, or cognitive error are treated as perceptual dimensions whose valence must be inferred from transition consequences. OHIRL separates four roles: M_psi learns next-packet prediction, D_omega models residual dynamics, C_eta is a fixed internal post-transition trajectory evaluator, and B_xi learns to use the resulting value evidence for later policy updates and action scoring. C_eta uses a recovery-positive and persistence/growth-negative residual-regulation orientation; a coefficient-origin audit shows that equal-unit, raw-equal, and random monotone variants preserve more than 92% of the released top-action rankings, while sign inversion preserves 0%.
  The reward-free protocol exposes observation transitions while withholding environment rewards, delayed external evaluators, success labels, and action-goodness labels. A conditional error decomposition separates B_xi evidence-estimation error from residual policy-optimization error. In a 2x2-XOR packet task, medicine and chili acquire opposite value under visual XOR contexts, and the same pain or spice increase can be positive or negative depending on consequence structure; B_xi reaches 0.952 balanced reward-sign accuracy. In a full online-interleaved audit, M_psi reaches holdout R2=0.907, B_xi reaches 0.940 sign accuracy, and the policy reaches 0.979 optimal-action accuracy, while immediate packet scores, prediction-error rewards, shuffled targets, zero reward, and error-reduction controls collapse. Hidden-reward CartPole and Taxi controls, public-context no-leakage audits, and module-role ablations further test information boundaries and component necessity.

</details>


### [184] [EfficientRollout: System-Aware Self-Speculative Decoding for RL Rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18967)
*Minseo Kim,Minjae Lee,Seunghyuk Oh,Kevin Galim,Donghoon Kim,Coleman Hooper,Harman Singh,Amir Gholami,Hyung Il Koo,Wonjun Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EfficientRollout 是一款针对 LLM 强化学习 rollout 过程的加速框架，通过自适应的“自投机解码”技术和系统感知调度，实现了在保持模型质量的同时显著提升训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL 模型训练中的 rollout 生成是计算的瓶颈，而传统的投机解码（SD）在 RL 环境下存在分布不匹配和计算资源利用率低的问题，导致其性能提升在 RL 场景中受限。

Method: 提出了 EfficientRollout 框架：1. 自投机解码（Self-SD）：利用目标模型自身量化版本作为草稿模型，解决策略演化导致的分布不匹配问题；2. 系统感知决策：设计了一套 SD 切换策略与动态调整草稿长度的机制，根据当前负载与计算状态决定是否启用投机，避免在内存受限（memory-bound）场景下无效的并行验证。

Result: 在加速的基础 AR rollout 基准之上，EfficientRollout 将 rollout 延迟和端到端延迟分别降低了 19.6% 和 12.7%，且完全保留了原始的模型性能。

Conclusion: EfficientRollout 通过自驱动的投机采样与系统感知的动态策略，成功解决了强化学习（RL）生成过程中的延迟瓶颈，实现了在保证模型性能的前提下显著降低推理延迟。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a representative post-training paradigm for LLMs, enabling strong reasoning and agentic capabilities. However, rollout generation remains a dominant latency bottleneck because autoregressive sampling decodes responses sequentially and a small number of long-tailed generations often determine completion time. Speculative decoding (SD) offers a natural way to address this bottleneck, as it is a well-established technique for serving fixed LLMs that reduces latency by rapidly drafting tokens and accepting them through parallel verification while preserving the target-model distribution. However, its practical speedups do not directly carry over to RL rollouts: (i) the evolving target policy makes any fixed drafter increasingly mismatched with the policy's output distribution; and (ii) active batch sizes shrink throughout rollout decoding, shifting decoding from compute-bound to memory-bound regimes where parallel verification can exploit underutilized compute. Therefore, accelerating RL rollouts requires both a drafter that remains effective under long, high-temperature generations from an evolving policy and system-aware use of SD that avoids compute-bound regimes. We present EfficientRollout, a system-aware self-SD framework designed to address this gap for RL rollouts. EfficientRollout induces a quantized drafter from the target model (i.e. self-speculative decoding), keeping it coupled to the evolving policy without separate drafter pretraining or online adaptation. It further coordinates a system-aware SD toggle policy with acceptance-aware draft-length adaptation, enabling speculation only in beneficial regimes while matching the drafting budget to evolving drafter quality. EfficientRollout reduces rollout and end-to-end latency by up to 19.6% and 12.7%, respectively, over an accelerated AR rollout baseline, while preserving final model quality.

</details>


### [185] [DIPHINE: Diffusion-based $Φ$-ID Neural Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18997)
*Simon Pedro Galeano Munoz,Mustapha Bounoua,Giulio Franzese,Pietro Michiardi,Maurizio Filippone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DIPHINE 是首个利用扩散模型实现复杂系统 $Φ$ID（集成信息分解）的神经网络估计器，解决了现有方法在非高斯连续数据应用上的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 $Φ$ID 计算方法仅限于高斯或离散系统，无法处理连续非高斯动力学系统，限制了其在现实世界复杂系统研究中的应用。

Method: 提出了一种基于得分的扩散模型（score-based diffusion models）神经网络估计器 DIPHINE；通过该网络估计 $Φ$ID 所需的所有互信息项，并采用 Möbius 反演法将其重构为十六种信息原子（冗余、唯一、协同）。此外，对反演误差进行了理论分析。

Result: DIPHINE 在合成基准测试中展现了准确的原子重构能力，且在互信息估计性能上优于现有方法；在处理真实生理数据时，能够无需分布假设地提取出具有生理意义的信息动力学结构。

Conclusion: DIPHINE 通过结合扩散模型与 Möbius 反演，成功实现了对连续非高斯复杂系统信息动力学的无偏、高效分解，为理解复杂系统的“信息架构”提供了通用工具。

Abstract: Uncovering the true informational architecture of real-world complex systems requires disentangling how their components uniquely store, redundantly share, and synergistically integrate information over time. Integrated Information Decomposition ($Φ$ID) is a framework for decomposing the information dynamics of multivariate systems into sixteen non-overlapping atoms that characterize redundant, unique, and synergistic modes of information storage, transfer, and integration. Existing methods to compute $Φ$ID are restricted to Gaussian or discrete systems, preventing its application to continuous non-Gaussian dynamical systems. We address this limitation by proposing DIPHINE (Diffusion-based $Φ$-ID Neural Estimator), the first neural estimator that leverages score-based diffusion models to jointly estimate all the mutual information terms required by $Φ$ID from a single amortized network, recovering the sixteen atoms through Möbius inversion. We provide a theoretical analysis of error propagation through the inversion, showing that the Jacobian of the mapping from mutual informations to atoms is integer-valued and that the synergy-to-synergy atom is provably the hardest to estimate. We demonstrate accurate recovery of ground-truth atoms on synthetic benchmarks, superior performance compared to established mutual information estimators, and the ability to extract physiologically interpretable information-dynamic structure on an application involving real data without any distributional assumptions.

</details>


### [186] [A Hybrid LSTM--Vision Transformer Architecture for Predicting HRRR Forecast Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19026)
*David Aaron Evans,Jay C. Rothenberger,Kara J. Sulia,Nick P. Bassill,Chris D. Thorncroft*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种 LSTM-ViT 混合架构，通过整合垂直廓线观测改进了对复杂大气过程下的高分辨率天气预报（HRRR）误差的预测，显著优于传统的纯 LSTM 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于 LSTM 的天气预报误差纠正模型在处理复杂的垂直大气演变（如行星边界层过程、对流等）时性能下降，缺乏对垂直结构信息的捕捉能力。

Method: 开发了一种混合模型 LSTM-ViT。利用 LSTM 处理地面观测的时间序列，并引入 Vision Transformer (ViT) 来整合来自垂直廓线仪网络的垂直大气结构特征，从而预测天气预报误差。

Result: LSTM-ViT 在所有三个指标（降水、10米风速、2米温度）上均优于基线 LSTM 模型，特别是在短预报时效和强边界层活动期间。其中，降水预报误差的预测技巧提升了约两倍。

Conclusion: 通过结合时间序列学习与垂直结构注意力机制，LSTM-ViT架构有效提升了对复杂大气演变下的NWP预报误差预测能力，为提升业务预报信心提供了物理意义明确的路径。

Abstract: Forecast errors in high-resolution numerical weather prediction (NWP) systems are often linked to unresolved planetary boundary layer (PBL) processes, convection, terrain-induced circulations, and other vertically structured atmospheric phenomena. Previous work demonstrated that Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks can successfully predict forecast errors in the High-Resolution Rapid Refresh (HRRR) model using mesonet observations, but we believe performance degradation is linked to periods of complex vertical atmospheric evolution. To address this limitation, we develop a hybrid LSTM-Vision Transformer (LSTM-ViT) framework that combines temporal sequence learning from surface observations with atmospheric profiles from the New York State Mesonet profiler network. The LSTM-ViT framework is trained to predict HRRR hourly precipitation, 10 m wind speed, and 2 m temperature forecast errors at individual mesonet stations. Across all three predictors, incorporation of profiler-derived atmospheric structure improves forecast error prediction skill relative to the baseline LSTM architecture, with the largest gains occurring at shorter forecast lead times and during periods of enhanced PBL activity. Improvements are particularly pronounced for precipitation forecast error, where the LSTM-ViT framework achieves approximately a twofold increase in predictive skill relative to the baseline LSTM while better capturing convectively driven error evolution and reducing degradation associated with PBL processes. These results demonstrate that combining temporal sequence learning with vertically informed attention mechanisms provides a physically meaningful pathway for improving forecast error prediction in operational NWP systems. Our research offers forecasters enhanced guidance regarding model bias and forecast confidence.

</details>


### [187] [Geometric and Stochastic Analysis of Discontinuities in Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19036)
*Tho Tran Huu,Huu-Tuan Nguyen,Thien-Hai Nguyen,Nhat-Tri Ho,Viet-Hoang Tran,Tho Quan,Tan Minh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对SMoE模型中因Top-k路由产生的不连续性问题，本文通过几何与随机分析揭示了低阶不连续面是性能波动的核心来源，并据此设计了一种高效的局部平滑方法，实现了模型稳定性与性能的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏专家混合模型（SMoE）通过Top-k路由实现高效扩展，但该选择机制导致模型映射函数在专家切换边界处呈现严重的非连续性，可能导致输入微小变动引起输出剧烈跳变。研究旨在通过几何与随机分析揭示这些不连续性的本质，并提出有效的平滑策略。

Method: 1. 使用测度论工具对SMoE的Top-k路由不连续面进行几何与随机分析，分类不连续面并推导体积渐近估计；
2. 利用扩散过程模拟输入空间的随机扰动，证明输入路径几乎必然首先遇到一阶（order-1）不连续面；
3. 基于上述理论洞察，提出一种轻量级的“局部平滑”机制（Softening），通过在不连续区域附近引入专家权重的软化处理来增强模型稳定性。

Result: 1. 证明了SMoE模型中的低阶（order-1）不连续集占据了输入空间中不连续区域的主要份额；
2. 实验表明，提出的局部平滑方法不仅强制实现了映射的连续性，还在多项语言和视觉任务上提升了模型的准确性，同时保持了较低的额外计算负担。

Conclusion: SMoE模型的决策函数在专家切换边界处存在严重的不连续性，理论上低阶（order-1）不连续性占据主导地位。通过针对这些关键区域引入局部平滑机制，可以有效解决不连续问题并提升模型性能，且计算开销可控。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (SMoE) architectures are now widely deployed in state-of-the-art language and vision models, where conditional routing allows scaling to very large networks. However, this very Top-$k$ expert selection that enables conditional routing also renders the SMoE map inherently discontinuous. In the vicinity of these discontinuity surfaces, even inputs that are arbitrarily close may activate substantially different sets of experts resulting in significantly different outputs. In this work we give a rigorous geometric and stochastic analysis of these discontinuities. We first classify them by order, determined by the number of tied experts at a switching event. Using measure-theoretic slicing arguments, we establish asymptotic volume estimates for the thickened discontinuity surfaces, showing that lower-order discontinuity sets dominate, whereas higher-order ones occupy a vanishingly small relative volume. Next, modeling random perturbations in the input space via a diffusion process, we prove that the path eventually encounter a discontinuity, and moreover that the first hit almost surely occurs on an order-1 discontinuity with explicit finite-time probability bounds. We further derive occupation-time bounds that quantify the duration the random path spend in the neighborhoods of each discontinuity order. These theoretical results imply that inputs are more likely to lie near lower order discontinuities. Motivated by this insight, we propose a simple smoothing mechanism that can be directly applied to existing SMoEs, softly incorporating experts near discontinuities; our analysis guarantees that the added computational overhead remains small while providing localized smoothing near discontinuities, and experiments across language and vision tasks show that smoothing not only enforces continuity of the SMoE map but also enhances empirical performance.

</details>


### [188] [Pareto Q-Learning with Reward Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19134)
*Arnaud Lequen,Clément Legrand-Lixon,Léo Saulières*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PQLRM 算法，通过结合 Pareto Q-Learning 和 Reward Machines，高效解决了带有非马尔可夫奖励的多目标强化学习策略合成问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有方法在处理具有复杂、非马尔可夫奖励结构的多目标强化学习任务时，面临的策略合成困难和样本效率低下问题。

Method: 提出 PQLRM 算法，将 Pareto Q-Learning (PQL) 与 Reward Machines (RM) 相结合，利用 RM 的自动机结构分解奖励信号，并同时维护一组矢量值 Q 值估计以逼近帕累托前沿。

Result: 实验表明，PQLRM 在合成帕累托最优策略方面优于传统的 QRM 方法，且相比直接应用于交叉积 MDP 的朴素 PQL 算法，具有更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: PQLRM 结合了 PQL 和 RM 的优势，在处理具有非马尔可夫奖励结构的复杂多目标强化学习任务时，能够高效地合成帕累托最优策略，并比基准方法表现出更快的收敛速度。

Abstract: We present Pareto Q-Learning with Reward Machines (PQLRM), a multi-objective reinforcement learning algorithm for tasks whose reward structure is specified by a set of reward machines (RMs). PQLRM combines Pareto Q-Learning (PQL), which maintains sets of vector-valued Q-estimates to approximate the Pareto front, with enhancements from Q-Learning with Reward Machines (QRM), which exploits the factored automaton structure of the reward signal. This yields a multi-policy algorithm that remains sample-efficient under non-Markovian, RM-encoded rewards. Experimental trials show that PQLRM converges faster than a naive PQL baseline applied to the cross-product MDP and can synthesize Pareto-optimal policies that QRM cannot.

</details>


### [189] [OrthoReg: Orthogonal Regularization for Hybrid Symbolic-Neural Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19145)
*Till Richter,Niki Kilbertus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过正交正则化（OrthoReg）来实现混合模型中符号部分与神经网络部分解耦的方法，防止神经网络学习已知的物理规律，从而提高模型的可解释性和泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合建模（符号模型+神经网络）中存在一个核心痛点：神经网络倾向于重复学习本应由符号部分表达的物理机制，导致模型产生冗余、丧失物理可解释性，且传统的 L2 正则化在稀疏发现场景下失效。

Method: 引入 OrthoReg（正交正则化）技术。该方法直接对符号分量和神经网络分量之间的重叠进行惩罚，确保神经网络仅拟合符号分量无法表达的残差项，从而实现两者的解耦。

Result: 在存在部分库模型失配（library mismatch）的动力系统基准测试中，OrthoReg 相比现有方法展现出更优的符号恢复性能和分布外预测能力。

Conclusion: OrthoReg 方法通过强制执行符号分量与神经网络分量之间的正交性，有效解决了混合建模中常见的冗余和不可解释性问题，显著提升了符号发现的准确性和模型在分布外数据上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Dynamical systems are fundamental to modeling the natural world, yet modeling them involves a persistent trade-off: manually prescribed mechanistic models are interpretable by design but often overly simplistic and misspecified; in contrast, flexible data-driven neural methods lack physical insight. Hybrid modeling aims for the best of both worlds by combining a prescribed or symbolic, physics-based component with a flexible neural network. A critical challenge, however, is that the neural component may relearn mechanistic parts, yielding redundant and uninterpretable models, especially when the symbolic structure itself is discovered from data. Existing methods based on standard $L^2$ regularization rely on a projection argument that breaks when the symbolic component is learned through sparse discovery, allowing the neural augmentation to overlap with symbolic structure. We introduce \textbf{OrthoReg} (Orthogonal Regularization), which directly penalizes overlap between the symbolic and neural components, preventing symbolic structure from being absorbed by the neural residual. This yields a complementary decomposition: the symbolic part captures what the library can express, and the neural part captures what remains. On benchmark dynamical systems with partial library mismatch, OrthoReg improves symbolic recovery and out-of-distribution behavior.

</details>


### [190] [JourneyFormer: Encoding Airbnb Guest Journey with Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19108)
*Daochen Zha,Chun How Tan,Xin Liu,Bin Xu,Han Zhao,Xiaowei Liu,Tracy Yu,Hui Gao,Huiji Gao,Liwei He,Stephanie Moyerman,Sanjeev Katariya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Airbnb针对搜索排序问题提出的序列建模方案JourneyFormer，重点解决了长序列建模与稀疏标签导致的工程部署挑战，并在生产实践中取得了显著的业务收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生产环境下应用序列模型面临序列复杂（长、探索性强）和标签稀疏（如Airbnb的预订行为）的挑战，需要在模型效果和工程可扩展性之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出JourneyFormer序列建模模型，涵盖了嘉宾事件选择、ID嵌入、模型架构设计、标签归因等关键技术点，并包含针对模型训练和推理的加速策略。

Result: 在离线排序指标上获得提升，并通过两个生产环境的在线A/B测试实现了关键业务指标的显著增长。

Conclusion: JourneyFormer通过在Airbnb生产环境中的部署，证明了该序列建模方案在解决长序列、稀疏标签推荐问题上的有效性，并为类似工业界场景提供了系统性的设计和工程实践参考。

Abstract: Sequence modeling has become increasingly popular in recommendation and ranking algorithms, owing to its capacity to model users' historical behaviors and infer user intentions. Despite its theoretical simplicity, the practical deployment of a sequence model in production is non-trivial due to complexity of the sequence and sparse labels. For example, in Airbnb, guest sequences are often long, exploratory and complex, and we focus on booking labels, which are sparse. As such, we are often required to make various design decisions regarding data and modeling to strike a balance between effectiveness and scalability. This work delved into these production challenges and deployed JourneyFormer, a sequence modeling solution for search ranking at Airbnb. We detail crucial design considerations, covering aspects such as guest event selection, ID embeddings, model architecture, and label attribution. Additionally, we describe several tailored strategies to accelerate model training and inference. JourneyFormer has been successfully deployed within Airbnb's production, where its effectiveness and impact have been evidenced not only by improved offline ranking metrics but also by significant gains in key business metrics through online A/B testing across 2 production surfaces.

</details>


### [191] [Essential Subspace Merging for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19164)
*Longhua Li,Lei Qi,Xin Geng,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对模型合并中的任务间干扰问题，提出了基于本质子空间分解（ESD）的 ESM/ESM++ 合并框架，通过提取任务输出偏移的核心方向，实现了高效、低干扰的无训练模型融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并面临的核心挑战是多任务微调导致参数更新间的干扰，而作者发现任务更新的输出偏移主要集中在少数主方向（本质子空间）上，其余方向的累积效应引发了干扰。

Method: 提出了本质子空间分解（ESD）方法，将任务权重更新分解为本质子空间成分与非本质成分；基于此开发了静态合并方法 ESM（正交化融合）和动态合并方法 ESM++（低秩专家路由）。

Result: 在多个任务集和模型规模上的实验表明，ESM 和 ESM++ 较基线方法能更有效地保留任务能力，同时显著降低了任务间的干扰。

Conclusion: ESD/ESM/ESM++ 通过关注任务更新在激活空间中的核心方向（本质子空间），有效解决了多任务模型融合中的干扰问题，实现了无训练的参数合并，达到了保持任务知识与减少干扰的平衡。

Abstract: Model merging aims to enable multi-task learning by integrating the capabilities of multiple models fine-tuned from the same pre-trained checkpoint into a single model. Its core challenge is inter-task interference among task-specific parameter updates. In this paper, we analyze the output shifts induced by task updates and observe that their energy is concentrated in a small number of principal directions. We call the subspace spanned by these directions the essential subspace. In contrast, most remaining directions carry little task-relevant energy, but their accumulation across multiple task updates can cause severe interference during merging. Motivated by this observation, we propose Essential Subspace Decomposition (ESD), which decomposes each task update according to the principal components of its activation shift. Based on ESD, we introduce Essential Subspace Merging (ESM), a training-free static merging method that orthogonalizes and fuses essential components into one compact multi-task model. We further extend ESM to ESM++, a training-free dynamic merging method that decomposes task-specific residuals into low-rank experts and selects the most relevant expert through prototype-based routing during forward inference. Extensive experiments across multiple task sets and model scales demonstrate that ESM and ESM++ effectively preserves task knowledge while reducing inter-task interference.

</details>


### [192] [Seeing Before Reasoning: Decoupling Perception and Reasoning for Shortcut-Resilient Multimodal On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19120)
*Sihan Wang,Xiyao Liu,Lianqing Liu,Zhi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ViGOS 通过将视觉描述生成与推理过程拆分，并引入多维度教师监督机制，解决了 MLLM 自我蒸馏时的视觉信息弱化问题，实现了更强的视觉接地推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的链式思维 OPSD 技术在 MLLM 上应用时存在“捷径效应”：模型往往过度依赖文本参考目标而非图像信息，导致视觉接地能力不足。

Method: 提出 ViGOS 框架：采用“视觉描述+推理”的两阶段生成策略，并引入三个教师机制：1.图像感知教师（监督视觉描述）；2.特权推理教师（监督推理过程与最终答案）；3.参考教师（仅用于纠偏无效输出）。

Result: 在通用视觉语言、专家推理、视觉数学、空间接地及视觉语言先验等多个基准测试中，ViGOS 表现优异，尤其是在易产生捷径的视觉任务中显著优于基线。

Conclusion: ViGOS 有效解决了 MLLM 在自我蒸馏过程中的文本偏见（shortcuts）问题，在保持 OPSD 优势的同时，显著提升了模型在视觉任务中的真实接地能力。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a model on its own rollouts and uses a frozen copy to provide dense token-level targets conditioned on a reference target. This works well for LLM reasoning, but a direct extension to multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can create a shortcut: the privileged target may guide tokens mainly based on the text reference target rather than the image. We propose ViGOS, a visually grounded OPSD framework for MLLM post-training. The student first writes a visual description and then reasons toward the final answer. For valid rollouts, an image-only perception teacher supervises the description, while a privileged reasoning teacher supervises the reasoning and final answer on the same student prefix. A reference teacher is used only for invalid rollouts to recover the output format. Across general vision-language, expert reasoning, visual math, spatial grounding, and visual-language-prior benchmarks, ViGOS keeps the main benefits of OPSD and improves image-grounded behavior in shortcut-prone settings.

</details>


### [193] [ChronoSurv: A Clinical Pathway-Guided Graph Framework for Multimodal Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19140)
*Hugo Miccinilli,Theo Di Piazza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ChronoSurv 是一种用于头颈癌生存分析的多模态深度学习框架，通过基于有向图的层次化建模来捕捉临床诊断轨迹，解决了现有模型忽视临床工作流和数据非静态性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头颈癌的生存预测因多模态数据的异构性和高维性而极具挑战。现有的深度生存模型多采用静态融合或忽视时间演变特征，难以模拟真实的临床诊断与治疗工作流，导致预测性能受限。

Method: 提出了一种名为 ChronoSurv 的异构层次化有向图框架。该方法将患者的护理过程建模为“轨迹感知”的临床轨迹，通过异构消息传递机制处理多模态数据，利用层次拓扑结构（细粒度、粗粒度、全局）处理缺失模态，并结合临床诊断步骤进行对齐。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上的实验显示，ChronoSurv 达到了最先进（SOTA）的判别性能，且保持了可靠的校准性，消融实验进一步验证了各架构模块的有效性。

Conclusion: ChronoSurv 通过引入轨迹感知以及基于有向图的层次化建模，有效地捕捉了临床工作流中的结构特征，不仅提升了生存预测的判别能力，还保证了临床决策所需的统计校准性。

Abstract: Accurate survival prediction is essential for personalized treatment planning in head and neck cancer, yet remains challenging due to the heterogeneous and high-dimensional nature of multimodal clinical data. While deep survival models have improved predictive performance over classical statistical approaches, existing methods typically rely on static fusion strategies or temporally agnostic modeling, limiting their ability to capture structured clinical workflows. In this work, we propose ChronoSurv, a heterogeneous hierarchical directed graph framework for multimodal survival analysis. ChronoSurv represents patient care as a progression-aware clinical trajectory using directed graphs aligned with key diagnostic steps. A hierarchical topology incorporates fine-grained, coarse, and global representations, further supporting flexible adaptation to missing modalities, while heterogeneous message passing models complex and asymmetric relationships across modalities and clinical steps. Experimental results on two public datasets demonstrate that ChronoSurv achieves state-of-the-art discriminative performance while maintaining statistically reliable calibration. Comprehensive ablation studies further confirm the contribution of each architectural component, highlighting the potential of trajectory-aware graph modeling for multimodal survival prediction.

</details>


### [194] [Forecasting what Matters: Decision-Focused RL for Controlled EV Charging with Unknown Departure Times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19199)
*Giuseppe Gabriele,Fabio Pavirani,Seyed Soroush Karimi Madahi,Chris Develder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对电动汽车充电中关键特征缺失问题，本文提出一种决策导向的RL框架（DF-RL），通过端到端联合训练预测器与控制器，有效消除预测偏差积累，显著提升了充电调度的准确性与系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着电动汽车（EV）普及，智能充电控制面临挑战；而现有的基于强化学习的解决方案往往缺乏关键特征（如离场时间），且传统的“预测-决策”两阶段方法由于预测模型未针对决策任务优化，导致预测误差累积并降低控制效果。

Method: 提出了一种“决策导向的强化学习”（Decision-Focused RL, DF-RL）框架，通过将预测器的损失函数与强化学习智能体的决策质量直接挂钩，实现预测器与控制器的端到端联合训练。

Result: 相较于不具备离场时间预测功能的传统RL方法，DF-RL在总奖励（Total Reward）上提升了14%，并将未满足能量需求（Unsupplied Energy）降低了55%。

Conclusion: 通过将预测器（Forecaster）与强化学习控制器（RL Controller）进行端到端联合训练，能够有效解决下游决策中的预测误差传播问题，从而显著提升电动汽车充电策略的效率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: The recent growth of EV adoption poses challenges for power systems, including increased peak demand and potential grid instability. Smart control of EV charging -- e.g., based on reinforcement learning (RL) -- can alleviate these issues by learning temporal and contextual patterns from historical data. Yet, in real-world scenarios, key features, such as departure time, often are unavailable. This, in turn, makes it harder for an RL agent to learn and execute an effective charging policy. To mitigate this uncertainty, a trained forecaster can approximate the unknown features from available data. However, since these forecasting models are typically trained for accuracy (rather than their impact on a downstream agent's decision quality), their errors may propagate and hinder the overall performance of a controller that is using the forecasts. To avoid this, we propose a decision-focused RL (DF-RL) framework in which the forecaster is trained end-to-end, i.e., with feedback from the charging policy actions taken by the RL agent. Such joint training of both the forecaster and controller ultimately results in higher-quality actions: our proposed DF-RL method yields superior charging decisions compared to other baselines, achieving up to a 14% improvement in total reward and a 55% reduction of unsupplied energy (i.e., charging that failed to happen because the EV already left), relative to the RL method without departure time forecasting.

</details>


### [195] [Machine Unlearning for the XGBoost Model with Network Intrusion Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19220)
*Diana Magalhães,Eva Maia,João Vitorino,Isabel Praça*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对网络入侵检测领域的表格数据缺失，本文提出了 XGBoost-Forget，证明了其在不牺牲模型性能的前提下，能够实现高效的机器遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器遗忘研究多集中于深度学习和图像数据，而在依赖表格数据的网络入侵检测领域存在研究缺口，亟需一种针对 XGBoost 等集成树模型的遗忘方法。

Method: 提出了一种针对 XGBoost 模型的 Machine Unlearning（机器遗忘）方法，并利用物联网 (IoT-23) 和通用网络入侵检测 (GeNIS) 等表格数据进行验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，XGBoost-Forget 在保持模型原有预测能力的同时，实现了显著高于重训练的遗忘效率，且遗忘质量在各项评估指标中表现良好。

Conclusion: XGBoost-Forget 是一种高效的机器学习遗忘方法，适用于表格数据领域的网络入侵检测，能够在保持模型预测性能的同时快速有效移除特定数据的影响。

Abstract: Machine Unlearning (MU) has emerged as an important technique for removing specific data points from trained models without requiring full retraining. However, most existing MU research focuses on deep learning and image data, leaving a gap in the domain of network intrusion detection, which relies heavily on tabular data. This work introduces XGBoost-Forget, an unlearning approach for the XGBoost model, to address this gap. The approach is evaluated on two tabular Network Intrusion (NI) datasets, IoT-23 and GeNIS, using multiple metrics to assess model performance, unlearning efficiency, and forgetting quality. The results show that XGBoost-Forget maintains predictive performance close to the original model while providing significantly faster unlearning, demonstrating its potential for MU in tabular NI settings.

</details>


### [196] [Complementary Attention Head Pruning for Efficient Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19150)
*Yaniv Livertovsky,Shahar Somin,Gonen Singer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 Transformer 注意力头的压缩问题，本文提出了 CAHP 架构，通过图论聚类和信息论方法自动识别并保留具有功能多样性的注意头，实现了无需人工调参的高效剪枝，并解决了传统梯度法偏好保留输出层头的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 模型参数量巨大，在资源受限环境下部署困难；现有的结构化剪枝方法（如梯度排序或随机门控）存在性能不稳定、结构退化以及过度依赖超参数调节的问题。

Method: CAHP (Complementary Attention Head Pruning)：将多头注意力剪枝建模为一个全局图论问题；利用图聚类和信息论距离度量来识别并保留拓扑多样性的补集注意头；采用基于边际性能递减曲线的机制，自动确定最优修剪比例，无需人工预设 sparsity。

Result: 在 SST-5 和 MNLI 基准测试中，CAHP 在不同规模的模型上均优于现有的前沿剪枝方法，尤其在高压缩率下表现卓越；同时成功避免了梯度法常见的“邻近偏差”（即倾向于保留靠近输出层的头），能够保留关键的中间层功能性注意头。

Conclusion: CAHP 提出了一种基于图论的后置修剪框架，通过识别补集性注意头，克服了传统基于梯度的修剪方法存在的拓扑偏差和繁琐的超参数调整问题，在保持高性能的同时实现了有效的模型压缩。

Abstract: The remarkable success of Transformer-based models in natural language processing stems from architectural scaling, which leads to a large number of parameters and hinders deployment in resource-constrained environments. While structured pruning offers a pathway to compression, existing state-of-the-art methods often rely on gradient-based importance ranking or stochastic gating, which suffer from instability, structural degeneration, and the need for extensive manual hyperparameter tuning. In this paper, we introduce CAHP (Complementary Attention Head Pruning), a novel post-hoc framework that redefines head selection as a global graph-theoretical problem. Rather than evaluating heads in isolation, CAHP utilizes graph-based clustering combined with information-theoretic distance measures to identify and preserve a topologically diverse subset of complementary attention heads. Without requiring a predefined sparsity level or pruning ratio, the framework automatically determines the number of selected attention heads across layers by identifying a diminishing marginal performance curve, where pruning additional heads leads to a sharp degradation in performance, as determined by the chosen polynomial degree. Extensive evaluations on the SST-5 and MNLI benchmarks, across different Transformer model scales, demonstrate that CAHP consistently outperforms competitive baselines, particularly in high-compression regimes. Furthermore, our structural analysis shows that CAHP avoids the "proximity bias" of gradient-based pruning methods, which tend to preserve heads mainly in layers close to the output, and instead retains a functionally critical set of attention heads in the model's intermediate layers.

</details>


### [197] [Mechanism-Guided Selective Unlearning for RLVR-Induced Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19222)
*Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Shuning Wu,Xu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAST通过精准定位并只更新关键的注意力权重，实现了模型在特定推理能力上的“选择性遗忘”，有效避免了全参数更新带来的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全参数梯度上升方法在进行RLVR引起的推理遗忘时，往往会因参数耦合导致在保留任务（如GSM8K）上产生严重的性能下降（附带损害）。

Method: 提出MAST（机制对齐选择性目标定位）方法：通过分析注意力投影张量的“非主能量（off-principal energy）”、更新幅度和遗忘梯度耦合程度进行排序，仅针对性地更新排序最高的张量子集，而非全参数更新。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B和Qwen3-1.7B模型上，MAST成功实现了统计学意义上的目标遗忘（MATH遗忘达标），同时在保留任务上表现优异（GSM8K持平或微增，MATH保留性能损害极小），优于传统的全参数微调方法。

Conclusion: MAST是一种高效的RLVR推理遗忘方法，通过针对性修改关键注意力投影张量，实现了在遗忘目标任务的同时，将对保留任务（GSM8K/MATH）的附带损害降至最低，解决了全参数微调遗忘带来的灾难性遗忘问题。

Abstract: We propose MAST (Mechanism-Aligned Selective Targeting), a mechanism-guided method for unlearning RLVR-induced reasoning with substantially lower collateral damage than standard full-parameter updates. In matched SFT/RLVR checkpoints on Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B and Qwen3-1.7B-Base, the SFT-to-RLVR increment differs sharply from the SFT update in token-level delta-log-probability, and full-parameter gradient ascent forgets only by damaging retain MATH and GSM8K. MAST ranks attention-projection tensors by off-principal energy, update magnitude, and forget-gradient coupling magnitude, then updates only the top-ranked subset. On the primary model, MAST induces statistically significant target forgetting (MATH forget 45/150 to 37/150; McNemar p=0.0078) while preserving GSM8K (+0.8 pp) and MATH retain (-0.5 pp). The advantage reproduces across seeds, NPO/SimNPO objectives, and Qwen3, where MAST preserves GSM8K while full-parameter unlearning collapses it.

</details>


### [198] [The Reward Was in Your Data All Along: Correcting Flow Matching with Discriminator-Guided RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19162)
*Nicolas Beltran-Velez,Felix Friedrich,Zhang Xiaofeng,Reyhane Askari-Hemmat,Xiaochuang Han,Adriana Romero-Soriano,Michal Drozdzal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种无需人工偏好的 DRL 方法，通过在预训练特征空间中利用判别器提供的最优对数似然比奖励来引导强化学习，从而校正生成模型的结构性偏差，在提升图像保真度的同时增强语义质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的得分匹配和流匹配模型在优化目标（训练时域的 $\ell_2$ 回归）与推理时的视觉/语义质量之间存在不匹配，导致模型过度依赖昂贵且主观的偏好强化学习来纠正此类结构性缺陷。

Method: 提出“判别器引导强化学习”（Discriminator-Guided RL, DRL）：1. 在预训练表示空间中训练判别器以区分真实数据与模型生成样本；2. 将判别器的 logit 作为奖励，通过 KL 正则化的强化学习（RL）优化模型；3. 该方法利用对数似然比作为理论最优奖励，实现向真实数据分布的对齐。

Result: 在 SiT、JiT、REPA 和 RAE 等模型上表现出色，例如在 SiT 上将无引导 FID 从 9.38 降至 2.62，并在 DINOv3 语义空间 FD 上显著提升；同时改善了后续偏好对齐后的帕累托前沿（Pareto frontier），减少了过饱和与过亮等伪影。

Conclusion: DRL 在无需人工偏好的情况下，通过使用预训练表示空间中的判别器作为奖励函数，有效解决了匹配模型（Score-based/flow-matching）训练目标与推理样本质量之间的结构性不匹配问题，显著提升了图像保真度和分布对齐效果。

Abstract: Score- and flow-matching models often rely on preference-based reinforcement learning for two purposes: aligning with subjective preferences and, surprisingly, recovering properties such as visual realism and coherent object structure that matching-based training is intended to learn from the data itself. We argue that this reflects a structural mismatch. Matching losses measure $\ell_2$ regression error on the velocity or score field under training-time marginals, a proxy poorly aligned with the visual and semantic properties that determine sample quality at inference. Given a reward aligned with these properties, RL sidesteps the mismatch by evaluating the model on its own samples and following the reward landscape directly. The challenge is to obtain such a reward without relying on human preferences, which are expensive and conflate data realism with annotator inclinations.
  We propose Discriminator-Guided RL (DRL). DRL trains a discriminator to separate data from base-model samples in a pretrained representation space and uses its logit as the reward in KL-regularized RL. The pretrained space restricts the discriminator to perceptually meaningful directions, and the logit estimates the log-likelihood ratio between data and model, which is the optimal reward for targeting the data distribution. Across SiT, JiT, REPA, and RAE, DRL reduces guidance-free FID (e.g., $9.38 \to 2.62$ on SiT) and semantic-space FD (e.g., $88.2 \to 19.3$ on DINOv3 for SiT), with consistent gains across all backbones, and improves human-preference rewards without training on them. It also yields a better Pareto frontier between preference reward and image fidelity under subsequent preference-based post-training, increasing alignment while reducing low-level artifacts such as oversaturation and excessive brightness.

</details>


### [199] [Explaining Attention with Program Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19317)
*Amiri Hayes,Belinda Li,Jacob Andreas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种利用大语言模型将Transformer注意力头自动转换为可执行Python代码的管道技术，在保持模型高性能的同时，通过符号化表示实现了神经网络的高度可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决深度学习模型中“不透明神经计算”的瓶颈问题，通过将复杂的神经注意力机制转化为人类可读且可执行的符号化程序，以实现模型的可解释性与透明度。

Method: 提出了一种利用预训练LLM对注意力头行为进行逆向工程的方法：1. 在随机样本上计算目标注意力矩阵；2. 将注意力模式总结并提示给LLM，使其生成复现这些模式的Python程序；3. 在保留测试集上对生成的程序进行重排序和验证。

Result: 实验表明，不到1000个生成的程序即可复现GPT-2、TinyLlama-1.1B和Llama-3B的注意力模式，TinyStories上的IoU超过75%；用这些程序替换25%的注意力头，模型困惑度仅增加16%，且各项问答任务指标保持稳定。

Conclusion: 该研究通过利用LLM生成代码来模拟Transformer注意力头，成功验证了将神经网络组件转化为人类可解释、可执行符号化代码的可行性，为弥合神经网络黑箱与符号逻辑之间的鸿沟提供了有效的可扩展范式。

Abstract: A longstanding goal of research on interpretable deep learning is to replace opaque neural computations with human-meaningful symbolic descriptions. In this paper, we propose an approach for approximating the behavior of components of deep networks with executable programs. We focus on attention heads in transformer language models. For a given head, we first compute its associated attention matrices on a collection of randomly selected training examples. Next, we prompt a pre-trained language model with a summary of these matrices, and instruct it to generate a set of Python programs that can reproduce the associated attention patterns given only text from the input sentence. Finally, we re-rank programs according to how well our final set of programs predict behavior on held-out inputs. We demonstrate that a set of fewer than 1,000 such generated programs can reproduce the attention patterns of heads in GPT-2, TinyLlama-1.1B, and Llama-3B, achieving an average Intersection-over-Union similarity above 75% on TinyStories. Moreover, the best-fit programs can replace neural attention heads without substantially affecting model behavior: replacing 25% of attention heads with programmatic surrogates across the three models incurs only a 16% average perplexity increase, while maintaining performance on a variety of downstream question answering benchmarks. This work contributes a scalable pipeline for reverse-engineering attention heads in transformer models using human-readable, executable code, advancing a path toward symbolic transparency in neural models.

</details>


### [200] [AGDN: Learning to Solve Traveling Salesman Problem with Anisotropic Graph Diffusion Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19185)
*Bolin Shen,Ziwei Huang,Zhiguang Cao,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AGDN 提出了一种新型各向异性图扩散网络，通过融合节点相似性与距离的 MixScore 矩阵及多跳扩散机制，显著提升了 GNN 在解决 TSP 问题时的拓扑利用率与求解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图学习的 TSP 方法在利用图结构有效性方面存在不足，特别是无法在全连接图中提供充分的拓扑先验，且传统的图稀疏化技术往往会导致最优解中的关键节点丢失。

Method: 1. 构建 MixScore 转移矩阵：结合节点相似性和成对距离，优化图结构；2. 开发各向异性图扩散（Anisotropic Graph Diffusion）策略：实现多跳间的高效信息交换，避免了传统图稀疏化方法导致的连接丢失。

Result: 实验结果表明 AGDN 在不同规模和分布的 TSP 实例上均优于现有的同类方法，且计算效率具有竞争力，并表现出良好的零样本泛化性能。

Conclusion: AGDN 通过引入各向异性图扩散机制，有效解决了 TSP 问题中信息传递和拓扑结构利用的局限性，在多种规模和分布下展现了优越的性能与泛化能力。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) is a cornerstone of combinatorial optimization and arises in many practical scenarios. Although graph-based learning approaches have been explored for TSP, the question of how to exploit graph structure more effectively remains open. We present the Anisotropic Graph Diffusion Network (AGDN), a new Graph Neural Network framework designed to solve TSP. Our method tackles two central difficulties: (1) the lack of informative topological prior in fully connected TSP graphs, and (2) losing connected nodes in the optimal solution after the commonly used graph sparsification techniques. To overcome these issues, we construct a MixScore transition matrix that merges node similarity with pairwise distance, and we develop an anisotropic graph diffusion strategy that supports efficient information exchange across multiple hops. Comprehensive experiments spanning diverse instance sizes and node distributions show that AGDN consistently outperforms existing methods while keeping computation time competitive. Furthermore, AGDN generalizes well to problem sizes and distributions beyond those seen during training. The implementation is publicly available at: https://github.com/LabRAI/AGDN.

</details>


### [201] [SCAN: Enhance Time Series Anomaly Detection via Multi-Scale Neighborhood-Centered Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19255)
*Xingze Zheng,Hanyin Cheng,Siyuan Wang,Yiting Hao,Peng Chen,Yuan Jun,Yang Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决重构类异常检测方法的泛化难题，提出 SCAN 框架，通过引入多尺度聚类，利用聚类中心约束表征及双重判别准则，显著提升了异常检测的准确性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于重构的时间序列异常检测方法难以在“过泛化”（将异常视为正常）与“欠泛化”（无法准确重构正常模式）之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出 SCAN 框架：在表征层利用聚类中心约束正常模式的重构，防止模型容量过大导致过泛化；在判别层结合聚类隶属概率与重构误差，构建双重异常检测准则；并采用邻域中心表征进行多视图聚类以提升聚类效果。

Result: 在多个不同应用领域的真实数据集上展现了超越现有主流方法的性能（SOTA）。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 SCAN 方法通过多尺度聚类有效地平衡了重构算法中的过泛化与欠泛化问题，在多个真实数据集上达到了最先进的性能。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection plays a crucial role in a wide range of real-world applications. Reconstruction-based methods have become the mainstream paradigm, but they suffer from over-generalization and under-generalization problems, which are challenging to balance. To address this, we introduce multi-scale clustering to enhance reconstruction-based methods. At the representation level, we integrate the cluster center representations of normal patterns to constrain the model to target representative normal patterns for reconstruction, preventing dominance of powerful capacity and representation capability. At the anomaly criterion level, we derive anomaly confidence score based on cluster membership probability and combine it with reconstruction error, providing dual criteria for detection. Furthermore, the effectiveness of the cluster center representations and anomaly confidence score depends on the clustering performance. Accordingly, we extract neighborhood-centered representations for multi-view clustering to improve clustering performance. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world datasets from diverse application domains demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of SCAN.

</details>


### [202] [Detecting Hidden ML Training With Zero-Overhead Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19262)
*Robi Rahman,Sabiha Tajdari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验证明，即便开发者采取对抗性规避手段，利用GPU底层的硬件物理遥测信号（NVML）仍能有效且高精度地识别出AI训练工作负载，证实了硬件级监测对AI治理的支撑潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI计算治理的前提是能够有效监控GPU工作负载，但如果开发者可以轻易规避监测机制，治理方案将失效。因此，需要评估基于底层物理遥测的监控系统在对抗环境下的鲁棒性。

Method: 通过利用NVML（NVIDIA Management Library）提供的零开销、隐私保护型遥测数据（如功耗、利用率等非内容性物理信号），训练分类器对GPU工作负载（如模型训练）进行识别，并针对20种对抗性规避策略进行了5轮迭代博弈测试。

Result: 分类器在全语料库中达到了98.2%的训练任务识别精度，在面对各类对抗性伪装处理时，对最具挑战性的异常工作负载仍能保持43-87%的识别准确率。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于NVML遥测技术的GPU工作负载监测具有高度的鲁棒性，即使面对复杂的对抗性规避策略，也能保持较好的识别效果，从而为AI计算治理提供了技术可行性基础。

Abstract: Hardware-enabled monitoring of GPU workloads underpins many proposals for AI compute governance, but if developers can defeat monitoring mechanisms, such schemes are unworkable. We evaluate the adversarial robustness of GPU workload classification using only zero-overhead, privacy-preserving NVML telemetry: content-agnostic signals that observe physical effects of computation without accessing model weights, training data, or hyperparameters. Across 5 rounds of monitor-evader iteration, we evaluate 20 evasion strategy families on 9 GPU models spanning 4 architecture generations. We develop a classifier that achieves 98.2% binary accuracy at identifying training workloads across the whole corpus, and 43-87% accuracy against the most challenging unexpected workloads even when they are adversarially disguised.

</details>


### [203] [Risk Stratification for ICU Delirium using Pervasive Ambient Sensing Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19292)
*Jiaqing Zhang,Sabyasachi Bandyopadhyay,Miguel Contreras,Jessica Sena,Yuanfang Ren,Andrea Davidson,Ziyuan Guan,Tezcan Ozrazgat-Baslanti,Subhash Nerella,Azra Bihorac,Parisa Rashidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究证实ICU环境中的声音和光照强度可作为预测谵妄的独立指标，其中声音特征最具预测价值，集成环境数据可有效提升临床谵妄风险监测水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICU谵妄严重影响患者预后，但现有风险评估往往忽视光照和噪声等环境因素；研究旨在验证这些环境因素是否能独立预测谵妄。

Method: 研究人员利用来自9个ICU的309名患者数据，评估了四种高效序列神经网络模型（其中卷积模型表现最佳）。通过Shapley加性解释（SHAP）分析特征重要性与影响方向，对10个不同的预测窗口进行预测。

Result: 基于音频数据的预测模型在各维度均表现出色，卷积模型AUC达0.80。声音是预测谵妄的主导指标，将声音与光照结合可优化短期（<1周）预测效果。

Conclusion: 被动环境感知（特别是声音）能为ICU谵妄风险评估提供具有临床意义且可解释的信号，是将环境因素纳入ICU多模态预测与预防策略的可行路径。

Abstract: Delirium is a common and serious complication in the Intensive Care Unit (ICU), associated with increased morbidity, prolonged hospital stays, and higher healthcare costs. Despite its prevalence, early prediction and prevention remain challenging. Environmental factors such as ambient sound and light may influence the onset of delirium, yet they are often overlooked in risk assessments. In this study, we examined whether light intensity and sound pressure levels can independently predict delirium across multiple prediction horizons. We evaluated four efficient sequential neural network models on data collected from 9 ICUs across 309 patients to predict delirium for 10 prediction-window sizes. We reported feature importance and direction of influence using Shapley Additive Explanations analysis. The convolutional model achieved the strongest discrimination, with AUC = 0.80 on sound data and on combined data. Sound features were the dominant predictors overall. Integrating sound with light improved short-term ($<1$ week) prediction, with the combined model assigning the highest risk immediately after the sensing period. These findings suggest that passive ambient sensing, especially sound, can add a clinically meaningful, interpretable signal for delirium risk estimation and offer a practical pathway to enrich multimodal ICU prediction and prevention strategies.

</details>


### [204] [P-K-GCN: Physics-augmented Koopman-enhanced Graph Convolutional Network for Deep Spatiotemporal Super-resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19303)
*Xizhuo,Zhang,Zekai Wang,Fei Liu,Bing Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出P-K-GCN模型，通过结合图神经网络、Koopman算子理论与物理约束损失，实现了复杂物理系统（如不规则几何上的心脏电动力学）的高保真、高鲁棒时空超分辨率重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的时空模拟计算成本极高，而现有的数据驱动方法在物理一致性、处理不规则几何形状以及复杂时空演化建模方面存在不足。

Method: 提出了一种物理增强的Koopman增强图卷积网络（P-K-GCN）。包含三个核心组件：① 基于连续样条的图卷积网络（GCN）处理不规则几何空间依赖；② Koopman算子理论将非线性演化映射至线性化的潜在空间；③ 加入物理约束的损失函数以确保重建符合物理定律。此外，通过Rademacher复杂度和泛化界分析提供了理论保障。

Result: 在3D心脏电动力学的超分辨率重建任务中，P-K-GCN相比现有基准模型表现出更高的重建精度，且具备更好的稳健性。

Conclusion: P-K-GCN提供了一种在不规则几何结构上进行时空超分辨率重建的有效框架，通过物理增强和Koopman算子理论，实现了比基准模型更高预测保真度和鲁棒性的效果，并从理论上证明了其误差减少的合理性。

Abstract: High-fidelity simulation of spatiotemporal dynamics is computationally prohibitive, necessitating efficient super-resolution techniques to reconstruct high-resolution data from coarse-grained inputs. Traditional data-driven methods often lack physical constraints, and simple physics-informed learning struggles with irregular spatial geometries and intricately evolving temporal dynamics. To tackle these challenges, we propose a Physics-augmented Koopman-enhanced Graph Convolutional Network (P-K-GCN) for spatiotemporal super-resolution on irregular geometries. Specifically, a continuous spline-based GCN is first designed to extract spatial dependencies directly from coarse graph, and Koopman operator theory is incorporated to project the nonlinear dynamics into a compact latent space where temporal progression is linearized. Second, we augment the optimization objective with a physics-based loss to force the data-driven reconstructions to adhere to physical laws for improving predictive fidelity and robustness. Finally, we provide a rigorous theoretical analysis, establishing that the physics augmentation and Koopman regularization mathematically guarantees a reduction in super-resolution error by diminishing Rademacher complexity and tightening generalization bounds. We evaluate our framework on reconstructing spatially high-resolution cardiac electrodynamics across a 3D heart geometry from sparse low-resolution measurements. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior accuracy compared to baseline models.

</details>


### [205] [Diffusion-Proof: Recipe for Formal Theorem Proving Beyond Auto-Regressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19315)
*Ruida Wang,Rui Pan,Pengcheng Wang,Shizhe Diao,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Diffusion-Proof是首个将扩散大模型应用于形式化数学证明的框架，通过全证明生成与局部错误修正机制，有效克服了传统自回归模型在长推理链中的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自回归(AR)大语言模型在形式化定理证明中存在长距离相干性差和误差累积问题，限制了其推理性能。因此，研究人员探索利用扩散大模型(dLLMs)通过迭代去噪生成方式来解决这些局限性。

Method: 提出了“Diffusion-Proof”框架，包含两个核心模型：1. dLLM-Prover-7B：用于全证明序列生成，通过多令牌块迭代去噪提升长距离相干性；2. dLLM-Corrector-7B：利用扩散模型的填充能力，结合双向信息进行局部证明修正。

Result: 在ProofNet-Test上取得了1.61%的绝对提升，在MiniF2F-Test上取得了6.14%的绝对提升，并成功解决了一道深度推理模型DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B未能解决的IMO题目。

Conclusion: Diffusion-Proof 框架证实了扩散模型在形式化数学证明领域显著优于传统的自回归模型，特别是在长距离相干性和错误修正方面具有独特优势。

Abstract: Enhancing the formal math reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) has become a key focus in both mathematical and computer science communities in recent years. While significant progress has been made in using state-of-the-art Auto-Regressive (AR) LLMs for formal theorem proving, these models suffer from inherent limitations. Their next-token prediction generation methods may yield suboptimal performance due to the challenges of long-range coherence and the compounding of errors over long sequences. Recent advancements in diffusion LLMs (dLLMs), which generate text through iterative denoising of a multi-token block, offer a promising alternative. However, the application of dLLMs to formal mathematics, where maintaining long-range coherence is critical, remains largely understudied. To address the challenges above, we propose **Diffusion-Proof**, to the best of our knowledge, the first framework to train and apply dLLMs for formal theorem proving. Our frameworks contain training and inference methods for two models. The first one is *dLLM-Prover-7B*, which performs whole-proof writing with long-range coherent tactic usage. The second one is *dLLM-Corrector-7B*, which is a novel large block diffusion-based correction model. It leverages the in-filling capabilities of dLLMs to perform local proof correction using bi-directional information. Extensive experiments demonstrate that **Diffusion-Proof** relatively significantly outperforms the AR LLM baseline trained under the same dataset. **Diffusion-Proof** achieves an absolute improvement of **1.61%** on ProofNet-Test and **6.14%** on MiniF2F-Test benchmarks compare to the baseline. Notably, **Diffusion-Proof** successfully resolves one IMO problem that more advanced thinking model DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B could not solve, showcasing the unique advantage of dLLMs in formal theorem proving.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [206] [Mixed-Precision Communication-Avoiding SGD for Generalized Linear Models on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18463)
*Aditya Devarakonda,Irene Simó Muñoz,Giulia Guidi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合精度通信规避 SGD（CA-SGD）方法，通过优化通信同步策略与低精度硬件计算结合，在保持高模型精度的前提下，实现了显著的分布式训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式 SGD 训练受限于通信开销（AllReduce 同步），而非计算能力。现代 GPU 具备矩阵加速硬件和低精度支持，能够通过增加少量计算来换取更少的同步点，从而缓解通信瓶颈。

Method: 提出了一种混合精度通信规避随机梯度下降（CA-SGD）算法。该方法通过每 $s$ 次迭代进行一次 AllReduce（对 $sb\times sb$ Gram 矩阵进行操作）来分摊通信成本。核心策略包括输入矩阵和边缘向量使用低精度存储，Gram 矩阵计算使用高精度累加，通信使用高精度，而内部递归和权重更新则保持高精度，并给出了基于硬件低精度单位舍入误差的精度控制分析。

Result: 在 NERSC Perlmutter A100 GPU 上，该方法在 logistic、linear 和 Poisson 回归任务中，损失函数与 FP32 SGD 的差距在 0.5% 以内，并在多个数据集（epsilon, SUSY, HIGGS 等）上实现了 5.1-6.8 倍的加速。

Conclusion: 混合精度 CA-SGD 在保持与 FP32 SGD 相当精度的同时，通过减少同步频率显著提升了分布式训练的性能，且该方法可跨不同 GPU 代际迁移。

Abstract: Distributed stochastic gradient descent (SGD) is limited by communication rather than computation, since each iteration requires an AllReduce across processes. Communication-avoiding SGD (CA-SGD) amortizes communication over $s$ iterations by replacing $s$ consecutive AllReduces with a single AllReduce of an $sb\times sb$ Gram matrix, trading more computation and bandwidth for fewer synchronization points. Modern GPUs with matrix hardware and reduced-precision formats offset this by accelerating the Gram GEMM and shrinking BF16 traffic. We study mixed-precision CA-SGD for generalized linear models on NVIDIA GPUs. Our finite-precision analysis decomposes the local rounding error of one CA-SGD outer iteration into nine independent precision choices, depending on the hardware only through its low-precision unit roundoffs, so the resulting recipes transfer in principle across GPU generations. The recipe stores the input matrix and margin vector in low precision, computes the Gram matrix from low-precision inputs with high-precision accumulation, communicates it in high precision, and performs the inner recurrence and weight updates in high precision. On NERSC Perlmutter A100 GPUs, mixed-precision CA-SGD matches FP32 SGD loss within $0.5\%$ on logistic, linear, and Poisson problems and reaches $5.1$--$6.8\times$ speedup over FP32 SGD on epsilon, SUSY, HIGGS, synth, and Poisson-synth. Our software is available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.20448273

</details>


### [207] [Flexible Distributed Particle Filtering for the Internet of Things via Aggregate Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18483)
*Angela Cortecchia,Davide Domini,Giovanni Ciatto,Roberto Casadei,Danilo Pianini,Mirko Viroli*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用聚合计算（AC）构建分布式粒子滤波（DPF）的方法，通过将滤波逻辑与调度策略解耦，提升了DPF在异构物联网环境中的适应性与设计灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式粒子滤波（DPF）算法主要依赖于特定的架构和通信假设，在异构且开放的物联网场景中缺乏灵活性和适应性。

Method: 基于聚合计算（AC）的场（field-based）分布式粒子滤波建模方法，将估计过程和信息传播表达为计算场，从而解耦了滤波逻辑与协调策略。

Result: 通过计算机仿真验证，证明了在统一框架下可以导出多种DPF配置，并揭示了精度、通信成本和鲁棒性之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该方法将聚合计算（Aggregate Computing, AC）确立为一种有效的抽象层，能够支持在开放式物联网（IoT）环境中设计适应性强的分布式粒子滤波（DPF）解决方案。

Abstract: State estimation from uncertain, distributed observations is central in many cyber-physical applications. While Distributed Particle Filtering (DPF) algorithms address nonlinear and non-Gaussian estimations in distributed settings, most solutions remain tied to specific architectures and communication assumptions, limiting adaptability in open, heterogeneous deployments-most notably, the Internet of Things (IoT). In this paper, we propose a field-based formulation of Distributed Particle Filtering grounded in Aggregate Computing (AC). By expressing estimation and information dissemination as computational fields, our approach decouples the core filtering logic from coordination and data-flow strategies. This enables systematic customisation of key design dimensions, including fusion-center placement and resilience, aggregated measurement functions, as well as the type and scope of information propagation. Through a set of in-silico experiments, we show how diverse DPF configurations can be derived within a unified framework, highlighting trade-offs among accuracy, communication cost, and robustness. Overall, the proposed approach positions AC as an effective abstraction layer for engineering adaptable DPF solutions in open IoT environments.

</details>


### [208] [Splaxel: Efficient Distributed Training of 3D Gaussian Splatting for Large-scale Scene Reconstruction via Pixel-level Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18588)
*Wenqi Jia,Zhewen Hu,Ying Huang,Yu Gong,Stavros Kalafatis,Yuke Wang,Wei Niu,Chengming Zhang,Ang Li,Sheng Di,Yuede Ji,Bo Fang,Miao Yin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Splaxel 是一种新型分布式 3DGS 训练框架，通过由“同步 Gaussian 模型”转为“同步局部渲染像素值”，大幅降低了通信负担，从而实现了大规模场景下的高效、高质量 3D 重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分布式 3DGS 训练方法存在瓶颈：场景分割导致全局不一致，或全局 Gaussian 参数同步带来巨大的通信开销，导致训练效率随场景规模增大而迅速下降。

Method: 提出一种基于像素级局部渲染与全局合成的训练框架。具体包括：1. 放弃高昂的 Gaussian 参数同步，改为交换渲染后的局部像素值；2. 引入基于几何和透射率的可见性预测以消除冗余通信；3. 设计无冲突的相机视角整合策略以优化 GPU 利用率。

Result: 在包含高达 1.2 亿个 Gaussian 的大规模数据集上，Splaxel 相比当前最优的分布式 3DGS 框架实现了最高 7.6 倍的提速，且重建质量与基准持平。

Conclusion: Splaxel 成功解决了大规模 3DGS 训练中通信开销大和全局一致性难以兼顾的矛盾，通过像素级分发实现了高效的可扩展性，在大规模场景中不仅提升了训练速度，还保持了高质量的重建效果。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-fidelity and real-time 3D scene reconstruction, but scaling training to large-scale scenes requires optimizing hundreds of millions of Gaussians across multiple GPUs. Existing distributed approaches either partition scenes into isolated regions, causing global inconsistency, or rely on global Gaussian-level exchanges, which lead to substantial growth in inter-GPU communication and quickly dominate iteration time.
  We propose Splaxel, a communication-efficient distributed 3DGS training framework based on pixel-level local rendering and global composition. Instead of synchronizing Gaussians, each GPU renders its local subset and exchanges only partial pixel values, maintaining mathematical consistency while keeping communication cost stable as the scene size increases. Splaxel further reduces pixel-level redundancy through geometric and transmittance visibility prediction and improves GPU utilization via conflict-free camera-view consolidation. Evaluated on large-scale datasets with up to 120M Gaussians, Splaxel achieves up to 7.6$\times$ speedup over the state-of-the-art distributed 3DGS framework while preserving high reconstruction quality.

</details>


### [209] [ShuntServe: Cost-Efficient LLM Serving on Heterogeneous Spot GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18600)
*Seungwoo Jeong,Moohyun Song,Juhyun Park,Kyungyong Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ShuntServe 是一款针对异构 GPU 竞价实例集群设计的 LLM 服务系统，通过智能的资源优化和高效的故障迁移机制，在保证吞吐量的同时实现了大幅成本节约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 服务系统多面向同构集群，难以应对云环境下 GPU 竞价实例（Spot Instances）的高频中断且在异构集群中表现出的负载不均衡问题。

Method: 1. 基于屋顶线模型（Roofline Model）的性能估计器；2. 基于动态规划的模型放置与资源调度优化器（配置、并行化策略、层分配）；3. 实现“输出保持”请求迁移（Output-preserving migration）与基于共享张量存储的并发初始化，以实现快速故障恢复。

Result: 在 Llama-3.1-70B 和 Qwen3-32B 模型及 AWS 异构 GPU 集群（L4, A10G, L40S）的测试中，ShuntServe 的吞吐量比现有最优方案高出 1.35x-1.42x，且相比即需即用（On-demand）实例降低了约 31% 的成本。

Conclusion: ShuntServe 有效解决了在异构 GPU 竞价实例集群中部署大模型的挑战，通过性能评估、动态规划优化和高效的故障恢复机制，显著提升了吞吐量并大幅降低了服务成本。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) services become widely adopted, the cost of GPU resources for serving these models in cloud environments has emerged as a critical concern. Spot instances offer up to 90% cost savings over on-demand instances, but their frequent interruptions and limited availability pose significant challenges for continuous LLM serving. GPU spot instances, in particular, exhibit lower and more volatile availability than CPU-based instances, making homogeneous clusters that depend on a single GPU type vulnerable to correlated failures. Heterogeneous clusters spanning multiple GPU types can address this by leveraging complementary availability patterns across diverse spot pools, yet existing LLM serving systems are designed for homogeneous environments and suffer from load imbalance when deployed on heterogeneous GPUs. This paper presents ShuntServe, a cost-efficient LLM serving system for heterogeneous spot GPU clusters. ShuntServe employs a roofline model-based analytical serving performance estimator and a dynamic programming-based model placement optimizer that jointly determines node configuration, parallelization strategy, and layer assignment to maximize throughput across heterogeneous GPUs. To enhance fault tolerance when using spot instances, ShuntServe combines output-preserving request migration with concurrent initialization via a shared tensor store, minimizing migration downtime by overlapping replacement node preparation with ongoing serving. Evaluation on Llama-3.1-70B and Qwen3-32B with a heterogeneous AWS cluster of L4, A10G, and L40S GPUs shows that ShuntServe achieves 1.42x and 1.35x higher throughput than state-of-the-art baselines and attains 31.9% and 31.2% cost efficiency improvements over on-demand instances for offline and online serving, respectively.

</details>


### [210] [HI-HCQC: A Tightly-Coupled Hardware Interface with High-Efficiency Communication for Hybrid Classical-Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18642)
*Shibo Liang,Junchao Wang,Zeyuan Wang,Feng Wang,Xiaoyu Li,Lei Li,FuDong Liu,Zheng Shan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了HI-HCQC，一种基于RFSoC和PCIe的高效硬件接口方案，解决了混合量子-经典计算中通信延迟高及吞吐量低的问题，有效提升了量子控制系统的实时响应能力与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的超导量子控制系统多采用以太网等松耦合接口，导致通信延迟过高且任务吞吐量受限，难以满足混合量子-经典计算中高频数据交换的需求。

Method: 设计并实现了一个名为HI-HCQC的基于RFSoC（射频系统芯片）的硬件接口。该方案集成了高速RF-DAC、RF-ADC、可编程逻辑电路、嵌入式处理器、时钟同步电路，并采用PCIe Gen3 x8接口实现与主机之间的高速数据传输，从而实现紧耦合控制。

Result: HI-HCQC支持6个控制通道和1个复用读出通道，实验验证了其在量子比特谱分析、Rabi振荡、T1测量、单次读出、随机基准测试及CZ门表征等方面的功能。与传统系统相比，显著降低了端到端执行延迟并大幅提升了系统任务吞吐量。

Conclusion: 基于PCIe协议、采用RFSoC架构的紧耦合硬件接口设计方案，为实现可扩展且高效的混合量子-经典计算系统提供了可靠的硬件基础。

Abstract: Hybrid classical-quantum computing requires frequent data exchange between classical processors and quantum control hardware. However, existing superconducting quantum control systems are commonly connected through loosely coupled interfaces such as Ethernet, resulting in high communication latency and limited task throughput. To address this issue, we present HI-HCQC, an RFSoC-based hardware interface for tightly coupled hybrid classical-quantum computing. HI-HCQC integrates high-speed RF-DACs, RF-ADCs, programmable logic, embedded processors, clock synchronization circuits, and a PCIe Gen3 x8 interface, enabling direct microwave pulse synthesis, qubit readout, and high-throughput data transfer between host servers and quantum measurement-control units. Experimental results show that HI-HCQC supports six control channels and one multiplexed readout channel, achieves stable microwave generation and acquisition, and successfully performs qubit spectroscopy, Rabi oscillation, T1 measurement, single-shot readout, randomized benchmarking, and CZ-gate characterization. Compared with a conventional control system, HI-HCQC reduces end-to-end execution latency for representative quantum gate and circuit tasks and significantly improves task throughput. These results demonstrate that PCIe-coupled RFSoC control hardware provides a practical foundation for scalable and efficient hybrid classical-quantum computing systems.

</details>


### [211] [Re-Rooting-Based Fault-Tolerant One-to-All Broadcasting in Dense Eisenstein--Jacobi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18712)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对密集艾森斯坦-雅可比网络，本文提出了一种无需冗余结构的轻量级容错广播算法，通过智能重选广播源节点，在故障存在的情况下确保消息分发，且算法开销较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的密集艾森斯坦-雅可比网络广播算法在面对节点故障时无法保证消息的完整传递。为了提升并行及片上通信系统在故障环境下的鲁棒性，需要开发一种高效的容错广播机制。

Method: 提出了一种基于“重置源节点”（re-rooting）的容错算法。通过将广播源转移到特定的节点，使故障节点处于广播树的叶子位置，从而避开故障节点的转发压力。研究涵盖了一节点及二节点故障情况下的源选择策略，并证明了该方法的收敛时间受限于两倍的网络直径。

Result: 该方法可在 $O(\sqrt{N})$ 的时间内完成源节点选择；在存在一或两个节点故障时，广播可在两倍网络直径时间内完成；证明了该方法对两节点故障的普适性，并给出了三节点故障存在反例的界限。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种适用于密集艾森斯坦-雅可比（Eisenstein-Jacobi）网络的轻量级容错广播方法。该方法能够在存在一或两个节点故障的情况下，通过重置广播源节点确保消息成功分发，且无需额外的冗余通信结构，证明了其在网络通信可靠性提升方面的有效性。

Abstract: Dense Eisenstein--Jacobi networks are degree-six algebraic interconnection topologies with regular structure, vertex symmetry, small diameter, and efficient communication algorithms. These properties make them suitable for parallel and on-chip communication systems in which collective operations such as one-to-all broadcasting are frequent. Existing optimal broadcasting algorithms for dense hexagonal/Eisenstein--Jacobi networks assume fault-free operation. However, a faulty internal forwarding node may interrupt message propagation and prevent complete delivery. This paper proposes a lightweight re-rooting-based fault-tolerant broadcasting method for dense Eisenstein--Jacobi networks. The main idea is to relocate the effective broadcast source to a new source node such that each faulty node is located at graph distance equal to the network diameter from the new source. Consequently, faulty nodes become leaf-level nodes in the broadcast process and are not required to forward the message. We present source-selection algorithms for one- and two-node failures and prove that for any pair of faulty nodes in a dense Eisenstein--Jacobi network there exists a common distance-diameter node that can serve as a valid re-rooted source. The source-selection procedure requires linear time in the network diameter. Equivalently, since $N=3t^2+3t+1$, the selection cost is $O(\sqrt{N})$ in the number of nodes. Since the standard one-to-all broadcast completes in one diameter time and the relocation phase is also bounded by one diameter, the proposed method completes in at most twice the network diameter. We also show that the two-fault guarantee does not generally extend to arbitrary three-fault configurations by giving an explicit counterexample. The proposed approach improves broadcast reliability without constructing redundant spanning trees, backup paths, or additional broadcast structures.

</details>


### [212] [Closed-Form and Constant-Time New-Source Selection for Fault-Tolerant Broadcasting in Dense Eisenstein--Jacobi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18714)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 为了解决Eisenstein-Jacobi网络在发生两个节点故障时广播结构的重建问题，本文提出了一种无需全网搜索、可在$O(1)$时间内求出备用广播源的闭式算法，并通过实验证明了其高效性与正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集Eisenstein-Jacobi网络中，当出现两个故障节点时，需要一种不需要对全网进行扫描或测试所有边界候选节点的高效方法来重新选择广播源，以实现容错广播。

Method: 通过将两故障问题转化为起源与差分节点间的边界相交问题，利用Eisenstein-Jacobi网络的六边形结构，将距离-$t$边界划分为六条有向边。通过求解商格下的相交方程（涉及常数数量的代数系统），并利用固定字长算术模型，实现了$O(1)$时间复杂度的计数与源选择算法。

Result: 开发了两套算法：第一套用于计数所有有效的新源，第二套用于为任意节点故障对选择有效源。在大规模采样仿真中（50万故障对及4万次重构试验），证明了该选择器始终能返回有效源，确保所有非故障节点均能收到广播。

Conclusion: 本文提出的闭式、常数时间算法能够在存在两个节点故障的情况下，有效计算并选择Eisenstein-Jacobi网络中的备用广播源，解决了以往需要全网搜索或耗时测试带来的效率问题，且通过大规模实验验证了其正确性。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant broadcasting in dense Eisenstein--Jacobi networks requires efficient recovery when faulty nodes disrupt the original broadcast structure. A re-rooting-based method guarantees that, for any two faulty nodes, a valid new source exists at maximum graph distance from both faults. However, identifying such a source without scanning the network or testing all boundary candidates remains an open practical problem. This paper presents a closed-form, constant-time algorithm for counting and selecting a valid new source in dense Eisenstein--Jacobi networks under two node faults. The two-fault problem is reduced to a boundary-intersection problem involving the origin and a difference node. The distance-$t$ boundary, where $t$ is the network diameter, is partitioned into six directed sides of the Eisenstein--Jacobi hexagon. Since the network is a quotient structure, intersection equations are solved modulo the defining lattice, requiring evaluation of seven quotient-lattice shifts across all $6\times 6$ side pairs, yielding at most $252$ algebraic systems. The first algorithm counts all valid new sources for faults at $0$ and $A$. The second algorithm selects one valid new source for arbitrary fault pairs by solving translated side-pair systems, verifying each candidate, and shifting back. Each system is either a non-parallel $2\times 2$ linear system with at most one candidate, or a parallel system whose feasible candidates form an integer interval. Both algorithms run in $O(1)$ time under the fixed-word arithmetic model. Computational validation over $500{,}000$ sampled fault pairs and $40{,}000$ re-rooting trials confirms correctness: the selector always returns a valid new source, and the recovered broadcast reaches all non-faulty nodes.

</details>


### [213] [Closed-Form and Constant-Time New-Source Selection for Fault-Tolerant Broadcasting in Dense Gaussian Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18715)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于稠密高斯网络容错广播的代数重定向方法，利用商格代数构造实现了源选择过程的常数时间复杂度（O(1)），消除了对网络尺寸的依赖，并在实验中展现了比传统边界搜索法更高的性能和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对稠密高斯网络中现有的容错广播重定向方法依赖于耗时的边界搜索这一局限性，旨在通过数学手段简化源选择过程，使其与网络规模解耦，从而实现更高效、稳定的广播算法。

Method: 1. 提出了一种基于商格（quotient-lattice）感知代数构造的计数方法，通过九个商格副本上的侧对区间（side-pair intervals）并集，实现常数时间内对有效源的精确计数；2. 设计了一种偏移直接选择器（shifted direct selector），将寻找满足距离约束的源点问题转化为求解十六个带符号的线性方程组，并利用克莱姆法则和区间端点法在常数时间内完成求解。

Result: 1. 实现了源点选择的O(1)时间复杂度，在k=200时比传统边界搜索快5.92倍；2. 验证集包括26,623个节点计数无误，500,000组故障对测试全部成功，40,000次重定向广播试验成功。

Conclusion: 该研究成功地将稠密高斯网络中的容错广播重定向问题转化为一种代数构造方法。通过消除对网络拓扑或边界搜索的依赖，实现了常数时间（O(1)）复杂度的重定向源选择，显著提升了算法效率，且验证了其在处理大规模网络时的稳定性和正确性。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant broadcasting in dense Gaussian networks is recovered by re-rooting the broadcast at a new source at maximum graph distance from the faulty nodes. This paper extends the re-rooting framework by replacing its boundary-search source-selection step with a quotient-lattice-aware algebraic construction. The first contribution is a constant-time counting method for valid new sources, formulated as an intersection of two diameter-$k$ boundary sets in the Gaussian quotient. The exact count is obtained by a fixed union of side-pair intervals over nine quotient-lattice copies, giving a closed-form procedure without scanning the network or boundary. The second contribution is a shifted direct selector for two arbitrary faulty nodes. Given faulty nodes $A$ and $B$, the problem is translated to $C=\operatorname{mod}_{G_k}(B-A)$, and the selector finds $P$ satisfying $d(P,0)=d(P,C)=k$. For each of nine quotient-lattice shifts, sixteen signed linear systems are checked. Nonparallel systems are solved via Cramer's rule; parallel systems are handled by interval-endpoint selection. At most $9\times16=144$ shifted sign cases are evaluated, giving $O(1)$ selection under the word-RAM model. Validation reports zero count mismatches over $26{,}623$ tested nodes, $500{,}000$ valid outputs over $500{,}000$ sampled fault pairs, and $40{,}000$ successful re-rooted broadcast trials. The shifted selector achieves a $5.92\times$ speedup over boundary search at $k=200$, remaining stable as $k$ increases. These results make new-source selection algebraic, bounded, and independent of network size.

</details>


### [214] [ReMP: Low-Downtime Runtime Model-Parallelism Reconfiguration for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18741)
*Haipeng Yuan,Kaining Zheng,Yongshu Bai,Yuchen Zhang,Yunquan Zhang,Baodong Wu,Xiang Gao,Daning Cheng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ReMP 是一款支持低停机时间的在线模型并行（TP/PP）重配置框架，通过解耦运行时状态和 KV 缓存迁移技术，实现了秒级动态调整并行拓扑，解决了传统静态配置系统在应对动态负载时的效率瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理系统对于模型并行（TP/PP）的配置是静态的，无法应对实时变化的推理负载。频繁重启以调整拓扑会导致数分钟的服务中断、KV 缓存丢失及极高的重计算开销，严重影响服务质量。

Method: 提出了 ReMP 框架，核心技术包括：(1) 将模型并行拓扑与运行时状态解耦；(2) 设计二维 KV 缓存迁移机制，在 TP/PP 变更后保存缓存状态；(3) 实现端到端在线重构，避免了传统的服务重启。

Result: 在 7B 到 70B 模型上，ReMP 将拓扑切换时间由分钟级缩短至 1-7 秒（加速比提升 10 倍至 100 倍以上）；在动态负载下，显著提升了 TTFT、TPOT 和输出吞吐量。

Conclusion: ReMP 通过在运行时动态调整模型并行拓扑，打破了传统静态推理系统的限制，显著降低了重配置开销，在动态负载环境下表现出优于固定配置的性能，证明了在线重配置的可行性和必要性。

Abstract: Current large language model (LLM) inference systems universally deploy ultra-large-scale models using a combination of Tensor Parallelism (TP) and Pipeline Parallelism (PP). However, existing systems treat the model parallelism topology as a static configuration that cannot be flexibly adjusted at runtime. This rigid design creates a fundamental contradiction with the dynamically changing inference workloads in real-world scenarios. State-of-the-art systems lack online reconfiguration capabilities and can only switch configurations by restarting the service, resulting in several minutes of service interruption, KV cache loss, and prohibitive recomputation overhead. To address this problem, this paper presents ReMP, a runtime model parallelism reconfiguration framework that supports low downtime. ReMP achieves dynamic adjustment through three key techniques: (1) decoupling the model parallelism topology from runtime state to avoid full service reconstruction; (2) designing a two-dimensional KV cache migration mechanism to preserve reusable cache states after TP/PP changes; and (3) implementing end-to-end online reconfiguration. Experiments demonstrate that ReMP can complete most topology switches within 1-7 seconds on models ranging from 7B to 70B parameters, achieving speedups of tens to over a hundred times compared to the restart approach. Moreover, ReMP significantly outperforms fixed configurations under dynamic workloads, delivering superior performance in terms of TTFT, TPOT, and output throughput.

</details>


### [215] [Compressed-Resident Genomics: Full-Pipeline Device-Resident GPU LZ77 Decode with Position-Invariant Random Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18900)
*Yakiv Shavidze*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对基因组学大数据处理瓶颈，本文扩展了ACEAPEX编码器，通过全GPU流水线、紧凑坐标索引和区域解码策略，实现了比特精确的超高速并行解压与低延迟随机访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基因组数据（如ENA中的fastq.gz）规模庞大且增长迅速，但作为基因组归档标准的gzip格式本质上是串行的，且现有的GPU解压缩方案缺乏高效的随机访问能力，CPU解压工具则速度瓶颈明显，无法满足大数据处理需求。

Method: 基于ACEAPEX编码器进行扩展：1. 实现了完整的设备驻留GPU解压流水线（熵解码与匹配解析均在GPU上完成）；2. 设计了基于紧凑坐标索引的位置无关随机访问策略；3. 引入了区域解码策略以优化显存利用率。

Result: 在FASTQ数据上实现了高达260GB/s的解压速度；随机读取延迟仅为0.362ms（比samtools faidx快约6倍）；在大规模基因组数据上，通过范围解码策略在显存受限情况下仍能保持165.7GB/s的有效处理速度。

Conclusion: 该研究成功实现了亚毫秒级的随机访问解码和突破性的并行解压速度，证明了构建高性能、开源的GPU基因组解压堆栈的可行性。

Abstract: Genomic archives grow faster than decompression keeps up: the European Nucleotide Archive holds tens of petabytes of fastq.gz, and gzip is fundamentally sequential. GPU decompressors (nvCOMP DEFLATE at ~50GB/s on A100) decode whole files with no random access; CPU genomic tools (CRAM, samtools) support region seeks but only at CPU speed. We extend ACEAPEX, an absolute-offset parallel LZ77 codec included in the official lzbench 2.3 release, with three contributions absent from our prior work. First, a full device-resident GPU decode pipeline (entropy and match resolution both on-device) reaching up to 260GB/s on FASTQ, closing the match-phase-only gap of the earlier paper. Second, position-invariant random access with a compact coordinate index: an arbitrary read decodes in 0.362ms, ~6x faster than warm samtools faidx, with a read-to-block index 6.3x smaller than a .fai. Third, a range-decode strategy that decouples output size from VRAM, sustaining 165.7GB/s on a 50GB genome where whole-file decode runs out of memory. All results are bit-perfect. We also measure Meta's open DietGPU ANS on H100 at 592GB/s decode, faster than the proprietary entropy stage we currently use, showing a fully open high-throughput stack is viable. Code is MIT-licensed.

</details>
