### [216] [Urban Limits as Design Constraints: Identifying Suitable Locations for Distributed, Photovoltaic-Powered Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18940)
*Justin Chikhaoui,Thomas Leduc,Daniel Siret,Abdoulaye Gamatie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对边缘计算设施在城市中“落地难”的问题，提出了一套综合社会、环境和物理约束的城市选址评估方法，以促进可持续、公平的分布式数字基础设施建设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的边缘/雾计算方案多关注技术性能（低延迟、节能），但忽视了在城市环境中物理部署的可行性、社会公平性及环境制约因素。

Method: 构建了一套评估城市选址的综合方法论，利用既有城市基础设施和人工表面，结合法律框架、在建城市项目、市民咨询及科学文献，建立了一个基于地点的可行选址类型词汇表，并从能源、空间和定性三个维度进行评估。

Result: 以法国蒙彼利埃市为例，验证了将城市约束与本地资源相结合对于部署太阳能分布式数字基础设施的可行性，并展示了不同部署场景的权衡结果。

Conclusion: 该研究强调了在设计分布式数字基础设施时，采用“本土化（territorialised）”方法的重要性，即必须将物理、社会和环境约束纳入规划，从而实现数字服务的可持续转型。

Abstract: Urban territories face growing tensions between increasing digital demand, limited resources, and socially constrained built environments. Although distributed computing paradigms such as edge and fog computing are widely presented as solutions for reducing latency and energy dissipation, the scientific literature largely overlooks where such infrastructures can be physically and socially deployed within cities, and typically neglects urban constraints, environmental impacts, and equity considerations.
  This paper proposes a methodology for identifying suitable urban locations for deploying distributed servers under structural, environmental, and social limits. Relying exclusively on existing infrastructures and anthropised surfaces, it combines legal frameworks, ongoing urban projects, citizen consultations, and scientific literature to construct a place-based glossary of viable site typologies, evaluated through energy, spatial, and qualitative criteria. Applied to the French city of Montpellier, our results illustrate how urban constraints and local resources shape the feasibility of decentralised, solar-powered digital infrastructures, and highlight the value of territorialised approaches for rethinking digital services within urban limits.

</details>


### [217] [LiveStack: OS Support for Cluster-Scale Full-Stack Live Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18958)
*Yiliang Wan,Haifeng Sun,Yihan Yang,Jonas Kaufmann,Antoine Kaufmann,Jialin Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LiveStack 是一种新型操作系统级分布式全栈仿真框架，通过集成四个特定的仿真控制子系统，解决了集群仿真中保真度与性能难以兼顾的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿真方法无法同时兼顾生产环境软件栈的高保真度（full-stack fidelity）与集群规模仿真所需的性能（simulation performance），难以满足迭代式配置探索的需求。

Method: 构建了一个基于 Linux 虚拟化栈的操作系统级仿真框架，包含四个核心子系统：面向仿真的调度、实时内存层级管理、仿真感知 IPC 以及分布式仿真编排。这些机制共同维护了共享的仿真时间，并有效控制了共存实时主机间的干扰。

Result: 实现了集群规模的全栈仿真，能够在保持未经修改的生产环境软件栈特性的同时，提供足够的性能以支持迭代配置分析。

Conclusion: LiveStack 通过在操作系统层面实现仿真协同，验证了将仿真控制与编排纳入操作系统核心职责的可行性，为集群规模的全栈仿真提供了新范式。

Abstract: Cluster-scale full-stack simulation is essential for evaluating distributed software stacks and emerging hardware components before deployment. Such simulation must achieve both full-stack fidelity for the unmodified production stack and the simulation performance required for iterative configuration exploration. However, no existing method achieves both. We present LiveStack, an OS-level approach to cluster-scale full-stack simulation built on top of the Linux virtualization stack. LiveStack comprises four subsystems: simulation-oriented scheduling, live memory hierarchy management, simulation-aware IPC, and distributed simulation orchestration. Together, they coordinate live and modeled components under shared simulated time while controlling interference among co-located live hosts. These mechanisms point toward simulation-native OS support, where simulation control and orchestration become core OS responsibilities.

</details>


### [218] [A Composable CRDT Layer for Byzantine-Resilient Deterministic Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18966)
*Amos Brocco*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为确定性状态重构的新方法，将更新传播与状态推导解耦，使得CRDT在无需复杂共识的情况下就能实现拜占庭容错并保证强最终一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的CRDT通常假设参与者是诚实的，通过简单的验证或剔除来处理拜占庭行为往往不足或困难。需要一种能在不依赖协议内协调的情况下，实现拜占庭容错的机制。

Method: 提出了确定性状态重构（Deterministic State Reconstruction）模型。在Melda（一种用于JSON文档的非侵入式delta-state CRDT）中实现，通过仅让合法的更新子集参与状态重构，从而屏蔽错误或恶意更新的影响。

Result: 形式化证明了即使在存在欺骗、遗漏或消息乱序的情况下，只要副本基于相同的更新集，就能保证状态收敛。此外，该模型支持在不影响收敛性的前提下层叠身份认证、授权和保密性机制。

Conclusion: 通过将更新传播与状态推导解耦，实现了无需在更新验证阶段介入的拜占庭容错，证明了在对抗性环境下CRDT依然能保证强最终一致性。

Abstract: Conflict-free Replicated Data Types (CRDTs) ensure Strong Eventual Consistency without coordination, but typically assume benign participants and rely on validation or exclusion to handle Byzantine behavior.
  We address this problem through deterministic state reconstruction: rather than deciding which updates are admissible, all accepted updates are incorporated, while only a subset contributes to the reconstructed state. We instantiate this approach in Melda, a non-intrusive delta-state CRDT for JSON documents, and show that its reconstruction model guarantees convergence even under arbitrary update injection: adversarial updates are either structurally rejected or treated as inputs to the reconstruction process.
  We formalize this model and prove that replicas deriving state from the same set of updates cannot diverge despite equivocation, omission, or message reordering. We further show that authentication, authorization, and confidentiality can be layered without affecting convergence.
  Overall, this approach suggests that Byzantine tolerance can be achieved by decoupling update propagation from state derivation, allowing agreement on updates to be handled independently by external dissemination or consensus mechanisms.

</details>


### [219] [On the Notions of Bounded Bypass, and How to Make any Deadlock-Free MUTEX Protocol Satisfy One of Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19003)
*Rob van Glabbeek,Daniele Gorla,Myrthe Spronck*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文旨在解决互斥协议中“有限旁路（bounded bypass）”属性定义缺失且过于严苛的问题，通过形式化定义该属性及其变体，证明了 Bar-David 算法在不同寄存器模型下的有效性，并使用模型检查技术验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限旁路在互斥文献中长期存在，但缺乏严谨的活力属性定义，且现有部分 MUTEX 协议无法完全满足其原定义，因此需要对其进行更精确的分类与扩展。

Method: 1. 形式化定义：确立了修正后的有限旁路定义，并提出了两种弱化版属性。
2. 理论分析：研究并证明了 Bar-David 算法在原子寄存器与安全/常规寄存器环境下的活力提升效果（二次复杂度）。
3. 自动验证：采用逻辑公式描述各活力属性，并通过模型检查（Model Checking）对所有定义与证明进行了验证。

Result: 1. 修正了有限旁路的定义的歧义。
2. 证明了 Bar-David 算法在原子寄存器下可实现有限旁路，在安全/常规寄存器下可实现间歇性有限旁路，复杂度均为进程数的平方级。
3. 通过模型检查确证了所有定义的正确性及算法性能。

Conclusion: 本文通过重新定义有限旁路（bounded bypass）并引入两种中间活力属性（post-doorway 和 intermittent bounded bypass），完善了互斥协议（MUTEX）中活力属性的理论体系。研究证明了 Bar-David 算法能将死锁自由协议提升至满足这些属性，并利用模型检查验证了所有理论结论。

Abstract: In the literature on mutual exclusion, bounded bypass has been used for a long time as a strengthening of starvation-freedom, but, to the best of our knowledge, it still lacks a satisfying definition as a liveness property on its own. Moreover, we have encountered MUTEX protocols for which this notion needs to be slightly weakened in order to be met.
  To solve these issues, we first provide a formal definition of bounded bypass (that also corrects a previous definition from Raynal) and then introduce the notions of post-doorway and intermittent bounded bypass, two liveness properties that lie between starvation-freedom and bounded bypass. Essentially, intermittent bounded bypass weakens bounded bypass by ignoring the possible bypasses that may happen during the execution of a certain finite set of write operations to shared registers. Orthogonally, post-doorway bounded bypass ignores the bypasses that may happen during a finite initial phase of the lock protocol.
  Furthermore, we study an algorithm proposed by Yoah Bar-David in 1998 to enhance the liveness properties of any deadlock-free MUTEX protocol and prove that: (1) in the setting of atomic registers, this algorithm upgrades any deadlock-free mutual exclusion protocol to a bounded bypass one, with a bound that is quadratic in the number of processes; and (2) in the setting of safe and regular registers, the very same algorithm ensures the intermittent version of bounded bypass, still with a quadratic (but slightly different) bound.
  Finally, we provide logical formulae for the different notions of bounded bypass defined in this paper and use them to confirm all claims made here, by using model checking. This had a positive impact on the theoretical development of the work, since it allowed us to identify and correct small mistakes/ambiguities in definitions and proofs.

</details>


### [220] [Spotlight: Synergizing Seed Exploration and Spot GPUs for DiT RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19004)
*Ruiqi Lai,Dakai An,Wei Gao,Ju Huang,Siran Yang,Jiamang Wang,Lin Qu,Dmitrii Ustiugov,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Spotlight 是首个允许在低成本 Spot GPU 上进行 DiT 强化学习后训练的系统，通过创新的探索调度和弹性序列并行技术，在显著缩短训练周期并降低成本的同时，提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型（DiT）的强化学习后训练成本极其高昂，传统方法在降低计算成本（使用 Spot GPU）和维持训练稳定性（序列并行一致性）之间存在矛盾。

Method: 1. 基于“探索容忍模型权重陈旧性”的见解，利用空闲 Spot GPU 进行探索以消除关键路径延迟；2. 弹性序列并行（Elastic SP），通过持久化调度器和节点内权重复制实现亚秒级的集群重构；3. 预留感知（Preemption-aware）的任务调度器，用于处理 Spot GPU 的中断和负载均衡。

Result: Spotlight 在 Qwen-Image 后训练任务中，以 4 倍的速度达到目标验证评分，总成本降低 1.4-6.4 倍，并在多个数据集上实现了更优的图像质量。

Conclusion: Spotlight 通过在异构 GPU 环境下实现高效的强化学习训练，显著降低了扩散模型（DiT）后训练的成本和时间，并同时提升了生成图像的质量。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) is prohibitively expensive, requiring thousands of high-end GPUs. Existing works explore two directions to reduce cost: seed exploration improves training convergence by selecting high-contrast samples, yet adds compute to the critical path; spot GPUs offer 69--77\% lower cost, yet sit idle during training because DiT rollouts finish nearly simultaneously, which prevents LLM-style pipelining of rollout with training. Spot preemptions further break Sequence Parallelism (SP) groups, fragmenting GPU topology.
  We present Spotlight, the first system that harvests spot GPUs for DiT RL post-training. Spotlight rests on two key insights we devise: (1)~we show that exploration can tolerate stale model weights because exploration that uses the model weights from the previous iteration preserves the relative ranking of random seeds, allowing exploration to run on idle spot GPUs during training. (2)~SP reconfiguration can reuse on-node state, reducing group recovery from minutes to sub-second launches. Built on these insights, Spotlight introduces three techniques: a bandit-based exploration planner that maximizes reward variance within the training time budget, elastic sequence parallelism that reconfigures SP groups on the fly via persistent schedulers and intra-node weight copying, and a preemption-aware pull-based request scheduler that balances load and commits in-flight state upon preemption. We implement Spotlight on the open-source RL platform ROLL and evaluate it on Qwen-Image post-training. Spotlight reaches the same target validation score $4\times$ faster than baselines, reducing total cost by $1.4$-$6.4\times$ while achieving superior image quality on DeepSeek-OCR and Geneval datasets with resolution $512\times512$ and $1280\times1280$.

</details>


### [221] [Pulse: Training Acceleration for Large Diffusion Models with Automatic Pipeline Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19163)
*Boran Sun,Guoyong Jiang,Lin Zhang,Chen Chen,Yuechen Tao,Zhishu Che,Jieling Yu,Shan Chang,Huaxi Gu,Fangming Liu,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PULSE 是一种针对扩散模型 UNet 架构设计的自动流水线并行训练策略，通过将存在跳跃连接的层放置在同一设备上（skip locality），消除了高昂的跨设备通信开销，显著提升了在大规模分布训练中的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型（UNet架构）中长距离跳跃连接在传统流水线并行下会导致大量的跨设备 P2P 通信，限制了在 GPU 集群上的扩展能力。

Method: 1. 跳跃连接感知的分区器：利用动态规划在满足配对放置（collocation）约束的前提下平衡异构工作负载；2. ILP（整数线性规划）调度合成器：生成气泡极小的流水线波形调度；3. 混合并行调优器：在内存和网络约束下动态选择并行维度和微批次大小。核心思想是将跳跃连接的编解码层置于同一设备，实现激活值的本地缓存。

Result: 与当前最先进的并行策略相比，PULSE 将通信量减少了 89%，在通信受限的硬件上将训练吞吐量提升至 2.3 倍。

Conclusion: PULSE 通过跳跃连接感知（skip-aware）的放置策略和协同调度优化，从根本上解决了扩散模型在流水线并行训练中的通信瓶颈，显著提升了训练效率。

Abstract: Diffusion models are now a dominant approach for high-fidelity image and video generation, yet scaling their training across GPU clusters remains challenging. Unlike transformer-only architectures, diffusion backbones commonly adopt UNet-style encoder-decoder structures with heterogeneous layers and long-range skip connections. Under conventional pipeline parallelism, these non-local dependencies force large skip activations and their gradients to traverse multiple pipeline boundaries, making peer-to-peer (P2P) communication a dominant bottleneck and substantially reducing pipeline efficiency. In this paper, we present PULSE, an automatic pipeline-parallel training strategy that makes skip locality a first-class optimization objective. PULSE eliminates skip-induced communication by collocating skip-connected encoder-decoder layers on the same device and caching skip activations locally for later use in backpropagation. To realize this placement while maintaining high pipeline utilization, PULSE co-designs: (1) a skip-aware dynamic-programming partitioner that balances heterogeneous stage workloads under symmetric collocation constraints, (2) an ILP-based schedule synthesizer that generates bubble-efficient wave schedules for the resulting stage-to-device mapping, and (3) a hybrid parallelism tuner that selects pipeline/data-parallel degrees and microbatch sizes under memory and network constraints. Our extensive experiments show that the volume of communication can be reduced by 89 percent, and the training throughput can be increased by up to 2.3x on communication-bound hardware, compared with state-of-the-art parallelism strategies.

</details>


### [222] [TurboServe: Serving Streaming Video Generation Efficiently and Economically](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19271)
*Youhe Jiang,Haoxu Wang,Haotong Bao,Kai Jiang,Jianfei Chen,Jun Zhu,Fangcheng Fu,Jintao Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TurboServe 是首个专为流式视频生成设计的 Serving 系统，通过联合在线调度与资源规划，在保障严格延迟目标的同时，显著提升了多租户环境下的 GPU 利用效率和成本效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频生成具有长生命周期、按块生成、延迟敏感的特性，现有 Serving 系统在应对会话持续时间异构性和用户访问需求的时间异构性时，面临布局次优和资源利用率低的挑战。

Method: 提出一种在线调度系统，包含：1. 联合会话放置与GPU资源规划的闭环调度算法；2. 迁移感知放置控制器（实现负载均衡）；3. 负载驱动的自动扩缩容控制器（优化成本）；4. 支持合并分块处理、GPU-CPU卸载（会话挂起/恢复）及基于NCCL的GPU间在线迁移。

Result: 在真实生产环境与大规模GPU集群测试中，TurboServe 将最坏情况下的分块延迟降低了 37.5%，并将总 GPU 运营成本平均降低了 37.2%。

Conclusion: TurboServe 通过在线调度策略有效解决了流式视频生成在多用户、多GPU环境下的延迟和成本问题，显著优于现有基准系统。

Abstract: Streaming video generation is emerging as a new serving workload in which users interact with long-lived sessions that generate video progressively, chunk by chunk. Unlike offline video generation or typical LLM serving, streaming video generation must preserve session state across active and idle periods, repeatedly schedule ongoing sessions, and deliver each chunk under a tight latency target. This creates two key serving challenges in multi-user, multi-GPU environments: session duration heterogeneity, where long-running sessions make placement decisions suboptimal over time, and temporal user-demand heterogeneity, where the number of active sessions fluctuates sharply across bursts and idle periods.
  We present TurboServe, the first serving system designed specifically for streaming video generation workloads. TurboServe formulates serving as an online scheduling problem that jointly coordinates session placement and GPU provisioning. Its closed-loop scheduling algorithm combines a migration-aware placement controller, which rebalances sessions across GPUs to reduce the maximum per-chunk latency, with a load-driven autoscaling controller, which adapts the GPU budget to workload variation for improved cost efficiency. To support these decisions at runtime, TurboServe implements coalesced chunk processing for batching concurrent active sessions on the same GPU, GPU-CPU offloading for session suspension and resumption, and NCCL-based GPU-GPU migration for online rebalancing. We evaluate TurboServe on real-world production traces from Shengshu Technology across multiple model sizes and GPU clusters with up to 64 NVIDIA B300 GPUs. Compared with baseline serving configurations, TurboServe reduces worst-case per-chunk latency by 37.5% and total GPU operating cost by 37.2% on average. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/shengshu-ai/TurboServe.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [223] [RankGraph-2: Lifecycle Co-Design for Billion-Node Graph Learning in Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18379)
*Renzhi Wu,Zikun Cui,Junjie Yang,Tai Guo,Hong Li,Xian Chen,Li Yu,Ke Pan,Sri Reddy,Mahesh Srinivasan,Nipun Mathur,Haomin Yu,Hong Yan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对大规模图检索，Meta 推出了 RankGraph-2 框架，通过将图构建、表示学习和量化索引实时服务进行深度协同设计，在提升召回率和业务指标的同时，大幅降低了实时计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图检索工作通常将图构建、表示学习和实时服务拆分为孤岛进行处理，忽视了它们之间紧密的依赖关系。在十亿节点规模下，这种解耦导致了计算效率低下及性能瓶颈，亟需一种协同设计全链路的框架来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出 RankGraph-2 框架，核心方法包括：1. 引入基于热度偏差校正的图子采样；2. 使用个性化 PageRank (PPR) 预计算多跳邻居；3. 设计协同训练的残差量化聚类索引，将索引训练纳入模型训练目标，从而取消了在线耗时的近邻查询 (KNN)。

Result: RankGraph-2 相比基准模型 (GAT+DGI/PyTorch-BigGraph)，在二分图任务中实现了 2.1 倍至 3.8 倍的召回率提升；Serving 计算代价降低 83%；在 Meta 实际落地中，CTR 和 CVR 分别提升了 +0.96% 和 +2.75%。

Conclusion: RankGraph-2 通过全链路协同设计（工程与算法深度耦合），在超大规模图检索任务中实现了显著的性能提升和工程优化，验证了端到端系统 co-design 在工业级推荐场景中的巨大价值。

Abstract: Graph-based retrieval at billion-node scale requires jointly solving three tightly coupled problems -- graph construction, representation learning, and real-time serving -- yet existing work addresses each in isolation. We present RankGraph-2, a framework deployed at Meta that co-designs all three lifecycle stages for similarity-based retrieval (U2U2I and U2I2I), where each stage's requirements shape the others. Serving requires a co-learned cluster index to avoid expensive online KNN -- this pushes index co-training into the training objective. Training benefits from the observation that similarity-based retrieval tolerates pre-computed neighborhoods, eliminating online graph infrastructure -- this requires construction to produce self-contained data. Construction must also support hour-level refresh for item coverage. Acting on these cascading requirements, RankGraph-2 reduces hundreds of trillions of edges to hundreds of billions via subsampling with popularity bias correction, pre-computes multi-hop neighborhoods via personalized PageRank, and co-learns a residual-quantization cluster index that reduces serving computational cost by 83%. This lifecycle co-design enables a simple architecture to achieve 3.8 x higher recall than a GAT + Deep Graph Infomax model on a bipartite graph and 2.1 x higher than PyTorch-BigGraph on item retrieval. RankGraph-2 delivers up to +0.96% CTR and +2.75% CVR, and has powered 20+ retrieval launches across major surfaces.

</details>


### [224] [SHIFT: Semantic Harmonization via Index-side Feature Transformation for Multilingual Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18801)
*Youngjoon Jang,Seongtae Hong,Hyeonseok Moon,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对多语言密集检索中的语言偏向问题，本文提出了一种名为 SHIFT 的无需训练的索引修正方法，通过抵消语言分布偏移来提高跨语言检索质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多语言密集检索模型存在严重的语言偏向（倾向于检索与查询相同语言的文档），导致跨语言语义相关文档被忽略。

Method: 提出 SHIFT 方法：在索引阶段，利用平行翻译对估计目标语言相对于源语言的“相对语言向量”，通过从文档嵌入中减去该偏移量来校正语言偏向。

Result: 在四个多语言信息检索（MLIR）基准测试及多种模型上验证，证明了 SHIFT 能有效减轻语言偏差并提升检索性能。

Conclusion: SHIFT 是一种通用的、无需训练的方法，能有效缓解多语言密集检索中的语言偏向问题，从而提升跨语言检索性能。

Abstract: With the rapid expansion of massive multilingual corpora, Multilingual Information Retrieval (MLIR) has emerged as a critical technology for global information access. MLIR enables users to retrieve semantically relevant documents from multilingual text collections using a single-language query. However, recent multilingual dense retrieval models often exhibit a strong preference for documents in the same language as the query. This leads to severe language bias, where top-ranked results are dominated by documents of specific languages, even when documents in other languages contain more semantically relevant information. To address this issue, we propose SHIFT, a training-free method applicable in the indexing stage. Specifically, SHIFT utilizes parallel translation pairs to estimate a relative language vector for each target language with respect to a source language. Subsequently, SHIFT corrects the language-specific offset by subtracting this relative language vector from document embeddings during indexing. Our comprehensive evaluation across four MLIR benchmarks and diverse dense retrieval models confirms that SHIFT can effectively mitigate language bias and enhance MLIR performance.

</details>


### [225] [Rescaling MLM-Head for Neural Sparse Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18811)
*Youngjoon Jang,Seongtae Hong,Jonah Turner,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文发现稀疏检索模型SPLADE在更换更强骨干网络时，MLM头部的尺度不匹配会导致训练不稳定。通过引入一种简单的初始化权重缩放技术，成功使现代大模型在稀疏检索任务上达到高效且稳定的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究为什么用更强的预训练模型（替代BERT）作为SPLADE的骨干网络时，模型反而会出现性能退化甚至训练崩溃。

Method: 分析了导致LSR模型训练失效的原因是MLM头部输出的尺度不匹配。提出了一种零成本的初始化修正方法：在SPLADE训练前，将MLM头部的投影权重按常数比例进行缩放，以平衡稀疏激活和对比训练稳定性。

Result: 提出的校正方法使大规模语言模型骨干（如ModernBERT、Ettin）能够稳定训练，并显著提升了稀疏检索性能，在多个基准测试中达到了超越或等同于BERT-SPLADE的效果。

Conclusion: 学习型稀疏检索模型（LSR）的瓶颈不仅在于编码器容量，还在于用于构建稀疏词汇表示的MLM头部权重的尺度校准。通过简单的初始化缩放校正，可以有效解决现代预训练模型（如ModernBERT、Ettin）在LSR训练中的性能退化和不稳定性问题。

Abstract: Learned sparse retrieval (LSR) models such as SPLADE have traditionally used BERT-style masked language models as backbone encoders. A natural expectation is that replacing BERT with stronger pretrained encoders should improve retrieval effectiveness. However, we find that under standard SPLADE training recipes, backbones with large MLM-head L2 norms can suffer performance degradation and even training collapse under standard SPLADE training recipes. We identify this failure as a scale mismatch in the MLM head: SPLADE directly uses MLM-head outputs to construct sparse lexical representations, and query-document relevance is computed by an unnormalized dot product over these representations. As a result, an inflated MLM-head scale can amplify sparse activations, distort matching scores, and destabilize contrastive training under common training settings. To address this issue, we introduce a simple initialization-time correction that rescales the MLM-head projection by a constant factor before SPLADE training. This zero-cost adjustment improves training stability without modifying the model architecture or training objective. Across both in-domain and out-of-domain retrieval benchmarks, this simple correction substantially improves large-norm backbones such as ModernBERT and Ettin, turning unstable training runs into competitive sparse retrievers. In several settings, the corrected models further match or surpass the classic BERT-SPLADE baseline. These findings suggest that the bottleneck in adapting pretrained encoders to LSR is not encoder capacity alone, but the calibration of the MLM-head scale used to construct sparse lexical representations.

</details>


### [226] [LensKit-Auto](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18814)
*Max Breit,Anass Amezian El Idrissi,Rishikesh Giriraj Kulkarni,Luca Quade*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文重点升级并增强了自动化推荐系统框架 LensKit-Auto，通过引入新优化算法、可视化功能和元学习数据支持，简化了推荐算法选择过程，提高了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统中不存在通用的算法，针对特定数据集寻找最优算法与超参数组合具有挑战性，需要一个自动化的系统（AutoRecSys）来降低技术门槛并提高效率。

Method: 1. 将 LensKit-Auto 适配至最新版 LensKit 框架；2. 集成 Tree Parzen Estimator (TPE) 作为新的超参数优化方法；3. 开发模型复用功能与优化过程可视化工具；4. 适配元学习框架以构建元数据集，为未来集成元学习奠定基础。

Result: 实现了升级后的 LensKit-Auto 框架，不仅具备了更强大的搜索和优化能力，还提升了用户体验与系统的可解释性。

Conclusion: 通过更新至最新底层框架并增加优化算法、模型复用与可视化等功能，本研究提升了 LensKit-Auto 的实用性与易用性，使得非专家用户也能高效地为特定数据集选择最优的推荐算法及超参数。

Abstract: Recommender systems have a wide area of application, e.g. in fields like video streaming, social media, or digital marketplaces. But, for a recommender-system, finding the right algorithm with the right hyperparameters is a reoccurring challenge. There is no one-fits-all solution, since the performance of one algorithm can vary immensely on different data sets. Due to the challenges of finding the right algorithm and the broad use of recommender-systems, it is of interest to create an Automated Recommender System (AutoRecSys) that takes on the task of finding the right algorithm-hyperparameter-combination for a given data set. In this work, we present the enhancement of LensKit-Auto, a framework introduced by Vente et al., that solves exactly this task of finding a fitting algorithm-hyperparameter-combination. LensKit-Auto's biggest strength lies in its ease of use, where it operates as a black-box, into which the user can feed their data set and receive the information of which algorithm and hyperparameters work best on this data set. In this work, we bring LensKit-Auto up to date, so that it works with the new version of its underlying framework, LensKit. We also implement further functionalities, such as the Tree Parzen Estimator as an additional optimization method, the ability to reuse the found algorithm, updated documentation, and the ability to visualize the optimization process. We also adapt an existing meta-learning framework to generate a suitable meta-dataset for LensKit-Auto, which could enable the integration of meta-learning into LensKit-Auto in the future. The presented changes bring LensKit-Auto up to date and enhance its usability, so that even non-experts in the field can find the right algorithm for their use case.

</details>


### [227] [SAERec: Constructing Fine-grained Interpretable Intents Priors via Sparse Autoencoders for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18897)
*Jiangnan Xia,Xuansheng Wu,Yu Yang,Xin Wang,Ninghao Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 SAERec 模型，利用稀疏自编码器从文本语料中自动构建细粒度、可解释的意图空间，并通过多分支注意力机制将其融入推荐系统，从而提升推荐的准确性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有意图推荐模型大多直接从用户序列中聚类提取意图，存在对序列质量敏感、需预设意图数量、缺乏语义基础等问题，导致意图集粗糙且限制了推荐性能。

Method: 1. 利用大语言模型（LLM）提取文本嵌入；2. 使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）解耦并提取细粒度、可解释的意图；3. 通过检索机制为用户匹配个人和公共意图作为先验知识；4. 采用多分支注意力机制与自适应融合层，将意图信号注入序列推荐模型。

Result: 在公开数据集上表现优于现有的先进（SOTA）推荐模型，并在提供人类可理解的推荐解释方面表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: SAERec 通过利用 LLM 和稀疏自编码器（SAE）构建细粒度且可解释的意图空间，有效地解决了现有意图推荐模型中意图语义模糊和依赖序列质量的问题，在推荐表现和可解释性上均优于 SOTA 模型。

Abstract: Intent-based recommender systems have gained significant attention for improving accuracy and interpretability by modeling the underlying motivations behind user behaviors. Most existing models derive intents directly from user sequences via clustering or prototype learning. However, they are sensitive to sequence quality, require presetting the number of intents, and lack explicit semantic grounding. These issues lead to an incomplete and coarse intent set and limit the effectiveness of recommendation. In this paper, we propose the Sparse Autoencoder for intent-based recommendation (SAERec), a novel recommender that automatically constructs a fine-grained and interpretable intent space from a textual corpus to guide recommendation. Rather than treating texts as side signals, SAERec leverages them as high information density evidence for intent construction. Specifically, we first extract a comprehensive set of fine-grained interpretable intents from the latent space of large language models (LLMs) by using a sparse autoencoder (SAE) to disentangle and interpret text embeddings, which isolates intent-related semantics from textual noise. Then, for each user, we retrieve relevant intents from this set as priors to guide recommendation. It contains personal intents matching a user's current interests and public intents capturing general item patterns shared across users (e.g., quality, price). Finally, to integrate retrieved intents into sequence modeling, we propose a multi-branch attention mechanism that captures temporal dependencies and injects both personal and public intent signals, followed by an adaptive fusion layer to construct the final user representation for recommendation. Extensive experiments on public datasets demonstrate the superiority of SAERec, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art baselines while providing human-understandable explanations.

</details>


### [228] [Querit-Reranker: Training Compact Multilingual Rerankers via Efficient Label-Free Distribution Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19037)
*Yunfei Zhong,Jun Yang,Wei Huang,Yinqiong Cai,Haosheng Qian,Yixing Fan,Ruqing Zhang,Lixin Su,Daiting Shi,Jiafeng Guo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Querit-Reranker是一个无需大量人工标注的轻量级多语言重排序框架，通过合成查询挖掘和模型合并技术，在保持极高运行效率的同时显著提升了跨语言和跨领域的排序性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言重排序器在应用时面临跨语言/领域泛化挑战，且传统的针对性适配极其依赖昂贵的人工标注，亟需一种高效且标注数据需求少的方法。

Method: 1. 数据驱动流水线：先进行大规模通用相关性建模，再利用合成查询挖掘和教师模型软标签进行目标分布适配；2. 参数合并：采用球面线性插值（SLERP）合并不同任务适配后的检查点，在保持性能的同时避免推理时集成开销。

Result: Querit-Reranker在BEIR（从54.11升至59.28）和MIRACL（从59.87升至67.70）数据集上显著提升了nDCG@10；Querit-Reranker-4B在MTEB多语言重排序任务上达到SOTA水平。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了通过数据驱动的“合成查询挖掘”与模型参数合并策略，可以在无需大规模人工标注的前提下，实现高效且高性能的多语言重排序模型，为轻量化检索系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Deployable multilingual rerankers must generalize across languages, domains, and target ranking tasks while remaining efficient enough for second-stage reranking. However, adapting them to new target distributions typically requires extensive task-specific relevance annotations, which are costly to obtain. We present Querit-Reranker, a family of multilingual cross-encoder rerankers trained with a data-centric pipeline for label-efficient adaptation. We instantiate it as Querit-Reranker-A0.4B, initialized from an in-house MoE backbone with 0.4B activated parameters, and Querit-Reranker-4B, initialized from Qwen3-Embedding-4B. Our pipeline first learns general relevance modeling from large-scale ranking-oriented data, then adapts to target distributions through synthetic-query mining with teacher scores as continuous soft labels. To consolidate complementary task-adapted strengths, we further merge checkpoints via spherical linear interpolation, obtaining a single deployable model without runtime ensembling overhead. Using Qwen3-Embedding-0.6B as the shared first-stage retriever, Querit-Reranker-A0.4B improves average nDCG@10 from 54.11 to 59.28 on BEIR and from 59.87 to 67.70 on MIRACL. On MTEB Multilingual v2 Reranking, it also substantially outperforms larger embedding-based baselines, while Querit-Reranker-4B further achieves state-of-the-art performance among publicly available models. We release both models on Hugging Face.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [229] [CloakLM: Obfuscating GPU Memory Layout to Mitigate Model Ex-filtration for Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18400)
*Kunal Jain,Seokjin Go,Divya Mahajan*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: CloakLM 是一种软件内存混淆框架，无需硬件改动即可通过打乱模型权重存储结构和整形 PCIe 流量，防止在共享算力基础设施上的模型权重被窃取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大模型部署中，由于云设施的租户信任边界外存在被利用的风险（如 PCIe 侦听、HBM 转储等），导致模型参数极易被窃取，现有的防御措施对此类物理层/基础设施层面的攻击覆盖不足。

Method: 提出 CloakLM 框架，包含三种机制：1. PCIe 流量整形（流量模式平滑化）；2. 层间与层内权重洗牌；3. 物理 HBM 页面重映射。该方案在保持逻辑内存视图不变的同时，消除了权重存储的结构规律性。

Result: CloakLM 与 vLLM/PyTorch 集成后，在 LLaMA 和 Qwen 模型上的实验表明，其实现了近乎原生的推理性能，同时显著增强了对 PCIe 侦听和 HBM 转储攻击的抵抗力。

Conclusion: CloakLM 是一个无需硬件修改的软件防御框架，通过内存混淆技术能有效抵御模型权重泄露攻击，且对推理性能影响极小。

Abstract: Large foundation models deployed on third-party and shared accelerator infrastructure face a practical risk of model exfiltration that existing defenses do not fully address. In common serving deployments, model providers control the VM or bare-metal serving stack but not the surrounding hardware substrate. The host to GPU interconnect, accelerator fabric, and neighboring infrastructure components remain outside the tenant's trust boundary and have been shown to be exploitable. Hermes demonstrates lossless DNN reconstruction from passive PCIe observation, while TunnelS exfiltrates HBM contents at high throughput via driver-level access without disrupting inference. Co-tenant VMs can further access memory-mapped interfaces or misconfigured RDMA regions without physical co-location. These attacks exploit a common property of ML systems: model weights are stored in large, contiguous, and repeatedly accessed memory regions, making intercepted PCIe transfers and HBM dumps rich enough to reveal model structure and parameters.
  We present CloakLM, a software-only memory-obfuscation framework that removes this structural regularity without changing the inference stack's logical view of memory. CloakLM combines three mechanisms: PCIe traffic shaping, inter- and intra-layer weight shuffling, and physical HBM page remapping. Authorized execution retains a valid virtual memory layout with negligible overhead, while unauthorized observers see fragmented and semantically incoherent state.
  CloakLM integrates with vLLM and PyTorch, requires no hardware changes, and complements confidential computing. Evaluation on distributed inference workloads using LLaMA and Qwen models shows near-native performance while significantly increasing resistance to PCIe snooping and HBM dump attacks, making inference-time model exfiltration substantially less practical.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [On (Non-)Isomorphism of Self-Dual Lattices and Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18662)
*Huck Bennett,Kyle Fridberg*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了自对偶格的同构问题，通过利用其结构特性（分解与特征向量），改进了 LIP 的处理效率，在算法和复杂度类方面取得了突破，并将结论推广至自对偶码的等价判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受密码学应用的驱动，深入研究格同构问题（LIP）在自对偶格（self-dual lattices）这一特定且重要的领域中的复杂性。

Method: 1. 利用自对偶格的结构分解：$\mathcal{L} \cong \mathcal{L}_0 \oplus \mathbb{Z}^r$；2. 利用特征向量性质：$\langle\mathbf{v}, \mathbf{w}\rangle \equiv \langle\mathbf{v}, \mathbf{v}\rangle \pmod{2}$；3. 结合 Elkies 和 Gaulter 关于具有长最短特征向量格的研究工作。

Result: 1. 提出了一种针对自对偶格 LIP 的 $2^{n/2 + o(n)}$ 时间随机算法；2. 证明了自对偶格上的一类 LIP 问题属于 coNP；3. 对特定类别的自对偶码给出了 PCE 的多项式时间算法。

Conclusion: 通过利用自对偶格的结构特性（分解性质和特征向量），本文改进了求解自对偶格同构问题（LIP）的上限，包括给出了一种 $2^{n/2 + o(n)}$ 时间的随机算法及针对特定类别的 coNP 协议，并将这些结果拓展到了自对偶码的置换码等价性（PCE）问题。

Abstract: A recent line of work motivated by cryptographic applications has studied the complexity of the Lattice Isomorphism Problem (LIP). In this work, we study LIP on self-dual lattices $\cal{L} \subset \mathbb{R}^n$, which appear naturally in many applications. Our main results are a $2^{n/2 + o(n)}$-time randomized algorithm for LIP and a $\mathsf{coNP}$ protocol for LIP on a broad class of self-dual lattices. These results extend recent work on ZLIP, the problem of deciding whether a lattice is isomorphic to $\mathbb{Z}^n$. In particular, the former result extends the $2^{n/2 + o(n)}$-time algorithms for ZLIP of Bennett, Ganju, Peetathawachai, and Stephens-Davidowitz (Eurocrypt, 2023) and of Ducas (Des. Codes Cryptogr., 2024). The latter result extends the $\mathrm{ZLIP} \in \mathsf{coNP}$ result of Hunkenschröder (Math. Prog. Series A, 2024).
  Our results leverage two key structural properties of self-dual lattices $\cal{L} \subset \mathbb{R}^n$: (1) every such lattice $\cal{L}$ is isomorphic to $\cal{L}_0 \oplus \mathbb{Z}^r$ for some self-dual lattice $\cal{L}_0$ with $λ_1(\cal{L}_0)^2 \geq 2$, and (2) every such lattice $\cal{L}$ has \emph{characteristic vectors}, i.e., there exist vectors $\mathbf{w} \in \cal{L}$ such that for every $\mathbf{v} \in \cal{L}$, $\langle\mathbf{v}, \mathbf{w}\rangle \equiv \langle\mathbf{v}, \mathbf{v}\rangle \pmod{2}$. Our results use a line of work by Elkies and Gaulter on lattices with long shortest characteristic vectors, and can be strengthened assuming a positive answer to a related question of Elkies (Math. Res. Lett., 1995).
  We also study Permutation Code Equivalence (PCE) on self-dual codes, and we observe that similar structural properties imply a polynomial-time algorithm for PCE on certain such codes. This gives a natural class of codes with large hull for which PCE is easy.

</details>


### [231] [Fair Online Resource Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18679)
*Christopher En,Yuri Faenza,Andrea Lodi,Gonzalo Muñoz*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个满足Lipschitz公平性约束的在线资源分配模型，通过双对偶镜像下降法算法在确保公平的同时实现亚线性遗憾，并验证了其在复杂实际场景中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在难民重新安置和航空调度等序列到达的资源分配场景中，如何在满足资源容量限制的同时，通过公平性约束（即相似代理获得相似结果）平衡整体福利最大化。

Method: 1. 理论建模：引入Lipschitz公平性约束；2. 离线分析：利用优化理论推导公平代价的下界（$Ω(1/γ)$）；3. 在线算法：采用基于对偶镜像下降法（Dual Mirror Descent）的算法，通过分批处理与对偶变量估计实现资源分配；4. 实证评估：使用“难民经济计划”真实数据进行模型验证。

Result: 1. 在理论上明确了公平代价的界限；2. 证明了提出的在线算法相对于最优线下流体基准具有亚线性遗憾（Sublinear Regret）；3. 实验证明了算法在处理实际分配问题时的有效性及福利与公平之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 研究建立了资源分配中公平性（Lipschitz约束）与整体福利最大化之间的理论权衡，证明了公平分配的价值与最优无约束分配之间存在确定的性能界限，并提供了处理在线分配挑战的有效算法。

Abstract: We study the problem of fair online resource allocation, motivated by applications such as refugee resettlement and airline scheduling, where agents arrive sequentially and must be assigned to facilities with limited capacities. We introduce a model that maximizes the overall welfare subject to resource constraints and a Lipschitz fairness requirement, which ensures that similar agents arriving in the same batch receive similar expected outcomes. We first analyze the offline problem, proving that the value of the optimal fair allocation is at least an $Ω(1/γ)$ fraction of the optimal unfair allocation, where $γ$ is the fairness coefficient, thereby bounding the price of fairness. For the online setting, we propose an algorithm based on dual mirror descent that enforces fairness constraints within batches while estimating optimal dual variables. We prove that this algorithm achieves sublinear regret relative to the optimal offline fluid benchmark. Finally, we validate our theoretical results using real-world data from the Refugee Economies Programme, demonstrating the algorithm's performance and examining the trade-offs between welfare maximization and fairness enforcement.

</details>


### [232] [Tractable Gap-Constraint Languages for Complex Event Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18878)
*Antoine Amarilli,Florin Manea,Tina Ringleb,Markus L. Schmid*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本研究解决了复杂间隔约束下的子序列匹配问题在通用情形下的高效求解难题，通过引入“左凸性”正则语言，在保证计算效率的同时拓展了复杂事件识别的建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂事件识别（CER）领域中，标准的子序列匹配问题在存在复杂间隔约束时通常是NP-完全的，现有的易解情况过于受限，因此需要一种能覆盖更广泛现实场景且具备高效求解能力的理论框架。

Method: 引入了左凸性（left-convexity）正则语言的概念，通过动态规划算法处理具有任意间隔结构约束的子序列匹配问题，并应用这些约束来优化嵌入的寻找与枚举。

Result: 对于满足左凸性的语言，该问题在$O(|D| (|u| + |C|))$时间内是可解的（且在SETH假设下达到最优）；支持高效枚举所有满足约束的嵌入；证明了若约束语言非左凸（如包含$\{aa, \epsilon\}$），则问题恢复为NP-完全。

Conclusion: 该研究定义了“左凸性（left-convexity）”语言类，证明了在此条件下，带有复杂间隔约束的子序列匹配问题可以在$O(|D| (|u| + |C|))$时间内有效求解；同时指出了超出该范围（非左凸）时问题会退化为NP-完全。

Abstract: For strings $u, D \in Σ^*$, a subsequence embedding of $u$ in $D$ is a function $e \colon \{1, 2, \ldots, |u|\} \to \{1, 2, \ldots, |D|\}$ with $e(i) < e(i+1)$ for every $i \in \{1, 2, \ldots, |u|-1\}$ and the $i$-th symbol of $u$ equals the $e(i)$-th symbol of $D$. A gap-constraint for $u$ is a triple $(i, j, L)$ with $1 \leq i < j \leq |u|$ and $L$ is a regular language over $Σ$. An embedding $e$ satisfies a gap-constraint $(i, j, L)$ if the factor of $D$ strictly between positions $e(i)$ and $e(j)$ is a word from $L$. We investigate the subsequence matching problem with gap-constraints, which is relevant in the context of complex event recognition (CER): given $u, D \in Σ^*$ and a set $C$ of gap-constraints, find an embedding of $u$ in $D$ that satisfies all gap-constraints from $C$.
  In general, subsequence matching is NP-complete and the only known tractable variants restrict the interval structure of the gap-constraints. In this work, we show that we can solve subsequence matching with gap-constraints with an arbitrary interval structure rather efficiently (in fact, optimally under SETH) in time $O(|D| (|u| + |C|))$ if the gap-constraint languages satisfy a property which we dub left-convexity: whenever $u v w \in L$ and $v \in L$, then also $uv \in L$. Left-convex languages are sufficiently expressive to model interesting real-world scenarios considered in CER, e.g., length constraints $L = \{w \mid a \leq |w| \leq b\}$ for $a, b \in \mathbb{N}$. We also show how our algorithm can be used in order to efficiently enumerate all satisfying embeddings, which is particularly relevant for possible applications in CER. Finally, we show how non-left-convex languages can lead to intractability, i.e., if in addition to length constraints we allow $\{aa, ε\}$ as the only non-left-convex constraint language, then the problem is NP-complete again.

</details>


### [233] [Compact multi-text index for circular Cartesian tree matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18696)
*Roman Pauli,Eric Osterkamp,Dominik Köppl*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文实现了适用于笛卡尔树匹配（CTM）的ceBWT索引结构，并区分了动态可扩展与静态压缩两种实现方式，旨在解决CTM算法在实际应用中缺乏有效系统实现的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 笛卡尔树匹配（CTM）适用于具有自然结构变异且精确匹配语义意义不强的数据场景，但目前缺乏对其算法实用实现和系统性性能评估的研究。

Method: 实现了两种基于笛卡尔扩展Burrows-Wheeler变换（ceBWT）的索引结构：一种是动态可扩展的版本，另一种是静态压缩的版本。

Result: 提供了一套完整的CeBWT实现框架，支持动态扩展和静态压缩两种模式，填补了CTM领域在工程实现与经验评估方面的空白。

Conclusion: 研发了基于CeBWT的笛卡尔树匹配实用实现，为处理具有结构相似性而非精确匹配的数据提供了有效的索引与检索方案。

Abstract: Cartesian tree matching (CTM) is a structural pattern matching approach that identifies sequences with the same Cartesian tree topology, making it suitable for data with natural variability where exact comparisons carry little semantic meaning. While theoretical algorithms for CTM have been studied extensively, systematic empirical evaluations of practical implementations remain rare. This article presents an implementation of the Cartesian Extended Burrows-Wheeler Transform (ceBWT), a BWT-based index structure for CTM. The implementation supports both a dynamically extendable and a statically compressed index variant.

</details>


### [234] [Learning Augmented Exact Exponential Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18807)
*Tatiana Belova,Yuriy Dementiev,Danil Sagunov*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何将机器学习预测引入NP-难问题的精确指数算法中。通过一种新颖的通用增强方法，在弱化预测要求的前提下，有效缩减了搜索空间并实现了运行时的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现有的“学习增强算法”主要集中在多项式时间算法领域，作者探究机器学习预测是否能突破指数时间算法解决NP-难问题的边界，提升其效率。

Method: 提出了一种通用的增强框架，将机器学习预测整合到现有的精确指数时间算法中；利用预测来剪枝搜索空间，并证明了该框架在仅需预测具有两两独立性或无需预知预测器准确率的更宽松假设下即可工作。

Result: 研究表明，即便预测器的表现仅略好于随机猜测，也能显著减少搜索空间；算法的运行效率提升与预测质量呈正相关，且在比传统文献更弱、更贴近现实的设定下表现稳健。

Conclusion: 机器学习预测可以有效地在NP-难问题（特别是子集选择问题）中加速精确指数算法，即使预测噪声较大且模型不需要预知准确率，也能实现运行时的平滑加速。

Abstract: The field of learning-augmented algorithms has demonstrated that machine-learned predictions can bypass worst-case lower bounds across a wide range of problems. So far, however, the focus has been almost exclusively on polynomial-time algorithms, where predictions improve competitive ratios, approximation guarantees, or running times. In this paper, we raise the question of whether predictions can push the frontier of exact exponential-time algorithms for NP-hard problems. We answer this question affirmatively by proposing a general approach that augments an entire family of state-of-the-art exact algorithms for a variety of subset selection problems. We show that a noisy predictor that is only marginally better than random guessing suffices to provably reduce the search space, and that the resulting runtime speedup scales smoothly with the prediction quality. Importantly, our algorithms require only pairwise independence of predictions or, alternatively, do not require the knowledge of the predictor's accuracy - both strictly weaker and more realistic settings than typically assumed.

</details>


### [235] [Guarded Epoch Bloom Filters for Sliding-Window Membership](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19210)
*Faruk Alpay,Levent Sarioglu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种守卫纪元布隆过滤器，用于处理流数据滑动窗口内的成员查询。该方法通过时间分段与守卫机制，在保证窗口内数据不丢失（无假阴性）的同时，大幅降低了误报率，显著优于传统的计数布隆过滤器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技术（如计数布隆过滤器或稳定布隆过滤器）无法直接高效地满足流数据中“最近窗口语义”下的成员查询需求。现有方法往往在漏报率、复杂性或内存效率方面存在缺陷。

Method: 提出了一种守卫纪元布隆过滤器（GEBF），通过将固定的比特空间划分为若干旋转的“纪元（epoch）”，只在当前纪元插入数据，而在纪元交界处清空旧段，并引入一个“守卫纪元（guard epoch）”来满足确定性的活跃窗口不变性。此外，还提出了阻塞（blocked）变体以提升缓存局部性。

Result: 在 225 种合成流配置和 45 种实际 Web 服务器日志配置的实验中，当每个活跃项分配 14 比特时，该方法将中位数误报率从基准方法（计数布隆过滤器）的 0.191 降低至 0.02225，同时保持了窗口内零假阴性（无漏报）。

Conclusion: 守卫纪元布隆过滤器（Guarded Epoch Bloom Filters）是一种高效的滑动窗口成员查询方案，在保证窗口内无漏报（零假阴性）的前提下，显著降低了误报率，并实现了受控的过期数据保留，适用于允许存在少量 stale positives 的流处理场景。

Abstract: Approximate membership queries in streams often need recent-window semantics rather than membership over all items ever seen. This paper studies guarded epoch Bloom filters, a sliding-window alternative to counting and stable Bloom filters. The structure partitions a fixed bit budget into rotating epochs, inserts only into the current epoch, clears whole segments at epoch boundaries, and keeps one additional guard epoch. This guard yields a deterministic live-window invariant: every item inserted in the last W positions remains represented, while rotation-induced stale retention is bounded by one epoch beyond the target window. We give the construction, prove its live-coverage and bounded-staleness properties, derive a false-positive approximation, and include a blocked variant that improves locality by confining probes to one block per epoch. Experiments cover 225 synthetic streaming configurations and 45 configurations from a timestamp-ordered web-server access-log stream. At 14 bits per live item, the guarded epoch filter reduces median synthetic false positives from 0.191 for a four-bit counting Bloom baseline to 0.02225 while preserving zero measured live-key false negatives. The method is not a replacement for exact deletion; it targets systems where bounded stale positives are acceptable but false negatives inside the live window are not.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [From Embedded Properties to Trait Nodes: A Design Method for Identifying Reusable Metadata in Property Graph Schemas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18297)
*Yahya Sa'd,Renzo Angles,Vojtech Merunka,Roberto Garcia,Karel Klima,Pavel Beranek*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对属性图模式的元数据识别方法，通过一套基于语义属性的准则和决策流程，帮助设计师判断何时应将重复出现的属性转化为可复用的元数据结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 属性图模式中存在重复出现的描述性属性，但目前缺乏明确的工程方法来决策这些属性应采取“嵌入式设计”还是“外部化元数据结构”。

Method: 基于5GNF（五范式）建模视角，提出了一套包含五个准则（跨元素出现、概念独立性、无损外部化、复用潜力、治理相关性）的识别方法，并结合基于规则的决策工作流进行属性分类（特征候选、嵌入属性、边界案例）。

Result: 通过图书馆领域的案例验证与参与者分类任务测试，结果表明单纯的“重复出现”不能作为外部化的充分依据，元数据候选的识别需要更深入的语义解释。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种系统化方法，通过语义评估而非单纯依赖频率，为属性图模式中何时将描述性属性转化为可复用元数据提供了明确的决策依据。

Abstract: Property-graph schemas often contain descriptive properties that recur across heterogeneous nodes and edges, yet schema designers lack a clear method for deciding whether such properties should remain embedded or be treated as reusable metadata structures. This paper addresses this design-stage problem within a 5GNF-oriented modeling perspective by proposing a method for identifying metadata candidates based on five criteria: cross-element occurrence, conceptual independence, lossless externalization, reuse potential, and governance relevance. The method classifies properties into trait candidates, embedded properties, and borderline cases using a rule-based decision workflow. The approach is illustrated using a running example from a library domain and examined through an illustrative validation involving participant-based classification tasks in two schema contexts. The results show that recurrence alone is not a sufficient basis for externalization and that metadata-candidate identification requires semantic interpretation beyond frequency. The main contribution of the paper is methodological: it provides a more explicit and systematic basis for deciding when descriptive properties should be modeled as reusable metadata in property-graph schemas.

</details>


### [237] [Evaluating Learned Spatial Indexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19034)
*Sachith Pai,Jun Yang,Michael Mathioudakis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该文对面向空间区域查询的学习型索引进行了全面的实证研究，分析了影响性能的关键因素，并提出了一套行之有效的索引选择决策树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对面向空间区域查询（Spatial Range Queries）的学习型索引的权衡机制理解不足，且其性能受数据/查询分布倾斜、存取粒度及存储介质影响较大，缺乏系统性的对比评估体系。

Method: 搭建了一个统一的实验框架，通过标准化底层存储和查询执行流程，对代表性的学习型空间索引进行了系统性评测。重点研究了七个关键维度（如块大小、空间分布倾斜、细化与扫描成本、磁盘vs内存表现、构建成本等），并在真实数据集上进行了验证。

Result: 揭示了索引细化（refinement）与扫描（scan）成本之间的权衡规律；明确了块大小、选择性（selectivity）与布局有效性之间的相互作用；结果表明，学习型索引的优势高度依赖于特定的负载特性。

Conclusion: 研究人员总结了学习型空间索引在不同场景下的表现，提出了一个基于工作负载的空间索引选择决策树。实验证明，该决策树能以极小的决策遗憾（decision regret）提供近乎最优的查询性能。

Abstract: Learned indexes improve query performance by adapting search structures to data and workload distributions. Although many learned indexes have been proposed, their trade-offs remain insufficiently understood for spatial range queries, where performance depends not only on model accuracy but also on data and query skew, layout granularity, selectivity, and storage behavior.
  In this work, we perform an experimental study of learned indexes for spatial range queries. We examine a representative set of indexes and address seven fundamental questions: (1) How does block size influence query latency, and what configurations yield optimal performance under varying selectivities? (2) How do skewed data and query distributions impact index performance? (3) How do indexes balance refinement and scan costs, and which designs favor one over the other? (4) How do disk-based storage conditions alter optimal block size and latency trade-offs compared to in-memory settings? (5) What are the construction costs of different indexes, and under what query volumes are these costs amortized? (6) For a given data and query workload, which index is expected to perform best? (7) Do index-selection insights learned from synthetic data generalize to real-world data distributions? To enable the analysis, we use a framework with a common storage backend, standardized query execution pipelines, and controlled variations in data and query skew.
  Our experiments reveal critical insights into refinement vs. scan trade-offs, the impact of block size, and the interplay between selectivity and layout effectiveness. We synthesize these findings into a workload-based decision tree for index selection and validate it on real OpenStreetMap point sets with synthetic queries, confirming that its recommendations exhibit minimal decision regret and typically yield near-optimal query performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [238] [Recover, Discover, Plan: Learning Skills and Concepts from Robot Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18328)
*Bowen Li,Mayank Mishra,Y. Isabel Liu,Stone Tao,Nishanth Kumar,Alexander G. Gray,Ruwan Wickramarachchi,Jonathan Francis,Sebastian Scherer,Tom Silver*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ReSYNC 架构，通过强化学习与关系概念学习的交互，使机器人能从故障中自动提炼抽象知识，实现从被动恢复到主动全局避障的演进，显著提升了机器人解决复杂及未知任务的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法多关注被动式故障恢复，若针对每种故障均训练独立策略则效率极低；此外，机器人缺乏将局部失败经验转化为通用抽象知识以避免未来重复失败的能力。

Method: 提出了一种名为 ReSYNC 的双重学习框架：(1) 技能学习阶段：利用强化学习（RL）学习特定环境下的故障恢复行为；(2) 概念学习阶段：利用增量式方法从恢复经验中发现并细化关系谓词，并更新抽象规划模型，从而将局部恢复逻辑转化为全局避障策略。

Result: 在四个模拟域中实验结果显示，ReSYNC 在解决长程、未见任务时性能优于强基准方法超过 50%；同时成功实现了模拟到真实（sim-to-real）的迁移，展现了在现实世界中进行非抓取操控的能力。

Conclusion: ReSYNC 通过从失败经验中自主发现和优化抽象概念，成功实现了从被动恢复到主动故障规避的跨越，为机器人实现可扩展、具备故障意识的规划提供了新范式。

Abstract: Intelligent robots should not only recover from failures, but also acquire the abstract knowledge needed to avoid them in the future. While reinforcement learning (RL) can learn reactive recovery behaviors, training a separate policy for every distinct failure mode is highly inefficient. We introduce Recovery-Driven Synthesis of Relational Concepts (ReSYNC), the first approach that progressively discovers and refines state abstractions (relational predicates) from failure-recovery experience to support abstract planning. Unlike purely reactive methods, ReSYNC jointly learns skills and concepts through an incremental dual-learning process. In the skill-learning phase, the robot uses RL to learn to recover from failures seen in training tasks. In the concept-learning phase, the robot discovers new relational predicates and refines its abstract planning model to explain and generalize the learned recovery behaviors. This interaction enables ReSYNC to convert local recoveries seen during training into global failure avoidance at test time. Across four simulated domains, we show that ReSYNC's ability to continually expand and refine its abstraction library allows it to solve long-horizon, previously unseen problems, outperforming strong baselines by over 50%. Additionally, we demonstrate sim-to-real transfer of ReSYNC, where it performs real-world non-prehensile manipulation skills and generalizes to unseen scenarios through abstract planning. Overall, ReSYNC represents a significant step toward robots that autonomously acquire abstractions for scalable, failure-aware planning in the physical world.

</details>


### [239] [Guava: An Effective and Universal Harness for Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18363)
*Haowen Liu,Xirui Li,Shaoxiong Yao,Peng Shi,Tianyi Zhou,Jia-Bin Huang,Furong Huang,Jiayuan Mao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Guava 框架，通过系统化设计工作流、动作及观测空间，证明了通过“具身工具使用”能有效提升模型性能，并实现了在 4B 轻量级开源模型中以极少量仿真数据训练出具备高性能表现的具身操作代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然视觉-语言模型具有具身代理潜力，但通过“具身工具使用”来组合高层推理与外部模块仍缺乏明确的设计准则，且尚不清楚这种方式对不同规模模型（尤其是小模型）的适用性。

Method: 开发了一个名为 Guava 的框架，通过系统地探索代理工作流、动作空间和观测空间，明确了有效具身代理的三个关键要素：迭代式感知-推理-行动循环、语义动作抽象以及多模态观测；并利用蒸馏技术将具身能力注入到 4B 参数模型中。

Result: 该方法在仿真和真实世界环境中表现出与前沿闭源模型相当的性能，并展示了对未见目标、新指令和长程任务的强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 完善的“驾驭（harness）”框架可以作为具身操作的可扩展、模型无关的接口，使紧凑的开源模型能够以极少的训练数据展现出强大的具身能力。

Abstract: Language models trained on large-scale vision-language data have demonstrated strong potential for embodied agents. Harnessing models through embodied tools use offers a promising alternative to end-to-end vision-language-action systems by combining high-level reasoning with external modules for perception, planning, and control. However, it remains unclear what makes an effective harness for embodied manipulation, and to what extent such a harness can unlock embodied capabilities in a wide range of reasoning models. In this work, we present Guava, a harness framework for embodied tool use developed through systematic exploration of the design space of agent workflows, action spaces, and observation spaces. Our study identifies three key ingredients for effective embodied agents: iterative perception-reasoning-action loops, semantic action abstractions, and multimodal observations. To understand whether these design principles are universal even to small models, we develop an end-to-end training pipeline that distills embodied manipulation capabilities into a 4B open-source model using fewer than 2K trajectories collected entirely in simulation. Experimental results in both simulation and real-world environments show performance comparable to frontier proprietary models while exhibiting strong generalization to unseen objects, novel instructions, and long-horizon tasks. Results suggest that a well-designed harness can serve as a scalable, model-agnostic interface for embodied manipulation, enabling strong emergent embodied capabilities in compact open-source models with minimal training data.

</details>


### [240] [PAIWorld: A 3D-Consistent World Foundation Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18375)
*Yuhang Huang,Xuan Lv,Junyan Xu,Zhiyuan Yu,Jiazhao Zhang,Ruizhen Hu,Wancheng Feng,Shilong Zou,Hewen Xiao,Ziqiao Zhou,Kaiyun Huang,Zhiyu Peng,Juzhan Xu,Hang Zhao,Chenyang Zhu,Renjiao Yi,Yifei Huang,Douhui Wu,Yan Zhang,Kexu Cheng,Chunhe Song,Yunzhi Xue,Xiuhong Zhang,Leitao Guo,Yunji Chen,Bin Wu,Haibin Yu,Kai Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PAIWorld 是首个通过融合几何感知注意力机制和 3D 先验蒸馏，显式解决机器人多视角世界模型中 3D 一致性缺失问题的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界模型在处理机器人多视角输入时缺乏几何推理能力，导致跨视角对象漂移、深度不一致和纹理错位问题，限制了其在机器人操控任务中的应用。

Method: 提出了 PAIWorld 框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 几何感知跨视图注意力模块 (Geometry-Aware Cross-View Attention)，建立视图间的显式通信通路；(2) 几何旋转位置编码 (Geometric Rotary Position Embedding)，将相机射线方向和外参融入注意力机制；(3) 潜在 3D-REPA (Latent 3D-REPA)，从冻结的 3D 基础模型中蒸馏 3D 语义特征以确保空间一致性。

Result: PAIWorld 在多个机器人操控基准测试中实现了 SOTA 的多视角 3D 一致性表现，在 WorldArena 排行榜排名第一，AgiBot-Challenge2026 排名第二，并有效支持模型预测规划和策略微调等下游任务。

Conclusion: PAIWorld 通过将几何先验与跨视图通信机制集成到扩散变换器（DiT）中，成功解决了多视角机器人操控中的 3D 一致性问题，为构建更具可靠性和通用性的世界模型提供了有效路径。

Abstract: World foundation models (WFMs) are powerful simulators, yet they predominantly operate in a single-view setting and lack the multi-view 3D consistency required for robotic manipulation. While robotic systems rely on multiple cameras (egocentric, eye-to-hand, and wrist-mounted) for policy learning, current multi-view world models simply concatenate view tokens without explicit geometric reasoning. This causes cross-view object drift, depth inconsistency, and texture misalignment. We trace these failures to two deficiencies: the absence of an explicit inter-view communication mechanism and the lack of a 3D geometric prior. We argue that resolving both simultaneously is necessary and sufficient. To address this, we present PAIWorld, a framework that augments diffusion-transformer world models via three core components: (1) Geometry-Aware Cross-View Attention blocks that establish an explicit pathway across views, (2) Geometric Rotary Position Embedding that encodes camera ray directions and extrinsic poses into the attention mechanism, and (3) Latent 3D-REPA, which distills 3D-aware features from frozen 3D foundation models to ensure 3D consistency. Built upon a DiT-based world foundation model, PAIWorld achieves state-of-the-art multi-view 3D consistency on robotic manipulation benchmarks, ranking 1st on the WorldArena leaderboard and 2nd on the AgiBot-Challenge2026 leaderboard, while enabling downstream applications such as model-based planning, world action models, and multi-view policy post-training.

</details>


### [241] [VEGA: Learning Navigation VLAs from In-the-Wild Egocentric Video with Geometric Trajectory Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18426)
*Gershom Seneviratne,Yohan Abeysinghe,Jianyu An,Vaibhav Shende,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VEGA是一种从无标签第一人称视频中学习导航策略的新方法，通过重建场景几何自动生成训练数据，显著提升了机器人的目标导向导航能力及其避障安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网规模的以第一人称视角（egocentric）拍摄的导航视频包含丰富的视觉信息，但缺乏机器人坐标系下的障碍物感知轨迹和明确的导航目标，导致其无法直接用于策略训练。VEGA旨在弥合这一差距，从非标注视频中提取可用的导航训练数据。

Method: 该研究提出了VEGA方法：1. 通过单目视频重建局部场景几何；2. 对导航目标进行采样（文本、图像或空间航点）；3. 基于重建几何生成避障轨迹；4. 使用生成的轨迹分布训练基于流匹配（flow-matching）的VLA导航策略。此外，发布了包含250k场景和500万导航目标的VEGA-Bench基准。

Result: 在VEGA-Bench基准测试中，VEGA相比最强基线将碰撞率降低了33.0%，并将障碍物避让能力提升了17.9%。在真实世界的实验中，成功率提升至少150.0%，碰撞率降低至少66.7%，障碍物避让能力提升至少60.0%。

Conclusion: 通过利用视频导出的几何监督，VEGA方法为训练具备避障能力的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型提供了一种可扩展且高效的方案，证明了仅在训练阶段利用几何信息即可蒸馏出强大的视觉导航策略。

Abstract: We introduce VEGA, an approach for training navigation VisionLanguage-Action (VLA) models from unlabeled egocentric navigation videos. Internet-scale egocentric videos provide a scalable source of navigation-relevant visual observations, capturing cluttered scenes, close-range obstacles, and natural human motion through real-world spaces. However, these videos are not directly usable for policy learning because they do not provide obstacle-aware trajectories conditioned on explicit navigation goals in the robot's coordinate frame. VEGA addresses this gap by reconstructing local scene geometry from monocular video, sampling navigation goals (represented as text, image, or spatial waypoints) and generating obstacle-aware trajectories using the constructed geometry. The resulting trajectory distribution is then used to train a flow-matching VLA navigation policy. By using geometry exclusively during training, VEGA distills obstacle-aware planning directly into a vision-based policy. Furthermore, we introduce VEGA-Bench, a benchmark containing 250k scenes and approximately 5 million navigation goals paired with scene geometry, designed to evaluate goal progress, collision avoidance, and obstacle clearance of VLAs. Our evaluation shows that VEGA achieves competitive goal progress while reducing collisions by 33.0% and improving obstacle clearance by 17.9% over the strongest baseline on VEGABench, while improving success by at least 150.0%, reducing collisions by at least 66.7%, and improving obstacle clearance by at least 60.0% in real-world trials. Ultimately, we demonstrate that video-derived geometric supervision provides a scalable and effective signal for training obstacle-aware navigation VLAs. The code and benchmark will be released at the time of publication.

</details>


### [242] [N(CO)$^2$: Neural Combinatorial Optimization with Chance Constraints to Solve Stochastic Orienteering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18514)
*Anas Saeed,Marcos Abel Zuzuárregui,Stefano Carpin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 N(CO)$^2$ 框架，利用强化学习解决随机定向问题（SOP），无需人工设计启发式算法即可在不确定环境下实现高效、自适应的路径规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经组合优化（NCO）多集中于确定性问题，缺乏对随机组合优化（如 SOP）的有效解决手段，而传统启发式方法在不确定环境下的设计门槛较高。

Method: 提出 N(CO)$^2$ 框架，将强化学习（RL）整合到随机定向问题（SOP）的求解中，通过学习而非手动设计启发式规则来处理路径选择中的机会约束和不确定性。

Result: 模型在不同规模的 SOP 实例上表现出良好的泛化能力，其求解效率和性能表现优于传统的混合整数线性规划（MILP）方法。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了利用强化学习解决随机组合优化问题的可行性，N(CO)$^2$ 模型有效减少了对手动启发式算法的依赖，并在处理不确定环境下的任务时表现出高度的自适应性和竞争力。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) offers a promising alternative to traditional heuristic-based methods for solving complex graph optimization problems by proposing to learn heuristics through data. This class of problems frequently arises in automation, as it can be used to model a variety of applications. While NCO has been extensively studied for deterministic combinatorial optimization problems, there are only a few works that aim to solve stochastic combinatorial optimization problems. In this work, we present N(CO)$^2$: Neural Combinatorial Optimization with Chance cOnstraints to solve the Stochastic Orienteering Problem (SOP) without the use of hand-crafted heuristics. By integrating a reinforcement learning (RL) framework, the model optimizes path selection under uncertainty, effectively balancing exploration and exploitation. Empirical results demonstrate that our method generalizes well across diverse SOP instances, achieving competitive performance compared to the state-of-the-art mixed-integer linear program (MILP) for the task. The proposed approach reduces human effort in heuristic design while enabling adaptive and efficient decision-making in uncertain environments.

</details>


### [243] [Task Allocation and Motion Planning in Dynamic, Cluttered Environments via CBBA and Graphs of Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18516)
*Matthew D. Osburn,Cameron K. Peterson,John L. Salmon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成GCS（时空轨迹规划）与CBBA（分布式任务分配）的方法，解决了多智能体在动态复杂环境下任务分配与避障策略耦合的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂且动态的环境中，多智能体任务规划需要解决任务分配（谁去、何时去、去哪）与安全高效路径规划之间的紧密耦合问题，现有的方法往往难以平衡这两者的复杂依赖关系。

Method: 结合了基于凸集图（GCS）的路径优化算法与基于共识的束算法（CBBA）进行分布式任务分配。通过构建3D+时间配置空间，将分配策略与时空轨迹规划相结合，实现了任务完成时间的准确预测及障碍物避让。

Result: 在包含静态和动态任务的复杂模拟环境中验证了该方法的有效性，展现了其在处理动态目标分配与协同避障方面的能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的协同框架不仅能处理动态分配，还能在复杂、动态的环境中生成安全且高效的时空优化路径，有效结合了任务分配的灵活性与轨迹规划的安全性。

Abstract: Multi-agent task planning in cluttered, dynamic environments requires assigning tasks to agents while simultaneously determining safe, time-efficient trajectories through the environment. When tasks are dynamic, such as rendezvous objectives, allocation decisions depend not only on which agent is best suited for a task, but also on when and where that task can be reached. This paper presents a solution to this problem, which combines Graphs of Convex Sets (GCS) for trajectory optimization with the Consensus-Based Bundle Algorithm (CBBA) for distributed task allocation. In our approach, GCS finds optimal trajectories through dynamic environments using a time-extended (3D+time) configuration space. At the same time, CBBA coordinates task assignments across agents, enabling informed decision-making in a moving environment. We then connect allocation and planning to allow the agents to avoid collisions in the 3D+time configuration space and provide accurate time estimates for task completion. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in simulated cluttered environments with static and dynamic tasks.

</details>


### [244] [As You Wish: Mission Planning with Formal Verification using LLMs in Precision Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18519)
*Marcos Abel Zuzuárregui,Stefano Carpin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为了解决LLM驱动的机器人任务规划中自然语言歧义问题，本文提出了一种引入LTL约束与多模型验证反馈闭环的新架构，显著增强了任务规划的健壮性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统操作复杂且门槛高，现有的基于LLM的自然语言任务规划方案存在语言歧义问题，缺乏有效的任务执行保障机制。

Method: 提出一套包含多反馈闭环的规划架构，利用线性时序逻辑（LTL）对自然语言任务进行形式化约束，并通过两个不同的商用LLM分别执行“规范生成”与“验证”任务以消除偏差。

Result: 通过实验验证了该方法能有效降低语言歧义，确保规划满足用户规范，并分析了当前LLM在生成复杂LTL公式方面的能力上限。

Conclusion: 引入基于LTL验证的反馈闭环架构，显著提升了LLM在精密农业任务规划中的准确性与可靠性，证明了多模型验证机制在降低语言歧义和偏差方面的有效性。

Abstract: Though robotic systems are now being commercialized and deployed in various industries, many of these systems are highly specialized and often require an advanced skill set to operate and ensure they perform as instructed. To mitigate this problem, we recently introduced a mission planner leveraging LLMs to synthesize mission plans in precision agriculture based on mission descriptions provided in natural language. While the system demonstrates impressive performance, it also suffers from the inherent ambiguities of natural language. In this paper, we extend our system to address this issue by introducing multiple feedback loops in the planning architecture that leverage linear temporal logic (LTL) to ensure the mission planning system meets the specifications formulated by the user while still using natural language. To mitigate potential bias, this is achieved by using two different commercial LLMs in charge of the specification and verification subtasks. Through extensive experiments, we highlight the strengths and limitations of integrating mission verification into a fully autonomous pipeline, particularly regarding an LLM's ability to generate valuable LTL formulas, and show how our proposed implementation addresses and solves these challenges.

</details>


### [245] [DREAM-Chunk: Reactive Action Chunking with Latent World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18589)
*Wenxi Chen,Kaidi Zhang,Chi Lin,Zhiyuan Zhang,Yu She,Yuejiang Liu,Raymond A. Yeh,Shaoshuai Mou,Yan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DREAM-Chunk 为机器人动作分块模型引入了测试时潜空间世界模型，通过对未来进行“模拟预测与择优”，显著增强了机器人面对随机环境时的鲁棒性与执行准确性，无需额外微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动作分块（Action Chunking）方法虽然提升了推理与执行的频次匹配，但在面临环境随机性、硬件误差及部分观测时，开放循环（open-loop）执行往往表现脆弱。

Method: 提出了一种无需微调的测试时扩展方法（Test-time scaling method），通过轻量级潜空间世界模型对 VLA 模型采样生成的多个候选中子序列进行推演，基于未来状态预测结果选择最优执行路径。

Result: 在 Kinetix 基准测试及四个真实机器人操作任务中表现优异，通过增加候选样本数量，有效提升了模型在噪声环境下的鲁棒性和对复杂行为轨迹的执行质量。

Conclusion: DREAM-Chunk 通过引入测试时潜空间世界模型，有效解决了动作分块（Action Chunking）策略在随机环境下的鲁棒性问题，证明了利用测试时计算（Test-time computation）可显著提升机器人执行任务的反应能力与抗干扰性能。

Abstract: Action chunking has become a common interface for vision-language-action (VLA) models, enabling low-frequency policy inference to drive high-frequency robot execution. However, once an action chunk is committed, its open-loop execution can be brittle under stochastic dynamics, hardware execution errors, and partial observability. We propose DREAM-Chunk, a test-time scaling method that augments chunking-based policies with a lightweight latent world model, without requiring additional policy fine-tuning. At test time, DREAM-Chunk samples multiple candidate action chunks, rolls out their predicted latent futures, and selects actions from the chunk whose predicted state best matches the observed rollout. In this way, DREAM-Chunk uses additional test-time computation to cover multiple plausible stochastic futures and improve reactivity during long-horizon chunk execution. On the Kinetix benchmark, DREAM-Chunk improves robustness under increasing action noise and benefits from larger candidate sample sizes, especially when demonstrations contain corrective behaviors. We further validate DREAM-Chunk on four manipulation tasks across two robot platforms and two VLA policies under various sources of stochasticity. Across simulation and hardware experiments, DREAM-Chunk improves the robustness of action-chunking policies in stochastic dynamics.

</details>


### [246] [Benchmarking Action Spaces in Reinforcement Learning for Vision-based Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18594)
*Seyed Alireza Azimi,Homayoon Farrahi,Abhishek Naik,Colin Bellinger,A. Rupam Mahmood*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过对比四种动作空间在机器人操控任务中的Sim-to-Real表现，发现关节速度空间能显著提升运动平滑度和任务成功率，并为RL任务的动作空间设计提供了实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际的强化学习应用中，动作空间的选择直接影响机械臂的运动平滑性、安全性及任务执行表现，但目前缺乏针对不同动作空间对Sim-to-Real影响的系统性对比研究。

Method: 在真实的机械臂操控任务（物体抓取与推移）中，对比评估了四种动作空间（位姿增量、位姿速度、关节位置增量、关节速度）；通过仿真训练并将策略部署至真实物理环境进行Sim-to-Real迁移测试。

Result: 实验结果显示，关节速度（Joint Velocity）动作空间在任务性能和运动平滑性上均优于其他三种候选空间，且该结论在视觉驱动的抓取与推移任务中具有一致性。

Conclusion: 动作空间的选择对强化学习（RL）的仿真到现实（Sim-to-Real）迁移性能有显著影响。研究证实，对于基于视觉的机械臂抓取和推移任务，关节速度（Joint Velocity）控制空间在运动平滑度和任务成功率方面表现最优，并为机器人学习实践者提供了选型依据。

Abstract: In real-world reinforcement learning (RL), the choice of action space can play a key role in shaping motion smoothness, safety, and overall task performance. In this study, we evaluate pose increment, pose velocity, joint position increment, and joint velocity across two vision-based manipulation tasks: object picking and pushing. We train policies in simulation and deploy them to the real world using sim-to-real transfer. We find that action-space representation indeed significantly affects sim-to-real performance. In particular, we find that the joint velocity action space is best for the vision-based picking and pushing tasks in terms of smoothness and final task performance. We also provide practical guidance for RL practitioners in choosing action spaces for both simulation and real-world experiments.

</details>


### [247] [Admittance-Based Surface Alignment for Human-in-the-Loop Robotic Visual Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18601)
*Antara Banerjee,Colin Acton,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于导纳控制的闭环机器人方位控制方法，能够通过实时结合操作员输入和视觉感知反馈，在复杂工业环境中实现高精度的表面检测与顺应性运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在航空航天、半导体等高端制造领域，现有的离线运动规划难以应对工业视觉检测中实时的人机协作（遥操作或共享自主）以及感知噪声、表面不规则性带来的挑战，迫切需要一种具备响应式和顺应行为的控制架构。

Method: 提出了一种基于导纳（Admittance-based）的实时闭环控制流水线。其核心设计是将末端执行器建模为一个在粘性介质中运动的“虚拟球体”，通过质量-阻尼器系统将感知到的方位误差与操作员的控制输入相结合，从而生成同步的、顺应性的机器人运动。

Result: 在6自由度机械臂上进行了实验验证，实现了稳定的表面法向追踪，最终平均方位误差达到了0.4°。

Conclusion: 该文提出的基于导纳控制的实时闭环方位控制框架，能够有效统一操作员指令与视觉感知，在处理工业视觉检测中存在的人机交互需求和感知不确定性方面表现出高稳定性和精度。

Abstract: Precision visual inspection underpins quality assurance across aerospace, semiconductor, and medical manufacturing, where undetected surface anomalies on high-value parts translate directly into scrap, rework, and field failures. Robotic visual inspection requires precise alignment between the end-effector and local surface geometry in the presence of perception noise and surface irregularities. In industrial settings, a human operator is often kept in the loop via teleoperation or shared autonomy, introducing real-time adjustments that render purely offline motion planning inadequate. This motivates control architectures capable of reactive, compliant behavior under combined human and perceptual uncertainty. This paper presents a novel real-time, closed-loop robotic orientation control pipeline for precision visual inspection, with an admittance-based framework that unifies operator input and perception-driven surface alignment. We design the end-effector as a virtual sphere moving through a viscous medium, such that the resulting physically interpretable mass--damper system generates synchronized, compliant motion from orientation error and operator commands. We validate the framework on a 6-DOF manipulator demonstrating stable normal-tracking and a final mean orientation error of 0.4°.

</details>


### [248] [SC3-Eval: Evaluating Robot Foundation Models via Self-Consistent Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18610)
*Wei-Cheng Tseng,Gashon Hussein,Yuzhu Dong,Allen Z. Ren,Lucy X. Shi,XuDong Wang,Sergey Levine,Zhaoshuo Li,Jinwei Gu,Florian Shkurti,Ming-Yu Liu,Quan Vuong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人策略评估难题，SC3-Eval通过结合前向-逆向动力学控制、多视角修复和测试时一致性反馈，将视频基础模型转化为高精度的机器人策略行为评估工具，实现了无需真实环境的可靠模拟评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的机器人策略评估昂贵且难以扩展；现有的基于视频世界模型的评估方法存在误差累积（Autoregressive rollouts）、多视角不一致以及泛化能力不足的问题。

Method: 提出SC3-Eval，通过三种互补的机制改进预训练视频基础模型：1. 前向-逆向动力学一致性（通过预测与逆向动作恢复强化物理合理性）；2. 多视角一致性（通过视角间重绘/修复保持时空连贯性）；3. 测试时一致性（利用逆向模型作为不确定性度量，动态终止偏离轨迹的推理）。

Result: 在七个现实世界视觉-语言-动作策略上，SC3-Eval取得了0.929的闭环Pearson相关系数和0.119的MMRV，显著优于现有基准，并具备良好的任务泛化能力，能有效捕捉策略在真实世界中的失败模式。

Conclusion: SC3-Eval通过三种一致性机制有效弥补了视频世界模型在评估机器人策略时的长程误差累积和多视角不一致问题，实现了高度准确的闭环评估，能够精确复现真实世界的失败模式，提供比单纯聚合指标更有价值的诊断信息。

Abstract: Evaluating generalist robot manipulation policies in the real world is expensive, slow, and difficult to scale. Action-conditioned video world models offer a scalable alternative by simulating policy rollouts. Autoregressive rollouts accumulate compounding errors, observations across multiple camera views must remain mutually consistent, and the evaluator must generalize to policies whose behaviors lie outside the training distribution. We address these challenges with SC3-Eval, a self-consistent video generation recipe that adapts a pre-trained video foundation model into an accurate policy evaluator by enforcing three complementary forms of consistency. First, forward-inverse dynamics consistency jointly trains the model to predict frames from actions and to recover actions from frames, anchoring generated rollouts to a physically plausible action manifold and counteracting the drift a forward-only model cannot penalize. Second, cross-view consistency trains the model to inpaint each camera view from the other, keeping the multi-camera observation coherent over long rollouts without any explicit memory mechanism. Third, test-time consistency reuses the inverse dynamics mode at inference as a per-action-chunk uncertainty signal that terminates rollouts whose generated frames drift away from the requested actions. We also demonstrate SC3-Eval rollouts reproduce the failure modes that policies exhibit in real-world rollouts, supporting fine-grained diagnostic comparison rather than aggregate ranking alone. Across seven real-world vision-language-action policies, SC3-Eval attains a closed-loop Pearson correlation of $0.929$ and MMRV of $0.119$, outperforming three strong prior video-model-based baselines, and generalizes to new tasks.

</details>


### [249] [SRL: Combining SLIP Model and Reinforcement Learning for Agile Robotic Jumping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18625)
*Xiaowen Hu,Linqi Ye,Yudi Zhu,Chenyue Shao,Rankun Li,Qingdu Li,Yan Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种融合物理模型 (SLIP) 与强化学习 (RL) 的 SRL 框架，通过引入物理前馈控制显著提升了机器人跳跃的训练效率、稳定性和对复杂地形的适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有跳跃足式机器人难题：SLIP 模型虽具生物启发性但难以应对复杂不规则地形；纯 RL 方法虽适应性强但样本效率低（需大量探索），旨在结合两者优点。

Method: 提出 Spring-loaded Reinforcement Learning (SRL) 框架：将 SLIP 模型提供的物理前馈控制信号与强化学习（RL）的实时反馈控制相结合，实现对跳跃动作的持续优化。

Result: SRL 显著减少了训练时间；在跳跃任务中实现位置跟踪误差低于 0.1m，速度跟踪误差在 ±3% 以内；并在双足/四足、平地/楼梯等场景中通过了 sim-to-sim 和 sim-to-real 验证。

Conclusion: SRL 框架通过结合 SLIP 的物理先验与 RL 的自适应能力，有效解决了复杂地形下机器人跳跃的稳定性与训练效率问题，展现出强大的 sim-to-real 迁移潜力和实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Robotic jumping is pivotal in applications such as search and rescue and logistics, where crossing obstacles and enhancing mobility efficiency are critical. The Spring-Loaded Inverted Pendulum (SLIP) model leverages simplified spring-mass dynamics that naturally encode biologically plausible hopping motions, yet its performance degrades on irregular terrain due to idealized assumptions regarding contact and joint dynamics. Meanwhile, Reinforcement Learning (RL) can adapt to diverse and complex environments but often requires extensive data from unguided exploration. The complementary strengths of SLIP's physically grounded baseline and RL's adaptive capabilities motivate a hybrid framework that overcomes these individual limitations. We therefore propose Spring-loaded Reinforcement Learning (SRL), which integrates SLIP-based feedforward control signals with RL-driven real-time feedback, enabling continuous optimization of robotic jumping. Experimental results demonstrate that SRL can achieve more stable jumps with much less training time than the baseline method, maintaining an average position tracking error below 0.1 m and velocity tracking errors within +/-3% of the target values. Through bipedal and quadrupedal simulations of ground and stair jumping, as well as sim-to-sim and sim-to-real validations, SRL exhibits robust adaptability to various task requirements and environmental complexities, underscoring its potential for real-world deployment.

</details>


### [250] [Self-Supervised Mask-Aware Transformers for Fault-Tolerant FBG Force Sensing in Minimally Invasive Surgical Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18628)
*Peibo Sun,Shiyuan Dong,Shucheng Ye,Jianrong Cai,Yushan Liu,Hongen Liao,Tianqi Huang,Fang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对微创手术机器人中 FBG 传感器力估计易受噪声和故障影响的问题，提出了一种统一的掩码感知 Transformer，实现了在通道失效情况下的高精度稳健力估计，且无需复杂的多模型校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对微创手术中 FBG 传感器面临的非线性跨轴耦合及纤维故障导致的通道丢失问题，现有基于组合模型库的方法不仅扩展性差（随通道数呈指数级增长），且需要昂贵的逐模式校准。

Method: 1. 采用自监督掩码感知 Transformer 架构，通过掩码通道重建进行预训练；2. 使用平衡的“清晰-损坏”视图目标和动态损坏课程学习进行微调；3. 引入并行的异方差高斯负对数似然不确定性头，以单次前向传递预测每轴置信度。

Result: 在 8 通道 FBG 数据集上，该模型在正常情况下 RMSE 为 0.0066 N，在 4 通道严重故障下仍能保持 0.0126 N 的误差，性能优于包含 255 个神经网络的模型库（0.0154 N），且无需模式特定校准。

Conclusion: 提出了一种统一的、自监督的掩码感知 Transformer 模型，能够在不进行模式特定校准的情况下，解决微创手术机器人中多通道 FBG 传感器因耦合和通道失效导致的力估计难题，实现了稳健且高精度的实时推断。

Abstract: In minimally invasive surgical robotics, catheter-scale Fiber Bragg Grating (FBG) sensors are promising due to their ability to estimate multi-dimensional forces by multiplexing several optical channels. However, deploying these compact multi-channel sensors introduces two critical engineering challenges: inherent nonlinear cross-axis coupling during complex deformations, and intermittent channel dropouts caused by fiber fractures in constrained workspaces. These compounding issues severely degrade force estimation. Existing fault-tolerant approaches rely on combinatorial model banks, which scale exponentially with the channel count and demand prohibitively expensive per-pattern calibration. In this paper, we propose a unified, self-supervised mask-aware Transformer that explicitly models channel availability to enable graceful degradation under diverse and dynamic sensor failures. The encoder is pretrained via masked-channel reconstruction on unlabeled data streams and fine-tuned for force regression using a balanced clean-and-corrupted-view objective alongside a dynamic corruption curriculum. Furthermore, a parallel uncertainty head, trained via heteroscedastic Gaussian negative log-likelihood, predicts per-axis confidence in a single forward pass, circumventing the overhead of multi-pass ensembles. Evaluated on a catheter-scale 8-channel FBG dataset, our single unified model achieves a nominal Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) of 0.0066~N and degrades gracefully to 0.0126~N under severe 4-channel failures. This significantly outperforms a comprehensive model bank of 255 per-pattern neural networks (0.0154~N at 4-channel loss) while eliminating pattern-specific calibration.

</details>
