### [251] [DNN Koopman-Based Deviation Compensation for UGV Path Tracking Control on Coupled Slope and Potholed Road](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18630)
*Jian Zhao,Wenbo Zhou,Zhicheng Chen,Bing Zhu,Jiayi Han,Dongjian Song,Yinju Lin,Peixing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对越野环境下UGV路径跟踪的复合控制策略，通过结合自适应参数估计、LMPC和深度学习Koopman算子补偿，在保证系统稳定性和计算效率的同时，显著提升了复杂地形下的跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人地面车辆（UGV）在非结构化越野路面行驶时，复杂的坡度与坑洼干扰严重影响路径跟踪性能，传统的控制方法难以兼顾计算效率与鲁棒性。

Method: 1. 采用带解耦误差项的自适应遗忘递归最小二乘法估计轮胎侧偏刚度；2. 设计基于Laguerre函数模型预测控制（LMPC）以降低计算负载；3. 提出基于深度神经网络Koopman算子（DK）的偏差补偿方法处理坑洼路面干扰；4. 建立事件触发并行协作（EPC）机制实现LMPC与DK的耦合控制。

Result: 硬件在环（HiL）实验表明，该策略在多种工况下的路径跟踪性能提升了超过11.5%。

Conclusion: 通过结合LMPC与DK算子补偿机制，该策略在复杂越野干扰下显著提升了无人地面车辆（UGV）的路径跟踪精度与稳定性，验证了所提方法在多工况下的有效性。

Abstract: Unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs) operating in off-road scenarios are confronted with complex terrain disturbances that can substantially degrade path tracking performance. To address this challenge, this paper proposes a deep neural network (DNN) Koopman-based deviation compensation strategy for UGV path tracking control. Firstly, based on the vehicle dynamic function on coupled slope, an adaptive forgetting recursive least squares method with decoupled error terms is designed to estimate tire cornering stiffness. On this basis, a Laguerre model predictive control (LMPC) path tracking control strategy is designed by incorporating Laguerre functions, which can reduce computational resource usage while maintaining reliable tracking performance across different coupled slope scenarios. Then, by integrating Koopman operator theory with DNN, a DNN Koopman (DK) path deviation compensation method is proposed, which significantly improves the path tracking accuracy of UGV under potholed road disturbances. Furthermore, an event-triggered parallel cooperative (EPC) compensation mechanism that couples LMPC with DK is established based on compensation activation criteria and credibility verification. This mechanism improves path tracking accuracy on potholed road while ensuring the feasibility of overall steering command and stability of vehicle after DK compensation. Finally, a hardware-in-the-loop (HiL) experimental platform is constructed for validation. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed UGV path tracking strategy improves tracking performance by more than 11.5% across multiple operating conditions.

</details>


### [252] [ROBOSHACKLES: A Safety Dataset for Human-Injury Prevention in Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18632)
*Zhuowen Yin,Chongyang Liu,Wenzhang Yang,Renjue Li,Yinxing Xue*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对具身基础模型（EFMs）缺乏防伤害对齐的问题，本文提出了ROBOSHACKLES流水线，通过合成视频数据构建了首个大规模机器人危险动作数据集，揭示了当前主流模型在安全关键场景下100%的失效现状，并为后续研究提供了评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身基础模型（EFMs）的安全对齐研究不足，且由于伦理和安全原因无法收集真实的机器人伤害人类数据，难以评估和提升模型在极端情况下的避险能力。

Method: 提出了一种安全关键数据构建流水线：基于DROID真实观测数据，通过场景理解、风险感知图像编辑、时序提示词生成以及利用Wan2.7模型进行单次路径合成，构建了包含10,000个机器人危险动作视频片段的数据集（ROBOSHACKLES）。

Result: 构建了覆盖6种伤害类别（2种直接伤害、4种间接伤害）的ROBOSHACKLES数据集。对6种主流EFMs的评估显示，模型在测试场景下产生不安全动作的比例高达100%。

Conclusion: 现有的具身基础模型（EFMs）在面对安全关键场景时普遍缺乏安全性，研究表明即使在实验室环境下所有模型均会产生不安全操作。ROBOSHACKLES数据集为评估、防御及训练模型防范伤害提供了有效的基准和资源。

Abstract: Embodied Foundation Models (EFMs) integrate multimodal understanding, future-state reasoning, and executable robot actions. Yet their safety alignment for human-injury prevention remains underexplored, primarily because real-world data of robots harming humans or creating hazardous household situations cannot be safely or ethically collected. To address this challenge, we propose a safety-critical data construction pipeline for human-injury prevention in EFMs.Starting from real DROID observations, our construction pipeline proceeds through scene understanding, hazard-aware image editing, temporal prompt generation, and single-pass rollout synthesis. The temporal prompts specify the expected scene evolution, while Wan2.7 synthesizes realistic robotic rollouts from the edited hazardous states in a single pass. Using this pipeline, we construct ROBOSHACKLES, a 10,000-clip robotic video dataset derived from real DROID observations, spanning two direct-harm and four indirect-harm categories. To ensure dataset quality, we assess task completion and visual quality with automatic metrics, and evaluate six representative EFMs under a refusal-based safety criterion. Results show that all evaluated models produce unsafe actions in the tested safety-critical scenarios, yielding a 100% unsafe action generation rate. ROBOSHACKLES serves as a scalable benchmark and training resource for refusal learning and hazard anticipation before robot action execution.The dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/YZW00/RoboShackles.

</details>


### [253] [EffiNav: Fusing Depth and Vision-Language for Efficient Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18634)
*Zecheng Yin,Benedict Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 EffiNav 框架，旨在解决未知环境物体导航（ObjNav）任务中效率低下和路径冗余的问题。实验证明，该方法在仿真基准和真实机器人应用中均展现出卓越的导航效率、泛化能力和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知环境中进行物体目标导航（ObjNav）时，现有基于训练的模型存在泛化性差的问题，而非训练框架则往往效率低下，导致智能体出现过度探索已知区域或来回往复的冗余运动。因此，如何实现高效导航是当前研究的核心挑战。

Method: 提出了名为 EffiNav 的导航框架，旨在解决 ObjNav 任务中智能体探索效率低下、路径冗余及重复探索的问题。该方法通过失败分析进行改进，并表现出良好的可扩展性，可适配至记忆增强式导航任务。

Result: 在 HM3D 和 OVON 仿真基准测试中，EffiNav 的 SR 和 SPL 指标优于或持平于当前主流方法；通过在真实环境下的机器人实验及 GOAT-BENCH 数据集上的扩展，验证了其在复杂场景下极高的鲁棒性与适应性。

Conclusion: EffiNav 在多种标准数据集（HM3D, OVON, GOAT-BENCH）上表现出优异的性能，不仅在成功率（SR）和路径加权成功率（SPL）指标上匹配或超越了现有基准，还表现出更好的平衡性、泛化能力及在真实环境下的部署潜力。

Abstract: To locate a target object while exploring the unknown environment is a fundamental capability for autonomous agents, with applications ranging from search-and-rescue to field robots. A simplified version of such task is Object Goal Navigation (ObjNav). In ObjNav, successful arrival at the target object provides a basic measure of performance; however, the efficiency of the navigation trajectory is equally important, as it indicates how intelligently the agent explores and how much time remains for subsequent tasks. In unknown environments, the key to efficient navigation lies in deciding where to explore next. While many prior works aim to address this core challenge and achieved promising performance in certain settings, recent training-based models and non-training frameworks still suffer from generalization and efficiency issues respectively, which in the worst cases can lead to excessive exploration of already-visited areas or redundant back-and-forth motion. We evaluate EffiNav on two widely used simulation benchmarks Habitat Matterport 3D (HM3D) and Open-Vocabulary Object goal Navigation (OVON), and further validate its effectiveness on physical robots in real-world settings. We conduct failure analysis on massive simulation episodes. With minimal modification, we also extend EffiNav to a memory-augmented ObjNav task on the GOAT-BENCH dataset, demonstrating its adaptability beyond standard ObjNav settings. Across two standard metrics--Success Rate (SR) and Success weighted by Path Length (SPL), EffiNav matches or outperforms recent baselines, reflecting its efficiency, robustness, and practical applicability. Recognizing the different emphases of the two datasets, the performances reveals this framework is more balanced and generalizable for efficient ObjNav.

</details>


### [254] [A Scalable Embodied Intelligence Platform for Seamless Real-to-Sim-to-Real Transfer of Household Mobile Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18646)
*Kui Yang,Xianlei Long,Haoxuan Li,Yan Ding,Chao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BestMan是一个旨在解决移动操作在模拟与现实间无缝迁移的平台，通过自动化场景生成、模拟引导的技能学习以及统一的硬件中间件，简化了家庭服务机器人的策略开发与部署流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作在非结构化家庭环境中面临部署挑战，现有的真实到模拟再到真实的转换过程中存在场景重建昂贵、评估复杂以及跨硬件部署兼容性差等问题。

Method: 提出了自动化场景生成（ASG）模块用于高保真模拟重构；构建了一套模拟引导的任务形式化与技能学习架构，支持大规模混合技能评估；开发了硬件无关的统一中间件（HUM），实现了跨异构硬件的无缝部署。

Result: 实验结果表明，BestMan平台能够高效实现从模拟到现实的迁移，在处理大规模策略评估和跨异构平台部署上具有卓越性能。

Conclusion: 开发了BestMan平台，有效解决了移动操作在模拟与现实环境间的转化难题，为家庭场景下的移动操作研究提供了标准化的基准与高效的开发环境。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation is a fundamental capability in embodied intelligence robotics. The growing demand for robust and generalizable manipulation in unstructured household environments has driven rapid progress in embodied intelligence platforms. However, achieving a seamless transfer across the real-to-sim-to-real cycle faces three key challenges, including costly high-fidelity simulation scenes reconstruction, the complexity of systematic strategy evaluation in simulation, and incompatible real-world deployments. To address these challenges, we develop BestMan, a scalable and seamless real-to-sim-to-real platform that bridges the gap between the simulation and the real world, enabling effective strategy development, integration, and deployment for household mobile manipulation. Specifically, we design a novel Automated Scene Generation (ASG) module to reconstruct realistic simulations from real observations. Then, we propose a simulation-guided task formalization and skill learning architecture that supports the flexible integration and large-scale evaluations of hybrid skill strategies in simulation. Finally, to enhance the real-world scalability, we develop a Hardware-agnostic and Unified Middleware (HUM) to ensure seamless and compatible sim-to-real transfer across heterogeneous mobile manipulators for real deployments. Experimental results demonstrate the superior performance of our proposed platform in establishing standardized benchmarks and facilitating promising research in the field of mobile manipulation.

</details>


### [255] [High-Degree-of-Freedom Lightweight Bioinspired Leg for Enhanced Mobility in Small Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18680)
*Haoqi Han,Yifei Yu,Jiaming Zhang,Xinru Cui,Linxi Feng,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型四自由度微型并行腿部机构，通过同心设计与并行架构实现了低惯性、大工作空间及高灵活性的运动性能，适用于微型生物启发机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严格的空间限制下，如何提升微型机器人腿部机构的自由度以增强运动能力是当前面临的主要挑战。

Method: 提出了一种基于两个球形五杆机构的四自由度并行腿部机构，采用同心设计策略简化运动学分析，并将所有驱动器集成于主体以降低末端惯性。

Result: 该机构总质量为18.9g，末端输出力约为0.5N，工作空间超过22255 mm³，且通过实验验证了其优越的运动灵活性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功设计并验证了一种高自由度、低惯性的微型并行腿部机构，证明了其在微型生物启发机器人领域具有出色的运动灵活性和应用潜力。

Abstract: In microrobotics, enhancing locomotion capabilities by increasing the degrees of freedom (DoF) of leg mechanisms under severe spatial constraints remains a significant challenge. Inspired by insect locomotion, this paper presents a novel micro-scale parallel leg mechanism with four degrees of freedom, and systematically analyzes its mechanical design, electrical system, and kinematics. The design incorporates two spherical five-bar linkages to achieve spatial motion within a parallel four-bar configuration. Furthermore, a concentric design strategy is employed to simplify the analytical solution of the leg kinematics. Due to the parallel system architecture, all actuators are located on the main body, substantially reducing the equivalent inertia of moving parts compared to traditional high-DOF leg structures. The total mass of the system is only 18.9 g, with an end-effector output force of approximately 0.5 N and a workspace exceeding 22255 mm3. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed single-leg mechanism achieves excellent motion flexibility, highlighting its potential for micro bio-inspired robotics.

</details>


### [256] [Leveraging Energy Features for Surface Classification with Deep Learning: A Comparative Analysis Across Three Independent Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18698)
*Alexander Belyaev,Oleg Kushnarev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了移动机器人表面分类中能量特征的有效性，证明其作为独立特征表现稳健，且能稳定增强惯性传感器的数据表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于能量的方法在受限环境中表现出潜力，但在移动机器人表面分类领域仍缺乏深入研究。本文旨在探讨能量派生特征作为独立输入或惯性传感器补充数据的有效性。

Method: 在三个公开数据集上，评估了包括RNN、CNN、Transformer和Mamba模型在内的深度学习架构，并采用了自动化超参数调优和输入序列长度优化策略，对比了纯能量特征分类与“能量+惯性”联合分类的性能。

Result: 模型在所有数据集上均优于过往报告的性能；CNN表现最优；纯能量特征分类准确率为85-90%，惯性数据组合准确率为96-99%，能量特征的加入使整体准确率稳定提升1-2%。

Conclusion: 基于能量的特征在移动机器人表面分类中表现出良好的可行性。作为独立模态，该特征可达到85-90%的准确率；与惯性数据结合时，可提升1-2%的整体性能，证明了其作为潜在感知补充手段的价值。

Abstract: The energy-based method remains a comparatively underexamined approach for surface classification in mobile robotics, despite promising results in constrained environments. This study evaluated the viability of using energy-derived features as either a standalone classification modality or as supplementary input to inertial data. A comprehensive evaluation was conducted across three publicly available datasets, comparing the performance of modern deep learning architectures including recurrent neural networks, convolutional neural networks, encoder-only transformers, and Mamba state-space models, under automated hyperparameter tuning and input sequence length optimization. The models achieved higher accuracy than previously reported values on all evaluated datasets, with the convolutional neural network yielding the highest overall performance. When relying exclusively on energy-based features, the models attained classification accuracies in the range of 85-90%, approximately 5-10% lower than those achieved when combined with inertial features (96-99%). Augmenting inertial data with energy features resulted in a consistent mean accuracy improvement of 1-2%. These findings indicate that classifiers relying solely on energy features offer sufficient accuracy for standalone deployment, while also providing a consistent gain when used in combination with other sensing modalities.

</details>


### [257] [Selective Unit-Cell Actuation in Lattice Structures for Distributed Morphology in Soft Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18704)
*Trevor Exley,Altair Coutinho,Lucia Beccai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将气动执行器嵌于晶格单元胞内的单体结构设计，实现了高度可控的分布式驱动，支持通过空间驱动模式产生多种变形模式及复杂运动，推动了柔性机器人从结构到功能的一体化设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的柔性晶格机器人通常是在被动结构中外加执行器，难以实现精细化的分布式控制与多态变形。该研究旨在将“执行器-晶格”协同设计下沉至单元胞尺度，实现通过空间驱动模式而非均匀增压来控制整体形态。

Method: 提出了一种嵌入式气动单元胞，将弯曲支柱晶格几何结构与双向波纹管执行器集成在单个单体元件中；通过对这些单元胞进行平铺（Tessellation）和空间选择性驱动，实现复杂的整体变形。

Result: 通过1x1到3x3的平铺实验验证了位移与力的可扩展性；在3x3x3阵列中实现了弯曲、定向抓取等多种变形模式；通过主动与被动单元胞的异构组合，验证了基于非对称变形的爬行运动能力。

Conclusion: 在单元胞尺度上集成执行器与晶格结构是实现柔性机器人分布式变形与多功能运动的有效策略，为可扩展、单体化的机器人架构奠定了基础。

Abstract: Soft lattice structures are increasingly used in robotics to tailor compliance and guide deformation; however, actuation is typically introduced at the device or module level, with actuators inserted into otherwise passive architectures. In this work, we move actuator-lattice co-design to the unit-cell scale. We present an embedded pneumatic unit cell that integrates curved-strut lattice geometry with a bidirectional bellow actuator within a single monolithic element. When tessellated, the lattice functions as a distributed actuation field in which global morphology is governed by spatial actuation patterns rather than uniform pressurization. Experimental characterization of 1x1, 2x2, and 3x3 tessellations demonstrates scalable displacement and force generation with repeatable cyclic performance. Selective actuation of unit cells in a 3x3x3 array produces distinct global deformation modes, including bending and directional grasping, without altering hardware configuration. Additionally, coupling active and passive unit cells enables bending-driven crawling locomotion, demonstrating that heterogeneous tessellations can translate through asymmetric deformation. These results establish unit-cell-level actuation as a strategy for distributed morphing in lattice-based soft robots and provide a foundation for scalable, monolithic robotic architectures.

</details>


### [258] [Two-Phase Bilevel Search for the Moving-Target Traveling Salesman Problem with Moving Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18730)
*Allen George Philip,Anoop Bhat,Sivakumar Rathinam,Howie Choset*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了包含移动障碍物的移动目标旅行商问题 (MT-TSP-MO)，提出了 MICP 模型和 TPBS 启发式算法，有效解决了在动态环境中平衡目标访问时效性与避障任务的难题，性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的移动目标旅行商问题 (MT-TSP) 未考虑动态避障。本文旨在通过引入移动障碍物限制（MT-TSP-MO），建立更符合动态环境需求的轨迹优化模型及其求解算法。

Method: 1. 提出了用于精确求解的混合整数锥规划 (MICP) 公式；2. 设计了一种高效、可扩展的两阶段双层搜索 (TPBS) 算法以获取高质量的可行解。

Result: 在新提出的数据集（最多包含40个目标和40个障碍物）上的测试结果表明，所提算法在成功率、解的质量和计算效率上均优于现有的基准方法。

Conclusion: 提出的混合整数锥规划 (MICP) 和两阶段双层搜索 (TPBS) 算法在处理带移动障碍物的移动目标旅行商问题 (MT-TSP-MO) 时，在成功率、求解成本及计算耗时方面均显著优于现有基准算法，证明了该方法的有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: The Moving-Target Traveling Salesman Problem (MT-TSP) seeks a minimum cost trajectory for an agent that departs from a static depot, visits a set of moving targets, each within one of their assigned time windows, and returns to the depot. In this article, we study the Moving-Target Traveling Salesman Problem with Moving Obstacles (MT-TSP-MO), a generalization of the MT-TSP where the agent trajectory must avoid moving obstacles. We present a Mixed-Integer Conic Programming (MICP) formulation that can be solved using off-the-shelf solvers, as well as a fast and scalable Two-Phase Bilevel Search (TPBS) algorithm that computes high-quality feasible solutions for the problem. We evaluate our approaches against an existing baseline algorithm on a broad range of problem instances with up to 40 targets and 40 obstacles. The results demonstrate that both the proposed methods significantly outperform the baseline with respect to success rates, solution costs, and computation time.

</details>


### [259] [Generating Natural and Expressive Robot Gestures through Iterative Reinforcement Learning with Human Feedback using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18747)
*Chris Lee,Flora Salim,Benjamin Tag,Francisco Cruz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种结合大型语言模型（LLM）与强化学习（RLHF）的方法，旨在提升人形机器人（Pepper）配合语音的手势自然度与表现力，解决了传统方法生成动作僵硬的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人手势生成依赖人工预设，缺乏灵活性；现有的机器学习方法难以在高自由度下实现自然的交互。作者旨在利用LLM实现动态手势合成，并通过优化手段克服其在初期应用中动作僵硬、不自然的问题。

Method: 1. 利用ChatGPT根据对话内容动态生成机器人手势代码； 2. 引入基于人类反馈的增强学习（RLHF），通过迭代式用户研究收集反馈，对手势生成模型进行微调，以优化动作的自然度、相关性和流畅度。

Result: 实验结果表明，经过RLHF微调后的模型，能够使Pepper机器人生成比基础LLM模型更具表现力、更符合语境且动作更流畅的手势，人机交互效果得到显著提升。

Conclusion: 将大语言模型（LLM）与强化学习（RLHF）相结合，是提升类人机器人生成自然、流畅且具表现力配合语音手势的有效途径，能够显著改善人机交互体验。

Abstract: Expressive gestures are essential for natural and effective communication, complementing speech when verbal cues alone are insufficient (e.g., pointing). For social robots such as the humanoid Pepper, producing natural and expressive movements is critical for improving human-robot interaction (HRI) and long-term acceptance. However, generating gestures remains challenging due to reliance on expert-authored animations, resulting in rigid behaviors that are impractical for dynamic and diverse environments. Alternatively, machine learning approaches often struggle to capture perceived naturalness, becoming increasingly challenging with more degrees of freedom. Consequently, producing expressive robot gestures requires a system that can adapt to the environment while adhering to social norms and physical constraints. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) enable dynamic code generation, offering new opportunities for runtime gesture synthesis from natural language. In this paper, we integrate ChatGPT into the humanoid robot Pepper to generate co-speech gestures aligned with conversational output. While this baseline enables flexible gesture generation, the resulting motions are often perceived as stiff and unnatural. To address this limitation, we introduce an iterative reinforcement learning with human feedback (RLHF) system that finetunes gesture generation based on user evaluations, leveraging an iterative user study to compare Pepper's generated gestures. Our results show that RLHF improved the LLM's co-speech generative capabilities, producing more expressive, relevant and fluid movements.

</details>


### [260] [HALOMI: Learning Humanoid Loco-Manipulation with Active Perception from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18772)
*Zehui Zhao,Yuxuan Zhao,Gaojing Zhang,Chenxi Liu,Maolin Zheng,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HALOMI 是一个利用人类演示学习人形机器人 loco-manipulation 的框架，通过引入感知流形约束控制器和视角对齐策略，成功解决了跨形态迁移中的精度和鲁棒性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接将人类演示转移至人形机器人存在局限，主要表现为：基于世界坐标系的跟踪控制器在遇到 OOD 目标时不够鲁棒，且人类与人形机器人之间存在本质的观测和动作执行差距，难以直接迁移。

Method: 1. 扩展了通用操作接口（UMI），结合自我中心（egocentric）感知收集数据；2. 设计了一种流形约束控制器，在学习到的潜在行为流形中进行规划；3. 通过自我视角对齐和控制器感知的参考轨迹自适应技术，缓解观测空间和动作执行层面的差距。

Result: 在 Unitree G1 人形机器人上进行了 5 项任务验证。在 3 项定量评估任务中，HALOMI 的平均成功率达到 85%，并展现出执行动态抛掷和深蹲抓取等复杂动作的能力。

Conclusion: HALOMI 提供了一种通过人类演示学习人形机器人 loco-manipulation 的可扩展框架，有效克服了传统控制器在域外（OOD）环境下的脆弱性以及人类与机器人之间的感官和执行偏差，为人形机器人具身智能的学习提供了稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Human demonstrations, which can be collected at scale and naturally capture active hand-eye coordination, are a promising data source for learning humanoid loco-manipulation. However, directly transferring human demonstrations to humanoids requires a precise world-frame tracking controller, which is often brittle under Out-of-Distribution(OOD) targets, while human-to-humanoid gaps persist in both egocentric observation and action execution. To address these challenges, we present HALOMI, a scalable framework for learning humanoid loco-manipulation with active perception from human demonstrations. HALOMI extends Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) with egocentric sensing to collect ego-view and wrist-view observations along with head-hand trajectories at scale. We further propose a manifold-constrained controller that plans in a learned latent behavior manifold to enable precise and robust head-hand tracking in the world frame. To bridge the human-to-humanoid gap, we perform ego-view alignment and introduce a controller-aware reference trajectory adaptation to reduce mismatch in both observation and action execution. We validate HALOMI on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot with an actuated neck across five real-world tasks involving navigation, grasping, bimanual manipulation, whole-body coordination, and dynamic behaviors. Across the three quantitatively evaluated tasks, HALOMI achieves an average success rate of 85\%, while additional qualitative demonstrations show its ability to support dynamic tossing and deep-squat grasping.

</details>


### [261] [Space Is Intelligence: Neural Semigroup Superposition for Riemannian Metric Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18828)
*Chenghao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种摆脱传统显式规划器的运动规划新方法：将智能嵌入空间本身，通过学习场景的黎曼度量场，使代理通过跟踪测地线实现高效、鲁棒且具有极强零样本泛化能力的运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将智能高度依赖于代理（Agent）的策略学习或搜索算法，这往往会导致较高的计算复杂度和泛化瓶颈。作者试图通过“环境即智能”的范式，将运动规划问题转化为空间几何属性。

Method: 提出了一种将观察场景转化为配置空间（Configuration Manifold）黎曼度量的框架。使用“编码器-路由”（Encoder-Router）架构，通过帧参数（frame）、调制参数（modulation）和基础系数（basic coefficients）三种参数组，利用半群叠加机制（semigroup-superposition）生成度量场，代理任务简化为在该度量下的测地线跟踪（geodesic following）。

Result: 该模型在仅经由两个障碍物的场景训练后，即展现出对未见障碍物配置的强大零样本（zero-shot）泛化能力，且在无碰撞路径与碰撞路径的代价函数之间实现了数量级差异的显著区分。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将计算智能置于空间几何而非代理策略中的可行性。通过学习场景誘導的黎曼度量，模型实现了无需显式路径规划的运动规划，并具备极强的泛化能力。

Abstract: Traditional approaches place intelligence in the agent, whether as a learned policy or a search procedure. We instead place intelligence in the space itself: a scene induces a Riemannian metric on the configuration manifold, and action reduces to following the geodesics of that metric rather than invoking a separate planner or collision checker. A single Encoder-Router network realizes this idea through three complementary parameter groups -- frame parameters that orient the generators, modulation parameters that govern their spatial propagation, and basic coefficients that determine their strength. These groups combine through a shared semigroup-superposition mechanism to produce a single Riemannian metric field, yielding a compact architecture whose geometry scales naturally with scene complexity. Trained on a single two-obstacle scene, the model demonstrates robust zero-shot generalization across unseen obstacle configurations, with orders-of-magnitude separation between collision-free and obstacle-penetrating path costs.

</details>


### [262] [ZiMPedance: Impedance-Aware ZMP Modeling and Control for Payload Carrying with Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18883)
*Giovanni B. Dessy,Lorenzo Amatucci,Victor Barasuol,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将被动载荷接口动力学整合进MPC框架的方法，通过数学建模抑制了四足机器人负载运输过程中的振荡，显著提升了系统的稳定性、效率及末端跟踪能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人使用被动弹簧臂进行负载运输时，系统由于刚度-阻尼特性会产生振荡力，导致运动稳定性下降，现有的控制方法未能有效解决这种被动接口与机器人运动之间的耦合问题。

Method: 1. 推导了包含无源载荷接口动力学（刚度、阻尼、载荷质量）的扩展零力矩点（ZMP）公式；2. 将无源子系统动力学集成到单刚体模型（SRBD）中；3. 基于该增强模型构建了模型预测控制（MPC）框架。

Result: 仿真结果显示，该控制器的稳定性违例率从 7.0% 降低至 0.7%（降低 10 倍），水平地面反作用力效率提升 15%；硬件实验证明了在受干扰情况下该方案的稳定性优于基准控制器，且具备无源末端轨迹跟踪能力。

Conclusion: 通过将无源载荷接口的动态特性纳入MPC框架，能够有效抑制运动引起的谐振，显著提升四足机器人在负载运输任务中的稳定性及效率，并证明了无需额外执行器即可实现末端执行器轨迹跟踪的可行性。

Abstract: Load transportation with quadruped robots is strongly affected by the dynamics of the physical interface between the robot and the load. Passive spring-based arms reduce weight and complexity compared to active manipulators, but their spring-damper dynamics can introduce oscillatory forces that degrade locomotion stability. This paper derives an extended Zero Moment Point (ZMP) formulation that includes passive payload-interface dynamics, relating stiffness, damping, and payload mass to the stability margin. The analysis shows that underdamped configurations can resonate with locomotion harmonics. Based on this insight, we augment a Single Rigid Body Dynamics model with passive subsystem dynamics and integrate it into a Model Predictive Control framework. In simulation, the proposed controller reduces stability violations by up to $10\times$, from $7.0\%$ to $0.7\%$, and increase locomotion efficiency by lowering horizontal ground reaction force effort by up to $15\%$ compared to a nominal baseline. Hardware experiments with a $2\,\mathrm{kg}$ payload show stable locomotion under pull-release disturbances where the nominal controller fails. The same model also enables end-effector tracking through passive arm dynamics without direct arm actuation.

</details>


### [263] [C-ARC: Continuous-Adaptive Range Clustering for Non-Repetitive LiDAR Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18948)
*Nick B. Schroeder,Jonathan Lichtenfeld,Oskar von Stryk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对非重复性扫描 LiDAR 无法应用传统扫描线聚类算法的问题，本文提出了 C-ARC 框架，通过自适应分辨率网格和双图结构，实现了在非结构化点云数据上的实时、稳健聚类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚类方法多针对重复性扫描 LiDAR 设计（依赖扫描线索引和固定帧边界）。随着非重复性 LiDAR（如基于 Risley 棱镜的 Livox 系列）的普及，其非均匀采样和无旋转周期的特性导致传统算法失效，亟需一种新的聚类框架来适配这些传感器。

Method: 开发了名为 C-ARC 的持续自适应范围聚类框架。核心设计包括：1. 维护跨滑动窗口的持久双图结构，实现点插入与聚类提取的解耦；2. 引入基于指数控制环的自适应范围网格分辨率机制，无需预设扫描模式即可在稀疏性与碰撞风险间取得平衡。

Result: 在 Livox Mid-360 上实现了 20Hz 的实时处理性能； adaptive resolution 机制验证了对现有网格聚类方法的改进效果；分析出非重复高密度扫描模式下，无界单元占用是该框架的主要局限。

Conclusion: C-ARC 是首个专门针对非重复性扫描 LiDAR 设计的实时聚类框架，通过自适应网格和双图结构，在低算力下实现了稳健且实时的点云分割，弥补了传统基于扫描线方法在非重复性传感器上的缺陷。

Abstract: Real-time LiDAR clustering identifies structures in point clouds, which is an essential prerequisite for many mobile robotics algorithms. Current methods are mostly developed for repetitive mechanical LiDAR sensors. Recently, the use of non-repetitive LiDAR sensors is strongly increasing due to their small cost and form factor. Such non-repetitive Risley prism-based sensors violate two key assumptions of repetitive mechanical sensors: structured scan lines and well-defined frame boundaries. Their Rhodonea-curve trajectories produce non-uniform point distributions, and the absence of a rotation cycle renders conventional scan line indexing inapplicable. To meet such new requirements, we developed C-ARC, a Continuous-Adaptive Range Clustering framework that maintains a persistent dual-graph over a sliding window, decoupling high-frequency point insertion from on-demand cluster retrieval. This is crucial for key functionalities like SLAM or tracking. An adaptive range grid resolution mechanism calibrates grid dimensions at initialization using an exponential control loop, balancing the sparsity-collision trade-off without prior knowledge of the scanning pattern. Implemented as an open-sourced single-threaded C++17 library, C-ARC produces real-time cluster output at 20 Hz on commodity hardware for the Livox Mid-360. Evaluation on the Livox Avia identifies unbounded cell occupancy as the primary limitation for sensors with strongly concentrated scan patterns. The adaptive resolution mechanism additionally improves clustering quality for existing grid-based methods on non-repetitive data.

</details>


### [264] [A High-accuracy Event-based Underwater SLAM System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18951)
*Yifan Peng,Qihang,Liu,Haoying Li,Yuzhe Li,Junfeng Wu,Ziyang Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对水下复杂环境，提出了首个高精度事件驱动立体SLAM系统，通过结构感知TS生成策略和优化三角测量机制，显著提升了在复杂纹理和运动扰动下的追踪表现，并发布了首个水下事件数据集UWE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于事件相机的TS方法在水下复杂动态场景（速度波动、重复纹理、宽基线）中表现极差，导致系统频繁失效。

Method: 1. 设计了基于结构张量一致性和梯度的结构感知度量（Structure-aware metric）。
2. 构建了两阶段TS（Time Surface）生成策略：利用贝叶斯优化（BO）进行初始化前的先验预测，并在追踪阶段采用异步在线局部搜索。
3. 引入了基于先验视差的数据关联和“最新观测优先”的三角测量机制。

Result: 在公开数据集和自建的UWE数据集上进行了广泛评估，结果表明该系统相较于现有的先进事件驱动SLAM方法具有更高的精度和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出了首个高精度事件驱动的水下立体SLAM系统，通过结构感知度量和两阶段TS生成策略有效解决了现有方法在复杂水下环境中的鲁棒性问题。同时贡献了首个高质量水下事件数据集UWE，推动了领域发展。

Abstract: While event cameras offer immense potential for underwater SLAM, existing Time Surface (TS)-based methods prove highly unreliable when deployed underwater. Fluctuating camera velocities severely degrade TS imaging quality, while wide stereo baselines and repetitive underwater textures induce critical matching failures, frequently triggering system failure. To overcome these challenges, we develop the first high-accuracy event-based underwater stereo SLAM system. A structure-aware metric for TS is designed based on structure tensor coherence and gradients to quantitatively evaluate TS structural information density. By decoupling the optimal TS generation into two distinct stages based on system initialization, Bayesian Optimization(BO) first predicts an optimal prior TS sequentially before initialization while we set an asynchronous online local searching method periodically to obtain appropriate TS in real-time during the tracking stage. We use the prior disparity to guarantee precise data association and "latest-observation-first'' triangulation mechanism to realize stable triangulation. As a benchmark for these solutions and a resource for the community, we also contribute UWE, the first high-quality real-world underwater event dataset containing variable camera motions, complex textures and different trajectory features. Extensive evaluations on public datasets and UWE show the competitive accuracy performance of the proposed SLAM system compared to the state-of-the-art event-based method. The code and data will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [265] [Object-Centric Residual RL for Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real VLA Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18953)
*Kinam Kim,Namiko Saito,Heecheol Kim,Katsushi Ikeuchi,Jaegul Choo,Yasuyuki Matsushita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以物体为中心的残差强化学习框架，通过在模拟环境中使用姿态信息训练残差策略，解决了VLA模型在真实物理操作中因累积误差导致的鲁棒性不足问题，实现了高效的零样本迁移与自进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于模仿学习的VLA模型在处理精细物理交互时由于累积误差表现脆弱。现有的强化学习微调方法（如特权信息蒸馏、端到端视觉RL）在模拟到现实（sim-to-real）的迁移中存在域适应、部署成本或安全性问题。

Method: 提出一种以物体为中心的残差强化学习（Residual RL）框架。核心包括：1.利用物体姿态（pose）作为紧凑观测空间以消除视觉域差异；2.在模拟环境重建相同的遥操作演示以对齐域空间；3.在模拟中通过姿态噪声注入和Dropout训练残差策略，实现零样本（zero-shot）部署。

Result: 在Franka Research 3机器人上进行的5项操作任务中，该方法将VLA的零样本成功率从42%提升至76%，并利用优化后的轨迹实现了VLA的自提升。

Conclusion: 通过基于物体姿态的残差强化学习框架，无需额外真实世界训练，即可有效提升VLA模型在复杂物理交互中的鲁棒性，且能产生高质量数据实现VLA的自进化。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can generalize across diverse manipulation tasks, but their imitation-learning-based policies remain brittle in precise physical interactions due to compounding execution errors; Can a reinforcement learning policy trained purely in simulation improve the robustness of real-world VLAs zero-shot? Residual RL, which learns a corrective policy on top of a frozen VLA, offers a natural framework, but existing approaches face a fundamental sim-to-real dilemma: privileged-state methods require lossy distillation for deployment; image-based methods suffer from the visual domain gap; and real-world RL is costly and unsafe. We propose an object-centric residual RL framework that refines VLA actions using object poses, enabling a compact observation space that transfers consistently between simulation and reality. To align the two domains, we additionally replay the same teleoperation demonstrations in simulation to train a sim counterpart of the real-world VLA. The residual RL policy is trained only in simulation with pose noise injection and dropout, and transfers zero-shot to the real robot. Across five manipulation tasks on a real Franka Research 3 (FR3) robot, our method improves the success rate from 42% to 76% zero-shot, and the improved rollouts can be further reused to retrain the base VLA for self-improvement without additional teleoperation. Project page: https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/research/articles/object-centric-residual-rl/

</details>


### [266] [TactSpace: Learning a Physics-enriched Shared Latent Space for Tactile Sim-to-Real Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18959)
*Arunim Joarder,Arjun Bhardwaj,René Zurbrügg,Mayank Mittal,Florin Püntener,Sira Bielefeldt,Cosmin Roman,Vaishakh Patil,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人触觉仿真保真度不足问题，本文提出了一种多模态表征学习框架，通过对比学习将异构触觉数据映射至共享空间，实现了高效的零样本仿真到现实迁移，并开源了基于Isaac Lab的高性能仿真实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前仿真器缺乏对复杂触觉形变和传导机制的高保真建模，限制了机器人触觉任务从仿真到现实（sim-to-real）的迁移效果。

Method: 提出了一种多模态表征学习框架，通过特定模态编码器将异构触觉数据（如仿真穿透深度与真实电容数据）映射至共享潜空间；采用对比学习、自重构与交叉重构目标进行训练，并通过Isaac Lab实现高效的惩罚式触觉仿真。

Result: 实现了零样本仿真到真实的迁移；在下游任务中，相比单一仿真，多物理场仿真提升了表征信息量，使力预测误差降低了16.7%，形状重构误差降低了45.8%。

Conclusion: 通过构建跨模态共享潜空间，该方法成功实现了触觉感知的零样本仿真到真实的迁移，证明了通过多物理场仿真增强学习特征的有效性，并为机器人触觉研究提供了高效的仿真工具。

Abstract: Tactile sensing provides direct measurements of contact interactions that are essential for robotic manipulation. However, current simulators lack the fidelity to faithfully model the complex deformation and transduction mechanics of tactile sensors, severely hindering sim-to-real transfer in robot learning pipelines. To address this challenge, we propose a multi-modal representation learning framework that aligns heterogeneous tactile modalities within a shared latent space, eliminating the need for accurate raw-signal simulation while preserving relevant contact information. Our approach employs modality-specific encoders to project diverse tactile observations, such as simulated penetration depth and real-world capacitance, into a common embedding space. The model is trained using self- and cross-reconstruction objectives alongside contrastive alignment, encouraging modality-invariant yet information-rich representations. We evaluate the learned embeddings on indenter shape identification, force prediction, and geometric reconstruction tasks, training exclusively in simulation and testing directly on real sensor measurements. Our results demonstrate zero-shot sim-to-real transfer across physically dissimilar representations. Furthermore, incorporating multi-physics simulation modalities yields more informative embeddings that transfer across diverse downstream tasks, demonstrating a 16.7% reduction in force prediction error and a 45.8% reduction in shape reconstruction error. Finally, we release an efficient Warp-based implementation of a penalty-based tactile simulation model for Isaac Lab, enabling scalable tactile data generation.

</details>


### [267] [Congestion-Aware Robot Tour Planning in Crowded Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19031)
*Stefano Bernagozzi,Charlie Street,Masoumeh Mansouri,Lorenzo Natale*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种考虑人群拥挤状况的概率巡游规划算法，通过预测人群动力学（CLiFF模型）并在线优化MDP路径，显著提升了机器人复杂动态环境下的任务执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在拥挤环境中，自动移动服务机器人因避障频繁减速，导致任务效率下降；且人群移动具有随机性和时变性，现有路径规划方法难以有效平衡导航效率与拥挤因素。

Method: 1. 学习“循环线性流场”（CLiFF）地图，用于在给定初始观测的情况下预测人群移动轨迹；2. 构建并在线求解马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），将预测的人群拥挤度纳入路径规划决策中，实现高效率的路径重规划。

Result: 在真实世界的商场人群数据集上进行了评估，验证了该方法在处理拥挤环境、优化机器人运行效率方面的有效性及可扩展性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于CLiFF地图和马尔可夫决策过程的概率化路径规划方法，能够有效解决拥挤环境下的机器人导航任务，实现路径优化并实时适应环境变化。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile service robots are often required to complete tours that require navigating through a set of locations in an environment. Example domains include guiding people through a shopping mall, delivering packages in a fulfilment centre, or giving guided tours in a museum. However, in crowded environments, the presence of people may negatively impact robot performance. For example, humans will activate robot collision avoidance manoeuvres that slow the robot down. Crowds move stochastically and vary throughout the day. In this paper we present a probabilistic tour planner for crowded environments which explicitly reasons over human congestion. We learn circular linear flow field (CLiFF) maps which predict human trajectories given an initial observation. We then use these predictions to build and solve a Markov decision process online which efficiently routes the robot through the environment. Our approach is scalable enough to re-plan as new people are observed. We evaluate our approach on a real-world crowd dataset in a shopping mall.

</details>


### [268] [Sensor Configuration Matters: A Systematic Evaluation of Multimodal SLAM on Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19067)
*Roberto Corlito,Fabian Schmidt,Nils Seibert,Markus Enzweiler,Abhinav Valada,Arne Roennau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文通过实验分析了硬件配置（相机与IMU）对四足机器人SLAM性能的影响，发现立体视觉、全局快门对提升恶劣环境下的定位鲁棒性至关重要，并提供了传感器选型建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前针对四足机器人剧烈运动（足端冲击、高频振动、快速旋转）引发的感知失效问题，缺乏针对硬件配置影响的系统性量化评估与设计准则。

Method: 利用GrandTour数据集，在ANYmal D四足机器人平台上，系统评估了各类视觉、视觉惯性及激光雷达-视觉-惯性SLAM算法，对比了不同相机模态、快门技术及惯性传感器的性能表现。

Result: 1. 立体视觉表现优于单目和RGB-D；2. 全局快门显著改善运动造成的跟踪失败；3. 在剧烈震动环境下，标准惯性测量单元（IMU）集成反而可能降低部分纯视觉SLAM的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 硬件配置（如相机模态、快门类型、惯性传感器精度）对四足机器人SLAM性能有显著影响，提出了构建可靠敏捷感知系统的设计指南。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation of quadrupedal robots in diverse environments fundamentally relies on resilient Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM). While visual-inertial SLAM has matured across wheeled, handheld, and aerial platforms, a critical evaluation gap remains regarding how hardware-level sensor configurations affect performance under the aggressive dynamics of legged locomotion. Quadrupeds introduce distinct embodiment-induced sensory challenges, including foot-impact shocks, high-frequency mechanical vibrations, and rapid angular rotations, which degrade standard perception pipelines. To address this gap, we present a systematic evaluation of state-of-the-art visual, visual-inertial, and LiDAR-visual-inertial SLAM methods using the GrandTour dataset recorded on an ANYmal D quadruped. We isolate and quantify the impacts of camera modalities, shutter techniques, and inertial sensor tiers, analyzing their trade-offs across localization accuracy, algorithmic robustness, and computational resource utilization. Our empirical findings demonstrate that hardware selection has substantial influence on system resilience: stereo configurations consistently outperform monocular and RGB-D modalities, global shutter cameras significantly mitigate motion-induced tracking failures compared to rolling shutter cameras, and, crucially, standard inertial integration can degrade the performance of primarily vision-based frameworks under harsh legged locomotion. These insights additionally offer concrete design guidelines for tailoring custom sensor payloads to achieve dependable perception on agile legged systems.

</details>


### [269] [ReSiReg: Towards Spatially Consistent Semantics in Language-Conditioned Robotic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19088)
*Simon Schwaiger,David Seyser,Alessandro Scherl,Wilfried Wöber,Gerald Steinbauer-Wagner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对 VLM 稠密特征空间一致性差的问题，本文提出了 ReSiReg 特征重构方法，通过空间原型化增强语义检索效果，并发布了一款高效的 25M 轻量级 VLM，优化了机器人对环境的联合语义推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言模型 (VLMs) 生成的稠密嵌入存在噪声大、空间一致性差的问题，导致其在机器人对语义和 3D 环境的联合推理任务中表现不佳。

Method: 提出了一种名为 ReSiReg (Feature Reconstruction) 的方法：(1) 将 VLM 的中间特征聚类为视觉原型 (Visual Prototypes)；(2) 导出对应的语言描述符；(3) 将每个图像补丁重构为原型级语言嵌入的软混合 (Soft Mixture)，以增强空间的语义一致性。此外，还开发了一个 25M 的轻量级稠密 VLM。

Result: 定量实验表明 ReSiReg 在 OVSS (开放词汇语义分割) 和 3D 映射任务中的稠密检索性能显著提升；定性实验证明其在实际机器人操作任务中能产生更具空间一致性的目标激活；同时其 25M 模型在保持竞争力的前提下大幅减小了模型体积。

Conclusion: ReSiReg 方法有效提升了 VLM 在机器人任务中的空间一致性和稠密语义检索能力，证明了通过原型化重构特征是弥补现有 VLM 空间缺陷的有效途径。此外，其提出的紧凑型 25M 模型展示了高效机器人部署的潜力。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) enable robots to follow open-language instructions. However, dense VLM embeddings have shown to be noisy and lack spatial consistency. This is problematic for robotic applications, which require simultaneous reasoning over semantics and 3D space. We examine spatial structure across recent VLMs and propose ReSiReg, a feature reconstruction method that uses spatially consistent VLM intermediates to improve dense language-grounded retrieval. ReSiReg clusters intermediates into visual prototypes, derives their language descriptors, and reconstructs each patch as a soft mixture of prototype-level language embeddings. We evaluate quantitatively on OVSS and 3D mapping across backbones, and qualitatively in real-world manipulation scenes. Quantitative results show improved dense retrieval; manipulation scenes show more spatially consistent target activations. We further provide a compact 25M dense VLM for robotic applications, substantially smaller than and competitive with ViT-B baselines. Available at https://resireg.github.io

</details>


### [270] [ART-VS: Adaptive Resolution Tiling for Vision Transformer Visual Servoing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19089)
*Alessandro Scherl,Bernhard Neuberger,Simon Schwaiger,David Mulero-Pérez,Lucas Muster,Jose Garcia-Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对 ViT 特征视觉伺服中分辨率带来的鲁棒性与精度权衡困境，ART-VS 通过分阶段自适应特征颗粒度（粗对齐+局部细化），实现了无需训练的高性能机器人定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于 ViT 的自我监督视觉伺服在大分辨率下存在“鲁棒性与精度难以兼顾”的问题，增加分辨率带来的精度提升伴随着巨大的计算开销且鲁棒性改进有限。

Method: 提出自适应分辨率平铺视觉伺服（ART-VS）方法，包含两个阶段：1. 粗粒度阶段：使用 ViT 原生分辨率进行稳定对齐；2. 平铺高分辨率阶段：限制匹配范围至局部邻域以提高精度。该方法无需特定任务训练。

Result: 在扰动环境下实现了 95.4% 的收敛率（较基准提升 18.8pct），定位误差降低 53%，运行速度提升 10 倍以上，且显存占用降低 27%；在真实场景的类级抓取任务中表现优异（如透明瓶子 95/100，鞋子 98/100）。

Conclusion: ART-VS 提供了一种在视觉伺服任务中平衡鲁棒性与精度的有效策略，通过自适应调整特征粒度，实现了无需额外训练的高效、高精度机器人定位。

Abstract: Visual servoing with self-supervised Vision Transformer (ViT) features enables training-free robotic positioning with strong generalization, but faces a fundamental trade-off between robustness and precision. Coarse patch-level descriptors provide stable correspondences yet limit positioning accuracy. Increasing image resolution improves precision but yields only marginal robustness gains - under perturbation, high-resolution processing improves convergence success rate from 76.6% to just 81.0% despite 12x more ViT patches. Therefore, we propose Adaptive Resolution Tiling Visual Servoing (ART-VS), a two-phase method that adapts feature granularity to servoing progress: a coarse phase at native ViT resolution for stable alignment, then a tiled high-resolution phase that restricts matching to local neighborhoods improving positioning accuracy. Without any task-specific training, ART-VS achieves 95.4% convergence under perturbation, outperforming standard and full-resolution ViT-based servoing by 18.8 and 14.4 percentage points. Over the former it reduces positioning error by 53%, while running at over 10x higher speed and 27% lower VRAM than the latter. We validate ART-VS across three ViT backbones and demonstrate real-world category-level grasping of unseen object instances, achieving 95/100 on transparent bottles and 98/100 on shoes. Code available under https://art-vs.github.io/.

</details>


### [271] [GCNGrasp-VP: Affordance-Guided View Planning for Efficient Task-Oriented Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19091)
*Zanjia Tong,Wenlong Dong,Chengjie Zhang,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对遮挡环境下的任务导向抓取，本文提出了GCNGrasp-VP框架，通过将可供性场预测与高效的主动视点规划相结合，在无需场景重建的情况下，实现了精确、快速的任务相关区域观察与抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务导向抓取方法在物体遮挡时性能锐减，而现有的主动感知方法（视点规划）往往忽略了任务语义，且依赖耗时的场景重建过程，导致实时性差。

Method: 1. GCNGrasp-v2：一种能同时进行抓取评估与可供性场（Affordance field）预测的模型，具有常数级推理复杂度；2. Affordance-VP：一种基于可供性搜索的视点规划器，利用可供性场作为信息增益指标，无需显式场景重建即可引导相机观察关键区域。

Result: 在视点规划实验中，该方法仅通过一次视点调整即可显著优于基于场景不确定性的基线方法；实机验证表明在单物体场景下显著提升了抓取成功率，且保持在毫秒级的推理延迟。

Conclusion: GCNGrasp-VP提出了一种高效的框架，成功整合了任务导向抓取与主动视觉规划，解决了遮挡问题下的任务语义缺失和计算耗时瓶颈，在单物体场景中实现了高成功率与毫秒级延迟的平衡。

Abstract: Task-oriented grasping performance degrades significantly when object views suffer from occlusions. Existing task-oriented grasping methods typically assume task-relevant regions are visible in the initial frame, while view planning approaches enable active perception but often ignore task semantics and rely on time-consuming scene reconstruction. To address these limitations, we present GCNGrasp-VP, an efficient framework integrating affordance field prediction with active view planning. Central to this framework is GCNGrasp-v2, a task-oriented grasp model that simultaneously supports grasp evaluation and affordance field prediction, achieving constant-time inference complexity. Leveraging this capability, our Affordance-guided View Planner (Affordance-VP) utilizes the affordance field as an information gain metric to guide camera observation of task-relevant regions without requiring scene reconstruction. View planning results show that our method significantly outperforms scene-uncertainty-driven baselines with only one view adjustment. Real-world validation further confirms substantial improvements in grasp success rates for single-object scenarios while maintaining millisecond-level computational latency. Code and models are available at https://github.com/Instinct323/GCNGrasp-VP.

</details>


### [272] [Monocular 3D Occupancy Perception for Robots on Sidewalks via Hybrid 2D-3D Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19122)
*Yukai Ma,Joe Lin,Liu Liu,Honglin He,Lulu Ricketts,Brad Squicciarini,Yong Liu,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对人行道场景研发的 WalkOCC 框架，通过融合少量的 LiDAR 校准数据与大规模单目图像自监督训练，克服了 3D 标注成本高的问题，同时发布了专门的 Sidewalk3D 数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 3D 占据预测算法主要针对自动驾驶场景，依赖昂贵且密集的 3D 标注数据，难以适应“拥挤、杂乱、非结构化”的人行道环境及移动机器人（如配送机器人、轮椅）的部署需求。

Method: 提出了 WalkOCC 框架，采用混合射线投射（Hybrid Ray-marching）技术；通过从成对的 LiDAR-RGB 数据集中挖掘伪占据监督信号（Pseudo-occupancy supervision），结合大规模未标注单目图像进行联合表示学习，从而实现少监督下的高效 3D 占据感知。

Result: 在人行道场景下取得了更高的预测精度和细粒度分割效果（如路缘、排水沟），在跨设备和跨环境迁移中表现出更强的鲁棒性，显著优于现有的自监督基准模型。

Conclusion: WalkOCC 提出了一种无需昂贵 3D 标注的高效协同感知框架，利用混合射线投射和跨模态自监督学习显著提升了移动机器人在复杂人行道环境下的导航安全性与泛化能力。其开源的 Sidewalk3D 数据集为行人环境下的 3D 占据预测提供了重要的基准参考。

Abstract: Sidewalks in the real world are crowded, cluttered, and less structured than roads, making 3D occupancy prediction a key ingredient for the safe navigation of mobile robots such as delivery bots and electric wheelchairs. Existing occupancy learning pipelines are largely designed for on-road autonomous driving and often train on large-scale paired LiDAR-RGB datasets with dense 3D supervision and multiple camera inputs, which are costly to collect and do not adequately capture sidewalk-specific characteristics. We propose WalkOCC, a hybrid Ray-marching monocular 3D occupancy perception framework for robots operating on sidewalks. WalkOCC explicitly couples geometric grounding from LiDAR-RGB paired data with scalable learning from large-scale unpaired monocular images. It bootstraps pseudo occupancy supervision from paired sequences and jointly learns image-level representations on additional 2D-only data. It yields stable optimization and improved generalization without requiring costly 3D occupancy annotations. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent gains in prediction accuracy, fine-grained segmentation of subtle urban structures such as curbs and gutters, and robustness to environmental and cross-embodiment shifts compared with self-supervised image-based baselines. To facilitate evaluation and benchmarking, we also introduce Sidewalk3D, a large-scale sidewalk perception dataset with LiDAR-camera paired sequences collected across multiple locations and time periods, along with 3D semantic occupancy annotations for evaluation. Code and data will be made available.

</details>


### [273] [Viking Hill Dataset: A Lidar-Radar-Camera Dataset for Detection and Segmentation in Forest Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19154)
*Vladimír Kubelka,Oleksandr Kotlyar,Unal Artan,Martin Magnusson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发布了一个林下多模态数据集（含4D成像雷达、激光雷达、RGB），通过基准测评展示了雷达在复杂植被环境下的感知潜力，并支持进一步的定位与感知研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥补现有林业数据集缺乏同源配准的4D成像雷达数据的空缺，旨在通过雷达的抗干扰和抗遮挡优势，解决林下环境对感知系统的挑战。

Method: 构建了一个高分辨率多模态林下数据集（包含FMCW成像雷达、激光雷达、RGB相机、IMU和RTK-GNSS），在不同季节条件下采集；使用MinkowskiUNet对雷达和激光雷达点云进行语义分割基准测试，并进行了跨模态分析和基于树干直径的性能评估。

Result: 实验表明，雷达在地面（91% IoU）和冠层（86% IoU）分类上具有与激光雷达相当的性能，但在精细结构（如树干）上表现较弱（56% vs 74%）；评估还揭示了树木尺寸对分割质量的影响。

Conclusion: 该研究为林下自主机器人感知研究提供了一个包含4D成像雷达的多模态基准数据集，并揭示了成像雷达在林下场景的潜力与局限性，特别是在大尺度植被分类上具备与激光雷达竞争的能力。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating under forest canopies need robust perception of trees and surrounding vegetation across varying seasonal conditions. Existing forestry datasets provide lidar or camera data with per-tree annotations, but none include co-registered 4D imaging radar -- a modality of growing interest for its resilience to visual degradation, surface contamination, and vegetation occlusion. We introduce a multi-sensor forest dataset collected by a mobile robot equipped with a high-resolution FMCW imaging radar, lidar, RGB camera, IMU, and RTK-GNSS. The site was recorded in two sessions under contrasting vegetation states, and 3D cuboid annotations -- including per-tree diameter estimates -- provide shared semantic labels across all three perception modalities. Furthermore, we provide baseline results for semantic segmentation of the radar and lidar point clouds using MinkowskiUNet. Radar achieves IoU scores competitive with lidar for dominant classes (ground 91%, canopy 86%) while lagging on geometrically fine structures such as tree trunks (56% vs. 74%). A cross-modality analysis further compares lidar and radar trunk segmentation against an RGB detection model, and a diameter-stratified evaluation reveals how trunk segmentation quality varies with tree size. Beyond segmentation, the co-registered multi-modal data and RTK-GNSS-aided reference positioning support research in mapping, localization, and sensor fusion under canopy. The dataset and annotation tools are publicly available.

</details>


### [274] [HT-Bench: Benchmarking and Learning Dexterous Full-Hand Tactile Representations with Egocentric Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19161)
*Yuzhe Huang,Jiaping Wu,Jiaming Jiang,Hezhe Lin,Aikebaier Aierken,Yunlong Wang,Kun Cheng,Ziyuan Jiao,Yuanxin Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人触觉表征学习缺乏通用基准的问题，本文提出了大规模全身触觉基准HT-Bench及模型HandTouch，通过多模态训练显著提升了触觉建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉传感器的多样性使得建立通用基准困难，作者旨在探索一种基于以自我为中心的视觉与全身触觉数据结合的可扩展研究方向。

Method: 提出了HT-Bench基准（包含10M RGB和7.8M触觉帧，涵盖226项任务）以及HandTouch模型（一种采用渐进式空间、跨模态和时序训练的向量量化视觉-触觉编码器）。

Result: HandTouch在细粒度触觉相似性检索（Recall@5从74.65%提升至85.23%）、触觉补全（RMSE从0.022降至0.010）及视觉到触觉合成（OOD cIoU从0.628提升至0.705）等指标上显著超越现有基线。

Conclusion: 研究表明，大规模以自我为中心的全身触觉数据是评估和推进灵巧操作中触觉表征学习的可扩展基准，HandTouch模型在各项任务中均表现优于现有基线。

Abstract: Establishing a universal benchmark for tactile representation learning in robotic manipulation remains challenging due to the diversity of tactile sensor designs, data formats, and robot embodiments. Rather than seeking to establish such, we explore a scalable and promising direction for future development: egocentric vision paired with full-hand tactile data. To this end, we introduce \textbf{HT-Bench}, a large-scale multi-task benchmark for dexterous full-hand tactile sensing, comprising 10M RGB frames and 7.8M tactile frames collected across 226 tasks. HT-Bench evaluates tactile representations from three key perspectives: whether they encode meaningful contact geometry, whether they can align tactile observations with visual information, and whether they generalize to unseen tasks. To assess these capabilities, HT-Bench includes four tasks: fine-grained tactile similarity retrieval, masked tactile inpainting, vision-to-tactile synthesis, and multimodal tactile frame prediction. We further propose \textbf{HandTouch}, a vector-quantized vision--tactile encoder that learns tactile representations through progressive spatial, cross-modal, and temporal training. Across HT-Bench, HandTouch consistently outperforms representative tactile encoder baselines, improving Recall@5 on fine-grained tactile similarity retrieval from 74.65\% to 85.23\%, reducing RMSE on masked tactile inpainting from 0.022 to 0.010, and increasing OOD cIoU on vision-to-tactile synthesis from 0.628 to 0.705. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of HandTouch and suggest that large-scale egocentric full-hand tactile data provides a scalable basis for evaluating and advancing tactile representation learning in dexterous manipulation.

</details>


### [275] [Hardware- and Vision-in-the-Loop Validation of Deep Monocular Pose Estimation for Autonomous Maritime UAV Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19176)
*Maneesha Wickramasuriya,Beomyeol Yu,Jaden Shin,Mason Huslig,Taeyoung Lee,Murray Snyder*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种硬件在环仿真框架，通过模拟逼真的海上视觉环境与真实的系统延迟，实现了室内高精度的无人机海上作业流程验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上无人机作业场景依赖视觉相对位姿估计，但在真实海面进行验证成本高、受天气影响大且风险高，亟需一种既能模拟真实环境复杂度，又能在室内实现硬件验证的测试手段。

Method: 提出了一种硬件在环（Vision-in-the-loop）的测试框架：结合了逼真的海上环境渲染、基于深度Transformer的单目位姿估计、以及融合高频IMU数据的延迟卡尔曼滤波（DKF）算法，在室内环境下完整模拟了感知延迟、异步更新和计算资源限制等真实海洋作业中的挑战。

Result: 实验成功实现了室内环境下的全自主起飞、轨迹跟踪及降落，验证了该框架在处理感知延迟和计算约束下的闭环控制稳定性和系统鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究构建了一个高保真的硬件在环仿真框架，弥补了纯仿真与真实海上作业之间的空白，为海上无人机自主飞行算法的研发提供了一个安全、可控的测试平台。

Abstract: Autonomous UAV operations on ships require reliable vision-based relative pose estimation, yet at-sea validation is costly, weather-dependent, and risky. This paper presents a hardware-validated vision-in-the-loop framework that enables fully autonomous indoor flight while emulating photorealistic maritime environments. Rendered maritime views are processed onboard by a deep transformer-based monocular pose estimator. Delayed vision measurements are fused with high-rate IMU data using a delayed Kalman filter to provide consistent state estimates for geometric control. The system captures critical embedded effects, including perception latency, asynchronous updates, and computational constraints, that are absent in pure simulation. Autonomous takeoff, trajectory tracking, and landing experiments demonstrate stable closed-loop flight. The results establish a safe and hardware-realistic intermediate stage for developing maritime UAV autonomy prior to shipboard deployment.

</details>


### [276] [Learning to Annotate Delayed and False AEB Events: A Practical System for Extreme Class Imbalance and Asymmetric Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19186)
*Mengxiang Hao,Xin Jiang,Xinghao Huang,Wenliang Su,Zhiteng Wang,Junjie Rao,Xiaotian Yang,Wei Liao,Chengyu Han,Gen Liang,Yulun Song,Zhitao Xu,Xianpeng Lang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对AEB系统优化中罕见关键事件（延迟/误触发）标注困难的问题，提出了一种结合专门数据增强与噪声抑制的自动化标注框架，在显著提升召回率的同时大幅降低了人工标注成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动紧急制动（AEB）系统的优化依赖于对罕见但关键的延迟和误触发事件的准确注释，而这类样本在海量数据中占比极低（<5%），导致人工标注成本极高且效率低下。

Method: 1. 开发了特定的数据增强技术：通过操作焦点目标属性、移植自车动力学、掩蔽非焦点代理来合成逼真的少数样本；2. 噪声抑制策略：结合稳定的硬度估计和探测器引导的自适应阈值，清洗被错误标记的多数类样本（真实触发）。

Result: 生产环境应用结果表明，该系统在延迟/误触发的召回率上提升了80%，人工工作量减少了50%。

Conclusion: 本研究构建了一个自动化的AEB注释框架，通过解决数据不平衡和标签噪声问题，显著提升了对关键罕见触发事件（延迟/误触发）的识别能力，有效降低人工成本，并为车载系统的持续优化奠定了数据基础。

Abstract: Autonomous Emergency Braking (AEB) optimization relies on accurately annotated real-world trigger events, particularly rare but critical delayed and false AEB triggers that expose system deficiencies. However, these minority samples comprise less than 5% of thousands of daily triggers, making manual annotation prohibitively expensive at scale. We present the first automated AEB annotation framework to address this problem. During development, we identified two fundamental challenges that severely impair delayed/false trigger annotation accuracy: (1) Extreme class imbalance where delayed/false triggers are overwhelmed by true triggers; (2) Asymmetric label noise where mislabeled majority samples (true triggers) suppress minority samples (delayed/false triggers) learning. To overcome these challenges, we propose two key innovations: (1) Specific data augmentation that synthesizes realistic samples by manipulating focal target attributes, transplanting ego-vehicle dynamics, and masking non-focal agents; (2) noise suppression using stable hardness estimation and probe-guided adaptive threshold to clean mislabeled true trigger samples. Crucially, we deploy our model as a practical annotation system with full-stack architecture, efficiently identifying critical delayed/false triggers from thousands of daily AEB events. Production results demonstrate 80% improvement in recall of delayed/false triggers and 50% reduction in manual workload. Beyond immediate gains, the system enables continuous self-improvement through accumulated high-quality annotations, establishing a necessary data foundation for on-vehicle AEB system optimization

</details>


### [277] [FAST-LIVGO: A Degeneracy-Robust LiDAR-Inertial-Visual-GNSS Fusion Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19190)
*Zhiyu Chen,Chunran Zheng,Jiayu Wen,XiaoLei Zhang,Jiaming Xu,Feng Pan,Yukang Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种紧耦合的多传感器融合导航与映射框架，通过结合LiDAR、惯导、视觉和高精度GNSS，利用ES-IKF、DTW在线对齐及退化感知机制，有效解决了长距离、高动态及几何退化环境下的定位漂移和鲁棒性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR-Inertial-Visual Odometry (LIVO) 系统在长距离环境下存在累积漂移，且在退化场景下易失效；而传统的GNSS辅助系统过度依赖局部里程计，在里程计退化时鲁棒性不足。亟需一种能融合多种传感器优势，具备高鲁棒性和抗退化能力的导航框架。

Method: 1. 提出基于误差状态迭代卡尔曼滤波（ES-IKF）的紧耦合多传感器融合框架；2. 设计基于动态时间规整（DTW）的在线时空对齐模块以处理高动态环境；3. 开发基于多普勒频移和载波相位差分的GNSS观测模型；4. 构建退化感知的双模离群点剔除策略，在LIVO退化时自动切换至GNSS辅助恢复模式。

Result: 在M3DGR公开数据集及固定翼无人机实测数据集（20m/s）上，该系统显著降低了累积漂移和地图重影现象，在精度和鲁棒性方面均优于现有的前沿（SOTA）方法。

Conclusion: 该框架通过引入误差状态迭代卡尔曼滤波、动态时间规整（DTW）对齐、高精度GNSS观测模型及退化感知双模离群点抑制策略，成功解决了长距离、高动态环境下传统LIVO系统漂移和退化问题，在复杂场景下具备极高的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Robust state estimation and mapping in long-term, large-scale, and highly dynamic environments remains a key challenge in robotics. Existing LiDAR-Inertial-Visual Odometry (LIVO) systems achieve strong local accuracy but suffer from accumulated drift over long distances and may fail in geometrically degraded or textureless scenes. Meanwhile, GNSS-aided fusion frameworks often rely on LiDAR or visual odometry for state prediction and outlier rejection, making them vulnerable when odometry degenerates. To address these limitations, we propose a tightly coupled LiDAR-Inertial-Visual-GNSS fusion framework based on an Error-State Iterated Kalman Filter. An online spatiotemporal alignment module using Dynamic Time Warping is introduced for highly dynamic conditions. To better exploit GNSS precision, we develop observation models based on Doppler shifts and fixed-anchor Time-Differenced Carrier Phase, providing millimeter-level relative constraints without augmenting historical anchor states. We further design a degeneracy-aware dual-mode outlier rejection strategy that switches between LIVO-prior-guided rejection and GNSS-aided recovery according to the LIVO degeneracy level. Experiments on the public M3DGR dataset and a custom 20~m/s fixed-wing UAV dataset demonstrate that our system reduces accumulated drift and map ghosting, outperforming state-of-the-art methods in accuracy and robustness.

</details>


### [278] [Invertible Neural Network Adapter for One-Step Flow Matching in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19194)
*Yu Zhang,Kangyi Ji,Yongxiang Zou,Rongtao Xu,Feng Zheng,Long Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于流匹配的可逆神经网络适配器，通过将动作生成简化为单步推理，在大幅降低VLA模型推理延迟的同时，保持了高水平的操控精度与任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于流匹配的机器人策略通常需要多次迭代才能生成动作，导致计算复杂度和推理延迟较高，难以满足机器人实时实时操作的需求。

Method: 提出了一种基于流匹配（Flow-matching）公式的可逆神经网络适配器。该方法通过将动作生成约束在可逆潜空间内，将原本需要多步迭代的去噪过程简化为单步推理过程。

Result: 模拟实验显示该方法在多项操作任务中达到或优于最优性能；实机实验中，将推理延迟从110ms降低至61ms，显著提升了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的部署效率。

Conclusion: 该研究通过改进流匹配架构，实现了单步推理下的高效、精确机器人动作控制，证明了该方法在满足实时性要求的同时，能够保持甚至超越传统迭代法的性能，为机器人操作任务提供了一种高效的落地方案。

Abstract: This paper presents an invertible neural network adapter for general robotic manipulation, designed to generate precise high-dimensional actions conditioned on multimodal observations, including visual, linguistic, and proprioceptive inputs, through a one-step denoising process. Built upon a flow-matching formulation, the proposed adapter effectively constrains the action generation trajectory within an invertible latent space, thereby enabling efficient and high-quality dexterous action synthesis with only a single inference step. Compared with conventional iterative flow-matching policies, the proposed framework substantially reduces inference complexity while maintaining strong action prediction accuracy and stability. Extensive experiments are conducted across a diverse set of simulation benchmarks and real-world robotic platforms to evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed method. Across simulation benchmarks, the proposed adapter consistently demonstrates superior or near state-of-the-art performance on a wide range of manipulation tasks. Furthermore, real-world experiments reveal a significant improvement in inference efficiency for vision-language-action (VLA) models, reducing the average inference latency from 110 ms to 61 ms while maintaining strong task performance.

</details>


### [279] [Constant Time-Delay Leader Following with Neural Networks and Invariant Extended Kalman Filters for Arbitrary Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19227)
*Luka Antonyshyn,Paulo Ricardo Marques de Araujo,Sidney Givigi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对无通信、无全局定位的车辆编队场景，提出了一种基于Seq2Seq神经网络、IEKF流形估计与几何模型预测控制的轨迹跟踪系统，在复杂轨迹与长延时条件下表现稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车辆编队技术高度依赖车载通信、通用坐标系或全球定位系统（GPS），在这些基础设施受限或不存在（如通信中断、GPS拒止环境）的场景下，车辆难以实现有效的轨迹跟踪。

Method: 1. 采用概率序列到序列（Seq2Seq）神经网络进行轨迹预测；2. 利用不变扩展卡尔曼滤波器（IEKF）进行预测热启动（warm-start），并在SE(2)流形上估计领航车轨迹；3. 引入基于几何的模型预测控制器（GMPC）以实现高效的路径跟踪。

Result: 在仿真和实际机器人车辆实验中，该方法在处理多变的非线性轨迹和长路径延时方面均优于纯IEKF基准模型及其他学习类算法。

Conclusion: 该研究成功实现了一种在缺乏通信、全局坐标系和GPS辅助下的自动驾驶车队恒定延时轨迹跟踪方案，通过结合深度学习与几何控制，显著提升了复杂非线性路径下的跟踪性能，且对专家经验的依赖度较低。

Abstract: This paper proposes a constant time-delay trajectory tracking method for vehicle convoys operating without inter-vehicle communication, a common coordinate system, or global positioning. The method integrates a probabilistic sequence-to-sequence (Seq2Seq) neural network with an invariant extended Kalman filter (IEKF) to warm-start the prediction process, allowing accurate estimation of a leader vehicle's relative trajectory on the SE(2) manifold. A geometric model predictive controller is further incorporated to fully exploit the manifold-based trajectory predictions for improved control performance. The system can handle arbitrary nonlinear trajectories with varying speeds and motion profiles while reducing the need for expert-based domain knowledge for the design of trajectory following systems, even under long trajectory delays. The effectiveness of the method is validated through comparisons with a pure IEKF baseline, learning-based methods, and the ground-truth trajectory in kinematic simulations, as well as in experiments using real robotic vehicles.

</details>


### [280] [Mobile Pedipulation for Object Sliding via Hierarchical Control on a Wheeled Bipedal Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19233)
*Yue Qin,Yulun Zhuang,Zelin Shen,Yanran Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种轮式双足机器人分层控制框架，通过结合基于TRB动力学模型的NMPC和专门的轨迹规划，实现了复杂的平面物体滑动操控能力，并通过实验展示了该方法在实际任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在扩展轮式双足机器人的功能，使其不仅具备移动能力，还能利用带轮的腿部进行物体操控（如推、滑等），以适应更复杂的任务场景。

Method: 1. 基于三刚体（TRB）动力学模型的非线性模型预测控制（NMPC），显式涵盖髋部侧翻自由度及多种轮地接触模式；
2. 开发了基于轨迹优化的机器人-物体运动规划器，处理地面与物体间的粘滑（stick-slip）转换；
3. 实现了运动与交互力的同时调节。

Result: 通过实机实验验证了两种典型任务：缩行（scooting）和侧向滑动（lateral sliding）。实验结果显示，机器人成功从桌底取出1 kg物体，并通过scooting方式将4 kg物体移动了0.228 m。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的分层控制框架成功实现了轮式双足机器人执行平面物体滑动任务（Pedipulation），通过硬件实验验证了该机器人能稳定协同完成移动与操控行为。

Abstract: In this letter, we present a hierarchical control framework that enables wheeled bipedal robots to perform planar object sliding tasks with their wheeled legs. The proposed approach formulates a nonlinear model predictive controller (NMPC) based on a reduced-order three rigid bodies (TRB) dynamical model that explicitly accounts for the hip roll degree of freedom and multiple wheel-environment contact modes, which is essential for lateral stepping and pedipulation tasks. Within this framework, the NMPC simultaneously regulates robot locomotion and interaction forces, allowing the robot to stably execute both rolling and object manipulation behaviors. A trajectory-optimization-based robot-object motion planner is developed to generate reference motions that incorporate stick-slip transitions in ground-object contact. Two representative pedipulation motions, namely scooting and lateral sliding, are validated through real-world hardware experiments, in which the robot successfully retrieves a 1 kg object from under a desk and slides a 4 kg object over a distance of 0.228 m via scooting.

</details>


### [281] [Seeing Through Occlusion: Deterministic Arm Kinematic Correction for Robot Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19240)
*Thomas M. Kwok,Nicholas Koenig,Yue Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AKC的方法，通过引入基于常数臂长的几何约束，解决了单RGB-D相机在机器人遥操作中因肢体自遮挡导致的关节深度估计不准的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单RGB-D相机运动捕捉系统在机器人遥操作中常因自遮挡（特别是上肢运动）导致深度估计性能下降，影响操作稳定性。

Method: 提出了一种手臂运动学修正（AKC）方法，利用常数臂长假设和腕部位置，通过基于勾股定理的确定性数学公式，重构被遮挡关节的深度信息。

Result: 实验验证显示，AKC在静态和动态运动中均表现出色（MSE和皮尔逊相关系数指标），能有效增强系统鲁棒性并保持解剖学一致性，并在模拟及物理机器人环境中验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: AKC方法通过简单的几何约束有效解决了机器人遥操作中因自遮挡导致的深度估计误差，具备高实时性、无需复杂概率建模及参数调整的优势，非常适用于人机交互场景。

Abstract: Markerless, single-RGB-D-camera motion capture provides a low-cost and non-invasive alternative to conventional marker-based systems for robot teleoperation; however, depth estimation often degrades in the presence of self-occlusion, particularly during upper-limb motion. This paper presents an Arm Kinematic Correction (AKC) method that improves depth estimation by enforcing geometric constraints based on constant arm lengths. The proposed approach reconstructs occluded joint depths by leveraging wrist positions and predefined arm lengths via a deterministic formulation based on the Pythagorean theorem, thereby avoiding the need for complex probabilistic modeling or parameter tuning. Experimental validation against a Vicon reference system demonstrates reliable performance for both static and dynamic joint motions, evaluated using root-mean-square error (RMSE) and Pearson correlation. Furthermore, motion-mapping teleoperation is successfully demonstrated in both simulated and physical robot environments. The results show that AKC enhances robustness and preserves anatomical consistency under long-duration, severe self-occlusion, even when paired with less reliable temporal filters, highlighting its practicality for real-time applications such as robot teleoperation and human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [282] [Shape Sensing of Continuum Robots using Direct Laser Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19265)
*Amber K. Rothe,Nidhi Malhotra,Jaydev P. Desai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种利用直接激光写入（DLW）技术，将应变传感器与柔性机器人关节一体化制造的方法，有效提升了连续体机器人的形状感知与闭环运动控制精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人因其柔韧性在手术中应用广泛，但其变形后的实时形状感知面临挑战，现有的成像或磁性传感等方法存在局限性，需寻找更高效的集成传感方案。

Method: 采用直接激光写入（DLW）技术，利用激光诱导聚合物碳化形成石墨烯传感器；通过线性与非线性模型对传感器进行表征，并将其集成于连续体关节中，进而实现关节角度预测及闭环控制。

Result: 传感器模型可实现低至1.76度的关节角度预测误差，在闭环控制测试中，机器人关节的角度跟踪误差小于3度。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了利用DLW技术在单一材料上集成柔性连续体关节和应变传感器的可行性，为机器人形状感知与闭环控制提供了一种高集成度、低成本且精确的解决方案。

Abstract: Continuum robots offer a promising approach for minimally invasive and natural-orifice surgical procedures due to their inherent compliance and dexterity. However, this flexibility also makes estimating the current shape of the robot challenging. Several approaches have been used to reconstruct the shape of these robots, including imaging, optical sensing, magnetic sensing, and resistive sensing. Strain sensors fabricated using direct laser writing (DLW) could provide an alternative sensing method. This technique involves using a laser to induce carbonization of certain polymers to create graphene patterns, such as strain sensors. In this paper, we demonstrate how a flexible continuum joint and a DLW sensor can be machined as one monolithic structure using the same laser and the same setup. The fabricated sensors are characterized using linear and nonlinear models, which are used to predict the joint angle with error as low as 1.76 degrees. Furthermore, we demonstrate how a DLW sensor can be used to implement closed-loop control in a robotic joint, achieving tracking error under 3 degrees.

</details>


### [283] [A Mixed-Reality Testbed for Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19267)
*H. M. Sabbir Ahmad,Ehsan Sabouni,Emrullah Celik,Zean Wan,Damola Ajeyemi,Christos G. Cassandras,Wenchao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合物理机器人与虚拟仿真环境的混合现实硬件在环（HIL）自动驾驶测试平台，支持多智能体协作及安全控制算法验证，有效填补了仿真与现实部署之间的技术差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决自动驾驶算法在仿真与真实世界部署之间存在的差距，需要一种既能模拟安全关键场景又具备真实硬件验证能力的综合性测试平台。

Method: 提出了一种混合现实（Mixed-reality）硬件在环（HIL）测试架构，结合了物理移动机器人和高保真虚拟仿真环境，支持多智能体系统研究，并集成了一套基于控制障碍函数（CBF）的在线学习控制器以保障安全性。

Result: 通过多智能体协作实验和基于真实机器人与虚拟智能体的混合测试，成功验证了该框架在复杂环境下的感知、规划与控制能力。

Conclusion: 该测试台有效地架起了仿真环境与真实硬件部署之间的桥梁，证明了其验证自动驾驶感知、规划及控制算法的实用性与有效性。

Abstract: We propose a mixed-reality, hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) testbed for autonomous vehicles that seamlessly integrates a physical testbed of mobile robots with a high-fidelity simulation environment. The virtual simulation enables the creation of diverse, safety-critical driving scenarios to validate state-of-the-art perception, planning, and control algorithms, while augmenting simulations with physical robots equipped with multimodal sensors in photorealistic virtual environments further facilitating rigorous validation. Our testbed also features vehicular connectivity using wireless communication and can accommodate a large number of agents through the combination of physical robots and virtual simulated agents, supporting research on multi-agent systems including Connected and Autonomous Vehicles (CAVs). Finally, we present a safety-guaranteed framework combining perception, planning and a novel online learning-based controller using Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) for CAVs. Experiments using the proposed framework are used to validate and demonstrate the key functionalities and the overall utility of the testbed to bridge the gap between simulation and real-world hardware deployment.

</details>


### [284] [Observability and Consistency Analysis for Visual-Inertial Navigation with Anchored Feature Parameterizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19307)
*Mitchell Cohen,Vassili Korotkine,James Richard Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于锚点特征参数化的VINS，证明了该方法能有效提升状态估计的一致性，减轻特征初始化误差的影响，并在实际测试中展现了优异的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉惯性导航系统（VINS）中，如何妥善处理特征表示以解决状态估计中的一致性（Consistency）和可观测性（Observability）问题，是提高导航鲁棒性的关键。现有方法常存在一致性不足的问题，作者旨在通过锚点（Anchored）特征参数化来改善这一现状。

Method: 1. 对基于锚点特征的VINS进行可观测性和一致性理论分析；2. 针对依赖于导航状态的不可观测子空间，提出了两种提高一致性的方法；3. 使用模拟实验验证理论，并在TUM-VI数据集上进行真实场景实验评估。

Result: 理论分析表明锚点参数化使不可观测子空间与地标状态无关，但仍受导航状态依赖。实验证实锚点表示在特征初始化不良时表现更优，在实际数据集上性能可与复杂的全局表示优化算法相媲美。

Conclusion: 锚点特征表示能够改善VINS的状态估计一致性。即使不使用额外的修正技术，其单向性和一致性也优于全局参考系下的表达，特别是在特征初始化较差的情况下。对于更高的一致性要求，建议结合特定的增强技术。

Abstract: This paper presents an analysis of the observability and consistency properties of filtering-based visual-inertial navigation systems (VINS) that utilize anchored feature representations. The unobservable subspace of VINS with anchored landmark parameterizations is shown to be independent of the estimated landmark state, which leads to improved estimator consistency properties without any additional modifications. However, the unobservable subspace is still found to depend on the estimated navigation state, necessitating additional consistency-enforcing techniques. Two methods to improve the consistency of VINS with anchored feature representations are presented. Simulation results showcase that all estimators employing anchored feature paramterizations exhibit improved consistency properties compared to algorithms that estimate features resolved in a global reference frame, especially in scenarios where feature initialization may be poor. Real-world experiments on the TUM-VI dataset showcase that the use of anchored feature representations alone can yield comparable performance to consistency-improved estimators employing a global feature representation, demonstrating the benefit of using anchored feature parameterizations for VINS.

</details>


### [285] [Modeling Branches for Active Manipulation using Iterative Parameter Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19314)
*Madhav Rijal,Rashik Shrestha,Trevor Smith,Yu Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种通过迭代估计材料参数来建模植物枝条的方法，并结合有限元模拟与变形感知规划，显著提升了机器人对植物枝条精细操纵的稳定性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业机器人在密集叶片环境中进行植物重定位、稳定及遮挡物清除时，需要精确的枝条操纵技术，而现有方法难以准确建模不同材质和几何形状的植物枝条。

Method: 1. 重建：从点云数据构建四面体枝条模型；2. 模拟：应用有限元法（FEM）进行物理行为模拟；3. 估计：基于真实观测的变形数据，通过迭代方法估算枝条的材料参数；4. 规划：使用变形感知运动规划器计算最优路径，以实现枝条的移动与稳定。

Result: 在30次实验中，相较于基准方法，该方法使枝条变形能量降低了35.69%，同时路径长度平均增加8.10%。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的基于有限元法的迭代参数估计与变形感知运动规划方法，能够有效优化农业机器人对复杂植物枝条的操纵任务，在减少变形能量的同时实现了稳定的环境交互。

Abstract: This study presents a method for modeling diverse plant branches by iteratively estimating material parameters to support delicate branch manipulation. Branch manipulation is necessary in agricultural robotics for plant repositioning, stabilizing, and clearing visual obstructions in dense foliage. The proposed method builds a tetrahedral branch model from point-cloud data and simulates its behavior using the finite element method. Using real observed deformation data, it iteratively estimates branch parameters and then computes an optimal path with a deformation-aware motion planner to move and stabilize branches within another robot's field of view. Across 30 trials on branches with varying geometries and material properties, the proposed method reduced the deformation energy by 35.69% while increasing the path length by 8.10% on average.

</details>


### [286] [Do as I Do: Dexterous Manipulation Data from Everyday Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19333)
*Bhawna Paliwal,Haritheja Etukuru,William Liang,Pieter Abbeel,Nur Muhammad Mahi Shafiullah,Jitendra Malik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对机器人灵巧手数据稀缺问题，提出 DO AS I DO 算法，通过重构和映射单目 RGB 视频中的人体交互，高效实现机器人操纵数据生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人灵巧手操作数据获取困难，且现有方法难以很好地解决人机形态差异（embodiment gap）以及从单目 RGB 视频中准确恢复手-物交互的问题。

Method: DO AS I DO 算法：1. 从各类 egocentric 和 exocentric 视频中重构手-物交互过程；2. 将重构的交互数据重新映射（retarget）为多指灵巧手可执行的动作序列，从而生成完整的机器人操纵数据。

Result: 在带有地面真值的数据集及野外在线收集的片段中，该方法在手-物交互估计和灵巧操纵轨迹提取方面表现优于现有 SOTA 技术。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 DO AS I DO 能够有效利用野外非受控的单目 RGB 视频，解决机器人灵巧手的数据获取瓶颈，为机器人学习提供了一种可扩展的方案，并给出了实用数据采集建议。

Abstract: How can we scalably generate data for robotic manipulation, especially on human-like platforms such as dexterous multi-fingered hands? Learning from human videos has recently emerged as a likely answer to this question. However, difficulties in estimating hand-object interaction and crossing the human-to-robot embodiment gap have hindered the adoption of abundant monocular RGB-only human videos as the primary source of robot manipulation data. In this work, we present DO AS I DO, an algorithm to reconstruct and retarget monocular RGB human videos to multi-fingered dexterous robotic hands. DO AS I DO reconstructs hand-object interactions from various egocentric and exocentric in-the-wild video sources. The algorithm then retargets these hand-object interaction estimates into a sequence of actions executable in the real world, yielding robot-complete manipulation data from disparate human videos. Overall, DO AS I DO outperforms previous state of the art in estimating hand-object interactions and extracting dexterous manipulation trajectories from RGB videos, as we show in experiments on datasets with ground truths and on a dataset of video clips collected online. Our experiments enable us to propose an efficacy playbook for practitioners collecting human data for manipulation.

</details>


### [287] [Zero-Shot Long-Horizon Dexterous Manipulation via Multi-View 3D-Grounded VLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19340)
*Jisoo Kim,Sangwon Baik,Taeksoo Kim,Sungjoo Kim,Junyoung Lee,Mingi Choi,Hanbyul Joo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种零样本灵巧操作框架，通过从多视角图像提取关键点并进行3D投影，结合动作生成与闭环修正，实现了复杂环境下长序列任务的稳健执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有的端到端操控模型在长序列灵巧任务中表现不稳定、对未见物体泛化能力弱的问题，提出一种基于视觉语言模型（VLM）和多视角几何融合的零样本方案。

Method: 1. 使用VLM进行任务定位与2D关键点检测；2. 通过多视角三角测量与参考视角语义射线投票（Ray Voting）将2D点提升至3D；3. 针对工具使用检索原子动作轨迹；4. 使用运动生成器生成任务条件下的抓取运动；5. 引入闭环状态验证与重规划机制。

Result: 在真实世界实验中，该系统在3D定位准确性和执行可靠性方面均优于现有的单视点RGB-D方法及微调后的VLA基线模型，并能成功处理未见过的物体和新型长序列操作任务。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了利用VLM进行视觉定位、结合多视角几何融合与闭环重规划，无需端到端训练即可实现零样本长序列灵巧操作的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: We present a zero-shot framework for long-horizon dexterous manipulation that grounds language instructions into executable 3D task plans from calibrated multi-view RGB images. Rather than training an end-to-end policy, our system uses a vision-language model (VLM) to produce reference-frame task grounding and primitive-level 2D keypoints, then lifts them into 3D via multi-view fusion. This lifting combines triangulation of view-wise VLM groundings with reference-view ray voting, which searches along a semantic camera ray for geometrically consistent candidates across neighboring views. The resulting 3D keypoints support both pick-and-place and tool-use: for tool-use, we retrieve an object-centric atomic action corresponding to the inferred skill category and align its stored 6D tool trajectory to the scene; for dexterous execution, we expand the lifted grasp keypoint into a task-conditioned grasp affordance region and generate feasible grasp-motion pairs with an arm-hand motion generator. Real-world experiments show improved 3D grounding accuracy and execution reliability over single-view RGB-D grounding and fine-tuned VLA baselines. We further demonstrate long-horizon manipulation through closed-loop status verification and replan, enabling zero-shot execution on unseen objects and tool-use tasks in novel scenes.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [288] [Pointwise is Pointless? A Multimodal Ablation Study for Precipitation Nowcasting with Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18436)
*Ophélia Miralles,Máté Mile,Christoffer Artturi,Thomas Nipen,Ivar Seierstad*

Main category: stat.ML
